### [1] [Advancing Utility Pole and Sign Detection Through Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04061)
*Carl Dickinson,Gaetano Di Caterina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于DETR的深度学习框架，用于从地面街景图像中自动检测、分割木制电线杆、估计其倾斜角度，并对附着电气警示标志进行分类。模型在自建4,570张标注图像的Google Street View数据集上训练，检测性能优于RetinaNet、Faster R-CNN和YOLOv3-Tiny，杆件检测mAP达90.43%，标志检测mAP达88.26%，倾斜角度估计平均绝对误差仅1.01度，数据集已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电线杆是供电系统和关键公共服务基础设施的重要组成部分，定期巡检对保证电网稳定与安全至关重要。传统人工巡检效率低且易受主观因素影响，需要自动化的视觉检测与评估方法，而木制电线杆在真实场景中缺乏辨识性特征，给自动检测带来挑战。

Method: 采用改进的Detection Transformer（DETR）作为基础检测模型，在自建4,570张标注图像（来自Google Street View）上训练；在DETR基础上扩展分割头以生成逐实例掩码，利用掩码估计电线杆倾斜角度；同时进行电气警示标志的检测与分类。

Result: 杆件检测mAP达90.43%，标志检测mAP达88.26%，均优于RetinaNet、Faster R-CNN和YOLOv3-Tiny等标准目标检测器；倾斜角度估计在1,433根测试杆中有1,367根准确估计，平均绝对误差仅1.01度；自建数据集已公开作为基准。

Conclusion: 基于DETR的框架在电线杆检测、分割、倾斜角估计和警示标志分类方面均取得优异性能，验证了Transformer架构在基础设施巡检视觉任务中的有效性，公开数据集为后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Utility poles are an essential part of the infrastructure used to support power distribution systems and other critical public services. Their regular inspection is crucial to ensure the stability and safety of the electrical grid. A deep learning framework is presented for the automated detection, segmentation and lean angle estimation of wooden utility poles, and classification of attached electrical warning signs, using ground-level imagery. The system is trained on a custom dataset of 4,570 annotated images extracted from Google Street View, featuring challenging real-world scenes with visually ambiguous wooden poles lacking distinctive features. The proposed model is based on the Detection Transformer (DETR), suitably modified and trained on the custom dataset. The model outperforms standard object detectors (RetinaNet, Faster R-CNN, YOLOv3-Tiny), achieving a mean average precision of 90.43% for pole detection and 88.26% for sign detection. Extending this model with a segmentation head enables per-instance mask generation, which is then used to estimate pole lean angle. The model accurately estimates lean for 1,367 out of 1,433 test-set poles, with a mean absolute error of 1.01 degrees. Moreover, the custom dataset created in this work is also made publicly available to be used as a benchmark.

</details>


### [2] [LoRetta: A Foundation Model and Extensive Dataset for Global-Scale Remote Sensing Dense Image Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04106)
*Siwei Yu,Han Guo,Zhenwei Shi,Zhengxia Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRetta，一个将稠密图像匹配重新表述为"定位-配准"两阶段任务的遥感基础模型，并引入全球尺度的多时相光学匹配基准LEVIR-GM（含103K对齐对和827K增强对，覆盖六大洲、五年、0.5-1024m分辨率）。LoRetta通过匹配性感知的仿射定位与引导式稠密配准，在LEVIR-GM上AUC达83.3%，比最强基线RoMa v2高1.6个百分点，PCK在1/2像素提升6.5/8.2点，推理延迟降低47.8%，并在宇航员-卫星和无人机-卫星地理定位任务中展示了可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球尺度遥感图像对的稠密匹配面临采集时间、季节、视角、空间分辨率和地表状态差异，导致大几何偏移、部分重叠和本质上不可匹配区域，使直接稠密对应预测不可靠且低效。现有方法未有效解决这些问题，需要一种重新表述的匹配范式。

Method: 将稠密匹配重新表述为"定位-配准"（localization-and-registration）两阶段框架：先定位可匹配重叠区域和仿射几何，再在对齐框架内细化稠密残差。基于此提出LoRetta基础模型，耦合匹配性感知的仿射定位与引导式稠密配准。同时构建LEVIR-GM基准，包含数据集原生匹配性标签，并建立统一评估协议以覆盖稀疏、半稠密和稠密匹配器。

Result: 在LEVIR-GM上，LoRetta的AUC达83.3%，超过最强基线RoMa v2 1.6个百分点；在1像素和2像素精度下PCK分别提升6.5和8.2个百分点，同时推理延迟降低47.8%。在宇航员-卫星和无人机-卫星地理定位实验中展示了其作为可复用几何对齐器的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 提出的定位-配准范式有效解决了全球尺度遥感稠密匹配中的大几何偏移、部分重叠和不可匹配区域问题。LoRetta在精度和效率上均显著优于现有方法，并具备跨任务的可迁移性，可作为通用几何对齐基础模型应用于多种地理定位场景。

Abstract: Dense image matching establishes pixel-wise correspondences and underpins broad applications in computer vision and photogrammetry. However, extending dense matching to global-scale remote sensing remains challenging because image pairs may differ in acquisition time, season, viewpoint, spatial resolution, and land-cover state. The resulting large geometric offsets, partial overlap, and intrinsically unmatchable regions make direct dense correspondence prediction unreliable and inefficient. We thus reformulate dense matching as localization-and-registration: first localizing the matchable overlap and affine geometry, then refining dense residuals within the aligned frame. Based on this formulation, we propose LoRetta, a foundation model coupling matchability-aware affine localization with guided dense registration. We also introduce LEVIR-GM, a global-scale multi-temporal optical matching benchmark with dataset-native matchability labels (103K aligned, 827K augmented pairs, six continents, five years, 0.5-1024 m resolution). We further establish a unified evaluation protocol for sparse, semi-dense, and dense matchers. On LEVIR-GM, LoRetta achieves an area under the curve (AUC) of 83.3%, outperforming the strongest baseline RoMa v2 by 1.6 points, with larger percentage of correct keypoints (PCK) gains of 6.5 and 8.2 points at 1 and 2 pixels, while reducing inference latency by 47.8%. Astronaut-to-satellite and unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-to-satellite geolocalization experiments further demonstrate its transferability as a reusable geometric aligner.

</details>


### [3] [GEB-Bench: Abstract Structures Told in Many Voices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04111)
*Tong Zhang,Zhiyuan Shi,Yun Peng,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GEB-Bench基准，以"抽象结构母题"（如自指、怪圈、莫比乌斯扭曲）为单位，通过自然场景、民间故事、数学定理、程序骨架四种"声音"跨模态讲述同一结构。评测12个开源与专有模型后发现：模型在同一声音内识别结构的能力远强于跨声音映射能力，抽象失败具有规律性，仅在顶级模型中跨声音映射才有所改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多评测模型在语义或视觉层面的理解，但很少评测模型能否抽取跨模态共享的抽象结构。作者借鉴《哥德尔、艾舍尔、巴赫》的思想，希望检验模型是否能在迥异表面下识别同一抽象结构，填补跨模态抽象推理评测的空白。

Method: GEB-Bench定义每个抽象结构母题用四种"声音"讲述：自然场景、民间故事（含可机械验证的形式装置）、数学定理、程序骨架；表面参数被声明为无关变量不计分。母题、声音及其间的结构变化构成小型的跨模态范畴，任务即为该范畴中的问题。作者评测了12个开源与专有模型，并基于模型错误模式与形式几何/感知几何的对齐关系、不同厂商模型的趋同性、表面复杂度的影响进行分析。

Result: 核心发现是"识别-跨声音映射鸿沟"：模型在单一声音内识别结构的能力显著优于跨声音携带结构的能力，所有模型都存在这一"税"。仅前沿层级的模型表现出足够强的映射能力以缩小鸿沟。两个支持性模式为：模型错误与设计的形式几何对齐程度高于感知几何，且不同厂商的前沿模型趋向于相同的错误答案；表面复杂度对所有模型的抽象读取都产生负担，更大的容量只是增加余量而非免疫。

Conclusion: 抽象失败不是随机的而是有规律的，当前模型普遍缺乏真正跨模态的结构抽象能力。GEB-Bench作为完全生成式基准，连同管线一起发布，可成为未来评估和推动抽象推理发展的工具。

Abstract: Can a model look at a river delta and a lightning bolt and see that they share a structure? We introduce GEB-Bench, a benchmark whose unit is an abstract structural motif--self-reference, a strange loop, a Mobius twist--in the spirit of Godel, Escher, Bach. Each motif is told in several voices: a natural scene whose composition is the structure, a folk story whose telling enacts it through a mechanically checkable form device, a mathematical theorem, and a programmatic skeleton; surface parameters are declared nuisance variables and never scored. Motifs, voices, and the structural changes between them form a small cross-modal category, and GEB-Bench's tasks are its questions. Evaluating twelve open and proprietary models, we find that abstraction failure is lawful. The central finding is a gap between recognition and cross-voice mapping: models identify a structure within one voice far better than they carry it across voices; every model pays this tax, and mapping strong enough to narrow it appears only at the frontier tier. Two patterns support it. Errors align more strongly with the designed formal geometry than with measured perceptual geometries, and frontier models from different vendors converge on the same wrong answers; and surface complexity taxes every model that reads structure, with capacity buying headroom rather than immunity. GEB-Bench is fully generative and released with its pipeline.

</details>


### [4] [Perception Before Reasoning: Dynamic Latent Reasoning for Video Understanding and Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04124)
*Haotian Xia,Zilin Xiao,Junbo Zou,Vicente Ordonez,Hanjie Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DyLaR（动态潜在推理）方法，通过将问题先grounding到感知潜变量中，再自适应决定是否追加推理潜变量来回答问题，在九项视频基准和四种多模态语言模型上提升了准确率，同时将每查询输出token从数千降至20以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频问答方法依赖冗长的文本链式思维（chain-of-thought）推理，但许多问题在定位到相关对象、动作或帧后即可回答，无需冗长的文本推理过程。这导致计算开销大、响应冗长。

Method: DyLaR采用两阶段机制：首先将问题grounding到感知潜变量（编码查询相关视觉证据的连续隐藏状态），然后自适应决定是否追加推理潜变量（在潜在空间中进行推理的连续思考）。训练过程包括：用验证过的视觉证据监督感知潜变量的grounding，用验证过的推理链蒸馏到推理潜变量，再用强化学习进一步优化是否推理的决策。

Result: 在九项视频基准和四种多模态语言模型骨干网络上，DyLaR相比同骨干基线提升了平均准确率，同时每查询仅生成不到20个token。例如在Qwen3-VL-4B上，平均准确率从54.0提升到58.2，响应长度从1,220.7 tokens降至18.5 tokens每查询。消融实验表明感知潜变量grounding、推理潜变量的推理链监督以及自适应路由各自都能提升准确率。

Conclusion: DyLaR证明在潜在空间中进行自适应推理比冗长文本推理更高效且更准确，实现了"先定位、按需推理"的视频问答范式，大幅缩短响应长度的同时提升性能。

Abstract: Video question answering requires models to ground language queries in visual evidence and, when necessary, reason over that evidence across time. Existing methods typically rely on long textual chain-of-thought rationales, even though many questions can be answered as soon as the relevant object, action, or frame is localized. We propose Dynamic Latent Reasoning (DyLaR), which first grounds a question in a short block of perception latents (continuous hidden states that encode query-relevant visual evidence), and then adaptively decides whether to append reasoning latents (continuous thoughts that reason over this evidence in latent space) before answering. DyLaR learns this behavior by grounding perception latents in verified visual evidence and distilling verified rationales into reasoning latents, followed by reinforcement learning that further refines when to reason. Across nine video benchmarks and four multimodal language model backbones, DyLaR improves average accuracy over same-backbone baselines while generating fewer than 20 tokens per query. On Qwen3-VL-4B, for example, DyLaR improves average accuracy over Qwen3-VL-4B-Thinking from 54.0 to 58.2 while reducing response length from 1,220.7 to 18.5 tokens per query. Ablations further show that grounded perception latents, rationale-supervised reasoning latents, and adaptive routing each improve accuracy.

</details>


### [5] [An Analysis and Implementation of Seam Carving for Content-Aware Image Resizing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04329)
*Francesco Tosoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文描述了一个基于Avidan和Shamir (2007)原始公式及Rubinstein等人(2008)前向能量准则的C++实现的无缝裁剪（seam carving）图像缩放算子，支持缩小、放大、多遍放大、权重遮罩保护/移除对象，以及能量图和接缝可视化，并详细说明了算法、参数、计算复杂度和设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无缝裁剪是一种内容感知的图像缩放算子，通过反复移除或插入8连通的单调最小重要性像素路径（接缝）来调整图像宽高，能在保留重要结构的同时丢弃低能量区域。本文旨在提供一个遵循原始算法描述的C++实现，以便研究者和开发者使用并理解该算子的行为与实现细节。

Method: 实现了原始的seam carving算法，包括：基于能量图（通常为梯度）的动态规划来寻找最小代价接缝；可选的前向能量准则；支持图像缩小（移除接缝）和放大（有序插入接缝）；采用多遍处理以支持大尺度缩放；支持用户提供的权重遮罩用于对象保护或移除；提供能量图导出和接缝可视化功能。文中详细描述了算法流程、参数设置和计算复杂度，并讨论了与原始描述相关的设计取舍。

Result: 给出了完整的C++实现，分析了算法的时间/空间复杂度，并在自然图像上进行了可视化演示，展示了对各种参数和操作模式下的行为。

Conclusion: 该实现忠实于Avidan和Shamir的原始方法，同时集成了前向能量改进，并通过模块化设计支持多种实际应用场景（保护、移除对象、大比例放大），为相关研究和应用提供了可复用的参考实现。

Abstract: Seam carving is a classical content-aware image resizing operator that modifies the width or height of an image by repeatedly removing (or inserting) seams, i.e., 8-connected monotonic paths of pixels of locally minimal importance. Because seams bend around salient content rather than uniformly scaling or cropping it, the operator preserves vital image structures while discarding (or duplicating) low-energy regions. This article describes a C++ implementation of the operator that follows the original formulation of Avidan and Shamir (2007), including the optional forward-energy criterion subsequently introduced by Rubinstein, Shamir and Avidan (2008). The implementation supports image reduction, image enlargement via ordered seam insertion, multi-pass enlargement for large scale factors, a user-supplied weight mask for object protection and removal, along with dumping of energy maps and visualisation of seams. We detail the algorithm, its parameters and its computational complexity, discuss design choices with respect to the original descriptions, and illustrate the behaviour of the operator on natural images.

</details>


### [6] [Teaching Foundation Models to Read mmWave: Pose-Guided Kinematic Representation for Human Behavior Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04127)
*Duo Zhang,Zhehui Yin,Zhiyun Yao,Haotong Qin,Xusheng Zhang,Hongliu Yang,Jianyu Sun,Junzhe Wang,Zizhou Fan,Michele Magno,Daqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出mmMind，一个利用3D姿态作为仅训练阶段监督信号的毫米波雷达-语言模型，以及mmMind-Bench真实世界基准数据集。通过在时空雷达编码器上预训练姿态引导任务，推理时仅需雷达输入即可与LLM对齐，实现行为描述和时空问答，在多项任务上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体需要理解物理环境中的人类行为，毫米波雷达提供隐私友好且非接触的感知方式，但雷达观测难以与语言对齐。现有雷达-语言方法依赖合成数据或缺乏对人体结构和运动的显式监督，导致跨模态理解能力不足。

Method: mmMind采用三阶段流程：(1) 使用同步的3D姿态作为训练期监督，预训练一个时空雷达编码器以捕捉身体构型和运动动态；(2) 预训练后移除姿态头，使推理时仅依赖雷达输入；(3) 将学到的雷达表征与大型语言模型（LLM）对齐，用于行为描述和时空问答。同时构建mmMind-Bench，包含23名参与者、7个室内环境、17.9小时的真实毫米波-语言基准。

Result: 在行为描述、问答和未见动作泛化等多个任务上，mmMind一致优于现有雷达-语言基线方法；消融实验证实姿态引导预训练对模型性能至关重要。

Conclusion: mmMind证明了利用人体姿态作为训练期监督能有效提升雷达-语言模型的跨模态理解能力，同时保持推理时无需姿态输入的高效性和隐私友好特性，为毫米波雷达与语言模型的结合提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents need to perceive human behavior in physical environments. Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar provides a privacy-friendly and contactless sensing modality, but radar observations are difficult to align with language. Existing radar-language methods often rely on synthetic data or lack explicit supervision for human body structure and motion. We present mmMind, a radar-language model that uses synchronized 3D pose as training-only supervision. A spatio-temporal radar encoder is pretrained to capture body configuration and motion dynamics, after which the pose head is removed so that inference requires radar alone. The learned radar representations are then aligned with an LLM for behavior captioning and spatio-temporal question answering. We also introduce mmMind-Bench, a real-world mmWave-language benchmark containing 17.9 hours of recordings from 23 participants across seven indoor environments. Experiments on captioning, question answering, and unseen-action generalization show that mmMind consistently outperforms existing radar-language baselines, while ablations confirm the importance of pose-guided pretraining.

</details>


### [7] [OutLangSplat: 3D Language Gaussian Splatting for UAV Outdoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04560)
*Xia Yan,He Wu,Yanghui Xu,Zizhao Wu,Jiazhou Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了OutLangSplat，将3D语言高斯表示扩展到无人机户外场景，通过2D-3D双分支特征表示和训练无关的一致性感知特征聚合策略，解决了遮挡和远距离视角导致的语义激活错误和目标响应缺失问题，并提供了首个无人机户外场景的开放词汇3D场景理解数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语言高斯溅射方法仅适用于室内或小规模场景，在无人机户外场景中因严重遮挡和远距离视角常常导致错误的语义激活和目标响应缺失，缺乏针对无人机户外场景的适应性方法。

Method: 1) 2D-3D双分支特征表示，结合区域级对齐与融合，提升空间一致性，减少目标响应不完整和背景误激活；2) 训练无关的贡献感知与一致性感知高斯特征聚合策略，利用像素贡献可靠性与跨视角语义一致性抑制不可靠噪声视角的响应；3) 在四个真实无人机户外数据集上手动标注构建新数据集。

Result: 在开放词汇语义分割和实例定位两个任务上均优于现有最先进方法，并通过消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: OutLangSplat首次将语言高斯表示成功适配到无人机户外大规模场景，同时提供了首个该领域的公开数据集，为户外3D场景理解提供了新的基准。

Abstract: 3D Language Gaussian Splatting embeds open-vocabulary language features into 3D Gaussian Splatting, providing an efficient explicit representation for text-driven 3D scene understanding. However, existing methods are limited to indoor or small-scale scenes, and tend to fail in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) outdoor scenes, where severe occlusions and long distance viewpoints often lead to incorrect semantic activations and missing target responses. In this paper, we present OutLangSplat which adapts language Gaussian representations to UAV outdoor scenes by improving feature representation and aggregation reliability. For the feature representation, a 2D-3D dual-branch representation with region-based alignment and fusion is designed to improve spatial consistency, reducing incomplete target responses and background misactivations. For the feature aggregation, we introduce a training-free contribution and consistency-aware Gaussian feature aggregation strategy that leverages pixel contribution reliability and cross-view semantic consistency to suppress unreliable responses from noisy viewpoints. A new dataset is provided by manually annotating various objects on four real-world public UAV outdoor scene datasets. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first accessible dataset of open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding for UAV outdoor scenes. Quantitative evaluations and ablation studies demonstrate that OutLangSplat outperforms SOTA methods on both open-vocabulary semantic segmentation and instance localization tasks. The datasets and codes will be open-sourced.

</details>

*Jiaju Han,Xuemeng Sun,Qike Zhang,Xiang Chen,Luwei Yang,Jiahuan Long,Yiwei Wei,Jiujiang Guo,Chengyin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Radar4D-VLM，一个仅依赖4D雷达点云（无需相机或LiDAR）的时序视觉语言模型，通过几何锚定的目标提议和分层token（对象/场景/运动学）投影到冻结的大语言模型骨干中，在K-Radar基准上实现98.13%的Top-64提议召回率，并系统评估了五种语言模型家族的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的视觉语言模型主要依赖相机和LiDAR，4D雷达作为独立感知模态长期被忽视，尽管其对恶劣可见度具有鲁棒性并能直接测量径向速度。作者希望探索仅基于4D雷达的多模态场景与运动推理，填补这一空白。

Method: Radar4D-VLM首先从连续十帧4D雷达点云扫描中提取几何锚定的目标提议，将雷达证据组织为对象、场景和运动学三类分层token；通过参数高效的投影器将这些token映射到冻结的语言骨干中；同时使用可审计的预测头联合建模目标数量、空间分布、运动状态、碰撞风险、语义类别和径向速度。

Result: 在K-Radar序列隔离的开发验证上，Top-64提议召回率达到98.13%（4m阈值），比固定晶格和均匀随机控制组分别高出6.40和22.83个百分点。24组匹配实验覆盖Qwen、Phi、Mistral、Llama和Gemma五种冻结骨干，雷达token接口在所有家族中均兼容；但对齐/置换/无语言的对照实验表明存在传感器依赖性，且未显示对齐语言监督带来的稳定直接增益。

Conclusion: 该工作为仅雷达的多模态场景与运动推理建立了可复现的基础，同时明确区分了接口兼容性与语言监督的实际收益，表明雷达token化接口本身是有效的，但语言监督的对齐收益尚不稳定。

Abstract: Vision-language models for autonomous driving primarily rely on cameras and LiDAR, leaving 4D radar largely unexplored as a standalone perceptual modality despite its robustness to adverse visibility and direct measurement of radial velocity. We introduce Radar4D-VLM, a radar-only temporal vision-language model that reasons from ten consecutive 4D-radar point-cloud sweeps without camera or LiDAR input. Radar4D-VLM extracts geometrically grounded object proposals and organizes radar evidence into a compact hierarchy of object, scene, and kinematic tokens. A parameter-efficient projector maps these tokens into frozen language backbones, while auditable prediction heads jointly model object count, spatial distribution, motion state, collision risk, semantic category, and radial velocity. Radar4D-VLM combines proposal-grounded temporal object tokenization, global scene context, and explicit kinematic tokens within a unified frozen-backbone interface. On sequence-isolated K-Radar development validation, its Top-64 proposal recall reaches 98.13% at 4 m, exceeding fixed-lattice and uniform-random controls by 6.40 and 22.83 percentage points, respectively. We further evaluate 24 matched runs spanning eight frozen Qwen, Phi, Mistral, Llama, and Gemma backbones under an identical adaptation budget. The radar-token interface remains compatible across all five language-model families, while matched aligned, permuted, and no-language controls show sensor dependence but no stable direct-head gain from aligned language supervision. These results establish a reproducible foundation for radar-only multimodal scene and motion reasoning while separating interface compatibility from the benefit of language supervision.

</details>


### [9] [Multi-View Face and Gesture Animation with Dynamic Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04722)
*Alireza Javanmardi,Vippin Kumar Jeetmal,Christen Millerdurai,Alain Pagani,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MVFGA，一个多视角一致的管道，用于生成逼真的上半身3D人体头像，分别建模面部和手部并与参数化上半身网格融合，再通过3D高斯溅射实现高质量动态渲染，同时构建了MVFGA-MoCap多视角动作捕捉数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么只关注头部而忽略手势，要么重建全身但面部保真度和手部姿态精度不足，难以捕捉面部表情和手势的微妙动态，而这些对于自然人际交流至关重要。

Method: 分别建模面部和手部，将其与参数化上半身网格模型融合，以捕获细粒度的面部表情和手部姿态；随后将3D高斯溅射到所得网格上，从新视角高质量渲染动态头像。还构建了包含受控面部表情序列、多样手势和自由交流的多视角上半身动作捕捉数据集MVFGA-MoCap。

Result: 实验表明MVFGA能生成具有高保真面部表情和手部动作的视觉逼真头像，在上半身头像动画方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MVFGA通过解耦面部与手部的建模并结合参数化网格与3D高斯渲染，有效弥补了全身体参数模型在表情与手部细节上的不足，为真实感上半身头像重建与动画提供了新方案。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic 3D human avatars with realistic upper-body motion remains challenging. Existing approaches either focus on the head and overlook hand gestures, or reconstruct the full body but fail to preserve fine-grained facial fidelity and hand pose accuracy. As a result, current methods struggle to capture the subtle dynamics of facial expressions and hand gestures that are crucial for natural human communication. While methods based on full-body parametric models enable avatar reconstruction from monocular or multi-view inputs, they often lack accurate facial animation and detailed hand articulation. To address these limitations, we propose MVFGA, a novel multi-view-consistent pipeline for generating realistic upper-body avatars. Our approach models the face and hands separately and fuses them with a parametric upper-body mesh model, enabling the capture of fine-grained facial expressions and hand poses for accurate upper-body avatar reconstruction. We then splat 3D Gaussians onto the obtained mesh, enabling high-quality rendering of dynamic avatars from novel viewpoints. Furthermore, we introduce MVFGA-MoCap, a multi-view upper-body motion capture dataset featuring controlled facial expression sequences, diverse hand gestures, and free-form communication. Experiments show that MVFGA generates visually realistic avatars with high-fidelity facial expressions and hand motions, outperforming baselines for upper-body avatar animation. Project page: https://dfki-av.github.io/MVFGA/

</details>


### [10] [RUTA: Principled Visual Token Allocation via Rate-Utility Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04132)
*Jian Zou,Xiaoyu Xu,Zhihua Wang,Yilin Wang,Balu Adsumilli,Kede Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RUTA是一种基于率-效用权衡的视觉token分配方法，通过联合学习保留哪些token以及为每个图像-查询对分配多少token，在LLM前进行视觉token缩减。在五个基准上，仅使用2.0%-4.2%的视觉token即可保留88.2%-94.4%的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像和长视频为视觉-语言模型提供丰富上下文，但产生的长视觉token序列使LLM侧计算和内存成本高昂。现有token缩减方法要么使用固定缩减率，要么依赖方法特定的学习阈值或重要性预测器，缺乏统一且自适应的分配原则。

Method: RUTA在LLM之前进行token缩减，联合学习（1）保留哪些token以及（2）为每个图像-查询对分配多少token。具体地：构造查询条件的候选token，为每个候选预测保留概率；训练时这些概率参数化独立的Bernoulli门，其求和提供关于token数量的可微估计；保留的token作为锚点，根据语义相似性和空间邻近性聚合非保留token的信息；通过带惩罚的率-效用目标函数优化，平衡下游任务损失与期望token使用量。

Result: 在五个基准上取平均值，相对于各骨干的全token基线，RUTA在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上仅使用2.0%的视觉token即保留88.2%的任务性能，在Qwen3-VL-8B上仅使用4.2%的视觉token即保留94.4%的任务性能。

Conclusion: RUTA通过原则上合理的率-效用优化框架，在极低token预算下实现了显著的性能保持，表明自适应、可学习的token缩减能够在计算效率和任务性能之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: High-resolution images and long videos provide vision-language models with rich context for multimodal reasoning and fine-grained perception, but the resulting long visual token sequences make large language model-side computation and memory costly. Existing visual token reducers often operate at prescribed rates, while recent methods adapt token counts across inputs using method-specific learned thresholds or importance predictors. We introduce RUTA, a principled Rate-Utility Token Allocation method that performs pre-LLM reduction by jointly learning which tokens to retain and how many to allocate to each image-query pair. RUTA constructs query-conditioned candidate tokens and predicts a retention probability for each candidate. During training, these probabilities parameterize independent Bernoulli gates, while their sum provides a differentiable training-time estimate of the token count for each pair. Retained tokens serve as anchors that aggregate information from non-retained tokens according to semantic affinity and spatial proximity. RUTA is optimized with a penalized rate-utility objective that balances downstream task loss against expected token usage. Averaged across five benchmarks and measured relative to each backbone's full-token baseline, RUTA uses only $2.0\%$ and $4.2\%$ of visual tokens while preserving $88.2\%$ and $94.4\%$ of task performance on LLaVA-NeXT-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B, respectively.

</details>


### [11] [UG-UMRE: Uncertainty-Guided Modality Augmentation and Distributional Calibration for Unified Multimodal Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04949)
*Bo Kong,Liruiz Jia,Yi Liang,Chao Liu,Dongfang Han,Tianwei Yan,Yuan Liu,Shengquan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UG-UMRE网络，通过不确定性驱动的单模态增强模块（UDUA）和联合随机不确定性对齐模块（JAUA）解决统一多模态关系抽取中因忽略固有随机不确定性导致的噪声传播问题，以及不同模态分布间深层异质性阻碍对齐的问题，在三个基准数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态关系抽取（UMRE）研究存在两个关键问题：一是忽略了固有的随机不确定性导致噪声传播；二是不同模态分布间存在深层异质性，阻碍了跨模态对齐。

Method: 提出UG-UMRE网络，包含两个核心模块：1) UDUA（不确定性驱动单模态增强模块）——基于变分信息瓶颈将特征建模为高斯分布，结合不确定性感知的自监督对比学习机制来过滤噪声并保持语义一致性；2) JAUA（联合随机不确定性对齐模块）——作为全局语义预校准机制，利用概率分布一致性构建共享潜在空间，通过同步跨模态统计属性消除分布差距。

Result: 在UMRE、MORE和MNRE三个基准数据集上达到最先进性能。进一步分析验证了UDUA和JAUA模块的可插拔性和有效性。

Conclusion: UG-UMRE通过不确定性引导的方式有效解决了UMRE中的噪声传播和跨模态分布对齐难题，所提出的两类模块具有可插拔性，为多模态关系抽取提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Relation Extraction (UMRE) aims to identify intra-modal and cross-modal relations between textual entities and visual objects. However, existing UMRE studies still encounter two critical issues: ignoring inherent aleatoric uncertainty causes noise propagation, and deep-seated heterogeneity between distinct modal distributions hinders alignment. To address these issues, we propose the Uncertainty-Guided UMRE Network (UG-UMRE). Specifically, we design an Uncertainty-Driven Unimodal Augmentation (UDUA) module, which models features as Gaussian distributions based on the Variational Information Bottleneck. By incorporating an uncertainty-aware self-supervised contrastive learning mechanism, UDUA effectively filters out noise while maintaining semantic consistency. Furthermore, we introduce the Joint Aleatoric Uncertainty Alignment (JAUA) module as a global semantic pre-calibration mechanism. JAUA leverages probabilistic distribution consistency to construct a shared latent space, eliminating the distributional gap by synchronizing cross-modal statistical properties, thereby laying a robust foundation for fine-grained interaction. Experiments on three benchmark datasets (UMRE, MORE, and MNRE) demonstrate that UG-UMRE achieves state-of-the-art performance. Further analysis validates the pluggable and effective performance of the proposed UDUA and JAUA modules.

</details>


### [12] [Dense Metric Depth Completion from Sparse Direct Time-of-Flight Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04737)
*Hakyeong Kim,Ruicheng Wang,Chengtang Yao,Jiaolong Yang,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用的稠密度量深度补全框架，能从稀疏dToF传感器测量中恢复稠密深度图。该框架采用深度引导的双分支Vision Transformer编码器，配合掩码联合注意力机制，并引入全面的dToF仿真管线生成训练数据。模型仅在合成数据上训练，即可在6个数据集和3个真实dToF设备上实现零样本泛化，在精度和计算效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接飞行时间（dToF）传感器能提供高精度度量深度且在恶劣条件下比间接ToF更鲁棒，但高制造成本和有限的光电二极管阵列导致深度图极度稀疏、低分辨率且含噪声，无法直接用于VR/XR、机器人和3D感知等需要稠密度量深度的任务。现有单目和深度补全方法难以处理dToF设备特有的采样模式和硬件伪影，在严重稀疏或噪声下性能急剧下降。

Method: 采用深度引导的双分支Vision Transformer编码器，分别处理RGB图像和稀疏dToF测量；提出掩码联合注意力模块，使深度token可靠地引导图像特征而不被覆盖。轻量级解码器无需扩散或精细后处理即可高效重建稠密度量深度。此外，设计全面的dToF仿真管线，再现闪光、亚VGA闪光和旋转传感器特性，包括硬件退化、不规则稀疏性和真实噪声分布。

Result: 模型完全在合成数据上训练，在6个数据集和3个真实dToF设备上实现了强零样本泛化能力，在精度和计算效率上均超越当前最先进方法，适用于不同传感器类型、稀疏程度和噪声条件。

Conclusion: 该框架为从稀疏dToF传感器进行稠密度量深度补全提供了鲁棒且实用的解决方案，代码和模型已开源，奠定了在多种真实设备上通用部署的基础。

Abstract: Direct Time-of-Flight (dToF) sensors provide highly accurate metric depth and are more robust than indirect ToF systems in challenging real-world conditions. However, their high manufacturing cost and limited photodiode array size produce depth maps that are extremely sparse, low-resolution, and noisy, making them unsuitable for VR/XR, robotics, and 3D perception tasks that require dense metric depth. Existing monocular and depth completion methods struggle to handle the unique sampling patterns and hardware artifacts of dToF devices, and their performance often deteriorates significantly under severe sparsity or noise. We present a generalizable framework for dense metric depth completion from sparse dToF measurements, capable of operating across diverse sensor types, sparsity levels, and noise conditions. Our model employs a depth-guided dual-branch Vision Transformer encoder that processes RGB images and sparse dToF measurements separately, while a masked joint attention module allows depth tokens to reliably guide image features without being overwritten by them. A lightweight decoder reconstructs dense metric depth efficiently, without diffusion-based or refinement-heavy post-processing. To address the scarcity of paired training data, we introduce a comprehensive dToF simulation pipeline that reproduces the characteristics of flash, sub-VGA flash, and rotating sensors, including hardware-induced degradation, irregular sparsity, and realistic noise distributions. Trained entirely on synthetic data, our model achieves strong zero-shot generalization across 6 datasets and 3 real dToF devices, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches in both accuracy and computational efficiency. This establishes a robust and practical solution for dense metric depth completion from sparse direct ToF sensors. Our code and models are open-sourced. See https://vclab.kaist.ac.kr/cvpr2026p3.

</details>


### [13] [TRNet: Topography-Guided Frequency Rectification and Structure-Aware Decoding for Multimodal Paddy Rice Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04154)
*Kaiwen Xiao,Chunlong Fu,Liping Zheng,Yanfeng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRNet模型，利用0.5米高分一号RGB影像与5米TanDEM-X DEM及坡度数据，通过视觉-地形双编码器、Topographic Energy-Spectral Rectification模块和Topography-guided Paddy Structure Decoder，在山地和丘陵地区实现高精度水稻制图，在内部测试集和外部陡坡测试集上IoU分别达85.10%和80.68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在山区和丘陵地区，地形导致光学外观变化并增加与视觉相似植被的混淆，使得利用甚高分辨率影像进行水稻制图极具挑战，现有方法难以有效应对地形干扰。

Method: 提出TRNet架构：1) 采用分离的视觉编码器和地形编码器以保留各模态特有特征；2) 在早期编码阶段引入Topographic Energy-Spectral Rectification，利用地形条件化的低频调制和非对称高频调节抑制陡坡杂波并增强低坡度水稻特征；3) 引入Topography-guided Paddy Structure Decoder，结合语义、水稻-背景边界和内部线索，并利用粗粒度地形作为上下文。

Result: TRNet在内部测试集Area A和外部测试集Area B（地形更陡、水稻覆盖率更低）上分别取得85.10%和80.68%的IoU，较原始Dual-Encoder U-Net分别提升9.15和18.83个百分点。消融实验和坡度分层结果证实，性能提升源于频率校正、结构学习以及陡坡误检的减少。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持将粗粒度地形作为上下文先验用于甚高分辨率水稻制图，表明地形引导的调制和结构解码策略能有效缓解山区复杂地形带来的精度损失。

Abstract: Mapping paddy rice from very-high-resolution imagery in mountainous and hilly regions is difficult because terrain alters optical appearance and increases confusion with visually similar vegetation. We present TRNet for 0.5-m GaoJing-1 red--green--blue (RGB) imagery, a 5-m TanDEM-X digital elevation model (DEM), and derived slope. Separate visual and terrain encoders preserve modality-specific features. At an early encoder stage, Topographic Energy-Spectral Rectification applies terrain-conditioned low-frequency modulation and asymmetric high-frequency regulation to suppress steep-slope clutter and conditionally enhance compatible low-slope rice cues. The Topography-guided Paddy Structure Decoder combines semantic, rice--background boundary, and interior cues, using coarse terrain as context. Experiments used an Area A internal test set and held-out Area B, which had steeper terrain and lower rice prevalence. TRNet achieved rice intersection-over-union (IoU) values of 85.10\% and 80.68\%, exceeding the original Dual-Encoder U-Net by 9.15 and 18.83 percentage points, respectively. Ablation and slope-stratified results linked these gains to frequency rectification, structure learning, and fewer steep-terrain false positives. The results support coarse topography as a contextual prior for very-high-resolution paddy rice mapping.

</details>


### [14] [Revisiting Pose Sensitivity in Splat-based Computed Tomography under Sparse-view Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04752)
*Kiseok Choi,Hyeongjun Cho,Inchul Kim,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对基于3D高斯分布的splat-based断层成像（CT）在真实稀疏视角条件下出现的条纹和带状伪影问题，通过分析发现伪影主要源于采集几何中的位姿不准确而非视角稀疏性本身，并提出了一个在重建过程中联合微调几何参数的稳定梯度框架，显著提升重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的splat-based断层成像在锥束稀疏视角CT中展现出高重建质量和快速收敛的优势，但在实际CT系统中存在有限且非均匀的视角分布时，会出现比传统重建方法更明显的条纹和带状伪影。作者通过分析发现这些伪影源于获取几何中的位姿误差，而现有方法未充分考虑这一问题，因此需要一种能够联合优化几何参数的框架来提升真实场景下的重建质量。

Method: 作者首先详细分析了splatting公式中的位姿敏感性，揭示了位姿扰动如何通过可微投影算子传播，以及为什么splat-based CT对几何错位特别敏感。在此基础上，推导出一个稳定的基于梯度的框架，在重建过程中联合精化几何参数（位姿），该框架保持轻量化并易于集成到现有管线中。

Result: 所提出的联合优化框架在真实稀疏视角条件下显著提升了重建保真度，同时保持了轻量化的计算开销，并且可以方便地集成到现有的splat-based重建管线中。实验表明，该方法有效缓解了由位姿不准确引起的条纹和带状伪影。

Conclusion: 本文确认了splat-based断层成像中伪影的主要来源是获取几何的位姿误差而非视角稀疏性，并提出了一个稳定、轻量、可集成的联合几何精化框架，在真实稀疏视角CT场景下大幅提升重建质量，为后续工作提供了重要的理论与实用基础。

Abstract: X-ray computed tomography (CT) reconstructs volumetric representations of objects from projection images obtained by transmitting X-rays through a target. Recent splat-based tomography, which represents a volume as a continuous distribution of 3D Gaussians, has demonstrated both high reconstruction quality and fast convergence in cone-beam sparse-view CT. However, when deployed in real CT systems with limited and non-uniform view distributions, we observe distinctive streak and strip artifacts that are far more pronounced than in conventional reconstruction methods. Through detailed analysis, we show that these artifacts primarily originate from pose inaccuracies in the acquisition geometry rather than from view sparsity itself. We revisit pose sensitivity in the splatting formulation and derive a stable gradient-based framework that jointly refines geometric parameters during reconstruction. Our study not only identifies how pose perturbations propagate through the differentiable projection operator but also reveals why splat-based CT is particularly vulnerable to geometric misalignment. The resulting formulation remains lightweight and easily integrable into existing pipelines while substantially improving reconstruction fidelity under real-world sparse-view conditions.

</details>

*Kishor Datta Gupta,Md. Mahfuzur Rahman,Fahad Rahman,Ahmed Rafi Hasan,Faysal Mehrab Chowdhury,Mohd Ariful Haque,Roy George*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriCLE是一个面向边缘平台的三模态视觉语言系统，用于飞机分类分组。它仅从单张RGB图像生成FLIR风格热像和伪LiDAR深度投影，在紧凑的Qwen3-VL骨架上融合对齐视图与任务指令。通过GSPO策略对齐，达到88.33%验证准确率和0.91加权F1，量化后适配8GB部署环境，每三模态样本处理1.48秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘平台进行空中观测时，需在有限内存、有限算力和间歇性连接条件下解读飞机图像。标准RGB识别模型和通用视觉语言模型在此场景下表现不足，尤其是当校准的热像和LiDAR飞机数据不可用时，需要一种能够从单一RGB图像出发、生成多模态表示并在边缘约束下进行工程相关分类的方法。

Method: TriCLE从单张RGB图像生成结构保持的FLIR风格热像视图和伪LiDAR深度投影，将对齐后的三模态视图与任务指令融合到紧凑的Qwen3-VL骨干网络中。模型对齐到基于推进方式、机身家族、尺寸、设计时代和配置的专家飞机分类体系。评估了监督微调、旋转保持SFT以及三种策略对齐方法（GRPO、GSPO和DAPO），其中序列级GSPO表现最佳。

Result: 序列级GSPO在有效飞机输出上达到88.33%验证准确率和0.91加权F1；在保留测试分区上达到78.00%准确率和0.793加权F1，同时保持94.00%可解析输出格式。经4-bit量化和注意力内存优化后，对齐的4B模型适配8GB部署目标，每三模态三元组处理时间为1.48秒。

Conclusion: TriCLE作为可解释、边缘可行的飞机分组实用原型具有潜力，但需要在真实对齐的热像和LiDAR传感器流上进行进一步验证。

Abstract: Edge platforms used for aerial observation must interpret aircraft imagery under limited memory, limited compute, and intermittent connectivity. This setting is difficult for standard RGB-only recognition models and general-purpose vision-language models, especially when calibrated thermal and LiDAR aircraft data are unavailable. We present TriCLE, an application-oriented tri-modal vision-language system for aircraft taxonomic grouping under edge constraints. From a single RGB aircraft image, TriCLE generates a structure-preserving FLIR-style thermal view and a pseudo-LiDAR depth projection, then fuses the aligned views with task instructions in a compact Qwen3-VL backbone. The model is aligned to an expert aircraft taxonomy based on propulsion, airframe family, size, design era, and configuration, so its outputs reflect engineering-relevant similarity rather than only surface appearance. We evaluate supervised fine-tuning, rotation-preserving SFT, and three policy-alignment strategies: GRPO, GSPO, and DAPO. Sequence-level GSPO gives the strongest validation performance, reaching 88.33\% validation accuracy and 0.91 weighted F1 on valid aircraft outputs. On a held-out aircraft test partition, GSPO achieves 78.00\% accuracy and 0.793 weighted F1 while preserving 94.00\% parseable output formatting. After 4-bit quantization and attention-memory optimization, the aligned 4B model fits an 8GB deployment target and processes each tri-modal triplet in 1.48 seconds. These results support TriCLE as a practical prototype for interpretable, edge-feasible aircraft grouping, while emphasizing the need for further validation on real aligned thermal and LiDAR sensor streams.

</details>


### [16] [Splat-Based Metal Artifact Reduction in Cone-Beam CT via Compact Attenuation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04764)
*Kiseok Choi,Jaemin Cho,Inchul Kim,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于splat的金属伪影消除框架，将物理合理的多色前向模型融入连续高斯表示（Gaussian Splatting）中，用于锥束CT重建。每个高斯通过紧凑的材料参数化编码能量依赖衰减，无需金属掩膜即可联合优化几何与材料属性，在模拟和真实锥束CT数据上比现有方法收敛更快、金属伪影抑制效果更好，同时保留高频结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线CT在遇到高衰减物体（如牙科填充物、骨科植入物）时会产生严重金属伪影。这些伪影源于X射线的多色性质——衰减随光子能量和材料成分强烈变化，打破了传统重建算法的单色假设。现有神经渲染方法试图通过可微多色投影模型解决这一问题，但仍存在平滑偏差、细结构丢失以及扩展到大规模锥束CT时计算代价过高的问题。

Method: 提出一种splat-based金属伪影消除框架，将物理基础的多色前向模型集成到连续高斯表示中用于锥束CT。核心创新包括：(1) 每个高斯使用紧凑的材料参数化编码其能量依赖衰减；(2) 无需依赖金属掩膜即可高效联合优化几何和材料属性；(3) 紧凑衰减公式能够捕捉生物组织和金属植入物的关键变异，解释金属引起的非线性同时保持高频结构。

Result: 在模拟和真实锥束CT扫描实验中，该方法比现有重建方法和神经场方法收敛显著更快，金属伪影抑制效果更有效。

Conclusion: 通过将物理多色X射线模型与高斯泼溅表示相结合，该方法在保持高频细节的同时有效抑制金属伪影，为大规模锥束CT金属伪影消除提供了高效且物理可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: X-ray computed tomography (CT) suffers from severe metal artifacts when high-attenuation objects such as dental fillings or orthopedic implants are present. These artifacts originate from the polychromatic nature of X-rays, where attenuation varies strongly with photon energy and material composition, breaking the monochromatic assumption used by conventional reconstruction algorithms. Recent neural rendering approaches attempt to address this mismatch through differentiable polychromatic projection models, but they still struggle with smoothness bias, loss of fine structures, and prohibitive computation when extended to large-scale cone-beam CT. We introduce a splat-based metal artifact reduction framework that incorporates a physically grounded polychromatic forward model into a continuous Gaussian representation for cone-beam CT. Each Gaussian encodes the energy-dependent attenuation of the underlying material using a compact material parameterization, which enables efficient joint optimization of geometric and material properties without relying on a metal mask. This compact attenuation formulation captures the essential variation across biological tissues and metallic implants, allowing our model to explain metal-induced nonlinearity while preserving high-frequency structure. Experiments on simulated and real cone-beam CT scans show that our method converges significantly faster and suppresses metal artifacts more effectively than existing reconstruction and neural field-based approaches.

</details>


### [17] [PADFormer: Pose-agnostic Anomaly Detection from Sparse View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04210)
*Ruiqi Wang,Yiming Qian,Fenggen Yu,Yuxuan Lu,Dakuo Wang,Hao Zhang,Jing Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PADFormer，一种基于图像空间的姿态无关异常检测方法，通过Vision Transformer直接重建无异常版本查询图像，无需3D重建，在PAD基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姿态无关异常检测（PAD）面临任意视角下异常的检测挑战，现有方法依赖复杂的3D重建，计算成本高且需要大量多视角数据，需要更高效、更通用的解决方案。

Method: 基于ViT的图像空间方法：1) 将跨视角掩码重建适配为仅用正常数据训练异常检测；2) 引入动态补丁选择与空间对齐机制，从稀疏参考视角下实现有效学习；3) 推理时使用多种掩码模式进行多次前向传播，生成无异常重建的集成结果，通过比较重建与查询图像检测异常。

Result: 在PAD基准上取得SOTA结果，同时在经典小样本异常检测（FSAD）任务上保持可比性能，展现高效性和泛化能力，且无需3D重建。

Conclusion: PADFormer证明纯图像空间方法可媲美甚至超越需要3D重建的复杂方法，在效率和泛化性上具有明显优势，为姿态无关异常检测提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Pose-agnostic Anomaly Detection (PAD) remains challenging as anomalies can appear under arbitrary viewpoints, requiring methods to handle significant pose variations. Existing approaches rely on complex 3D reconstruction, which are computationally expensive and require extensive multi-view data. We propose PADFormer, a novel image-space approach that leverages Vision Transformer (ViT) to directly reconstruct anomaly-free versions of query images while preserving pose information. Our key insight is to adapt cross-view masked reconstruction for anomaly detection through training exclusively on normal data, combined with dynamic patch selection and spatial alignment mechanisms that enable effective learning from sparse reference views under significant pose variations. During inference, we perform multiple forward passes with different masking patterns to generate an ensemble of anomaly-free reconstructions, ensuring comprehensive coverage of the query image. Anomalies are detected by comparing these reconstructions with the query image. PADFormer achieves state-of-the-art results on the PAD benchmark while maintaining comparable performance on classic few-shot anomaly detection (FSAD) tasks, demonstrating superior efficiency and generalization without requiring 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [18] [OmniVR: Joint Video-Audio Conditional Generation for Restoring Degraded Historical Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04224)
*Xin Lu,Zihao Fan,Mingchen Zhong,Jie Huang,Xueyang Fu,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniVR，首个联合音视频生成式修复模型，基于22B参数的音视频生成骨干网络（DiT），将老电影修复建模为条件生成任务，同时恢复视觉结构、时间运动和音频细节，并在六个视觉指标、音频质量及自然着色上超越所有先前方法，还构建了首个联合音视频修复基准OmniVRBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史影片同时遭受视觉和音频的双重退化（模糊、噪声、闪烁、嘶声、裁剪、丢失等），但现有方法对每个模态独立修复，导致质量缺口和跨模态不一致。需要统一的联合修复框架来同时解决视觉和音频退化并保证跨模态一致性。

Method: 基于22B参数音视频生成骨干构建统一多模态DiT，将低质量视频和音频编码为潜在条件，结合固定修复提示进行联合去噪。三个关键设计：(1)联合音视频退化pipeline模拟真实老片特征；(2)架构保留的T2AV到AV2AV转换加prompt annealing最大化保留生成先验；(3)首帧I2V锚定，配合损失重新加权和波形监督实现长视频外推与音频保真。

Result: 在200个真实历史片段上，OmniVR在所有六个视觉指标上超越所有先前方法，取得最佳音频质量，并产生自然的着色效果，是第一个同时解决这三个方面的方法。

Conclusion: OmniVR证明联合音视频生成式修复是可行的，统一的协调目标比独立多模态修复更能保留生成先验并保证跨模态一致性。提出OmniVRBench作为首个联合音视频修复基准，并提供公开代码、权重和项目页面。

Abstract: Historical films suffer from co-occurring visual and audio degradations---blur, noise, flicker, hiss, clipping, and dropout---yet existing methods restore each modality independently, leaving quality gaps and cross-modal inconsistency. We present OmniVR, the first joint audio-video generative restoration model. Built upon a 22B-parameter audio-video generation backbone, OmniVR formulates restoration as conditional generation within a unified multimodal DiT: the low-quality video and audio are encoded as latent conditions, combined with a fixed restoration prompt, and jointly denoised to recover visual structure, temporal motion, and acoustic detail under one coordinated objective. Three key designs enable this adaptation: (1) a joint audio-video degradation pipeline that simulates real old-film characteristics from Internet-collected data; (2) an architecture-preserving text-to-audio-video (T2AV) to audio-video-to-audio-video (AV2AV) transition with prompt annealing that maximally retains the generative prior; and (3) first-frame image-to-video (I2V) anchoring with loss reweighting and waveform supervision for long-video extrapolation and audio fidelity. We also propose OmniVRBench, the first benchmark that evaluates audio-video restoration across visual quality, audio quality, temporal consistency, and audio-visual synchrony on 200 real historical clips. OmniVR surpasses all prior methods on all six visual metrics, achieves the best audio quality, and produces natural colorization---the first method to jointly address all three aspects. Code and weights will be publicly released. Project Page: https://xin1u.github.io/OminiVR_PAGE/

</details>


### [19] [SIGNPOST-Bench: Benchmarking Text-Vision Conflict Resolution in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04244)
*Sirun Li,Minghao Liu,Ling Dai,Yong Li,Haoxin Lyu,Junting Zhou,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIGNPOST-Bench，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在文本与视觉线索冲突时如何仲裁的受控反事实基准。通过将每张图像转换为原始、空白、相似、随机和对抗五类变体（共5,111组、25,555张图像），注入局部场景文本干扰，发现对抗变体将中位定位误差从282km提升到1,347km（4.8倍），且6.5-20.1%的对抗样本预测落在注入目标50km内。结果表明视觉地理定位可作为场景文本仲裁的连续诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MLLM基准很少揭示模型在视觉与文本线索冲突时如何仲裁这些证据源。需要一个受控的反事实基准来系统评估模型在冲突多模态证据下的行为，特别是文本注入如何影响模型的地理定位预测。

Method: 构建SIGNPOST-Bench基准：将每个源图像通过合成、局部场景文本干预转化为五种变体（原始、空白、相似、随机、对抗），保持非文本内容不变，实现成对的定位性能变化测量和文本冲突引发的目标地理定向漂移测量。数据集包含4个来源的5,111组、25,555张图像，评估来自7个提供商的20个MLLMs。

Result: 对抗变体使中位定位误差从282km升至1,347km（4.8倍增长）；可地理编码的对抗样本中，6.5-20.1%的预测落在注入目标50km内；所有被评估模型从空白到对抗变体均表现出目标距离正向平均配对减少。兼容、无关和冲突文本替换产生不同影响，且干净输入定位性能不能完全预测对冲突文本的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 视觉地理定位可作为评估MLLMs场景文本仲裁能力的连续诊断工具，SIGNPOST-Bench提供了一个受控框架来系统评估MLLMs如何解决冲突的多模态证据。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) make grounded predictions in real-world scenes by combining visual and textual cues, yet existing benchmarks rarely reveal how they arbitrate between these evidence sources when they conflict. We introduce SIGNPOST-Bench, a controlled counterfactual benchmark for evaluating text-vision conflict resolution. Each source image is transformed into a counterfactual quintuplet of Original, Blank, Similar, Random, and Adversarial variants. Synthetic, localized scene-text interventions are designed to preserve non-textual content, enabling paired measurements of changes in localization performance and directed shifts toward geographic targets introduced by conflicting text. SIGNPOST-Bench contains 5,111 counterfactual groups and 25,555 image variants from four datasets. We evaluate 20 MLLMs from seven providers. Compared with Original images, Adversarial variants raise median localization error from 282 km to 1,347 km, a 4.8-fold increase. Among geocodable adversarial samples, 6.5-20.1% of predictions lie less than 50 km from the injected target across models, and every evaluated model exhibits a positive mean paired reduction in target distance from Blank to Adversarial. Compatible, unrelated, and conflicting text replacements produce distinct effects on model predictions, while clean-input localization performance does not fully predict robustness to conflicting text. These results establish visual geolocation as a continuous diagnostic of scene-text arbitration and provide a controlled framework for evaluating how MLLMs resolve conflicting multimodal evidence.

</details>


### [20] [Binding Biometrics with AI Agent Identifiers for Delegation of Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04292)
*Joseph Geo Benjamin,Anil K Jain,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BIND框架，利用生物特征密码系统将用户生物特征与AI代理身份ID及权限范围安全绑定，生成可验证的授权令牌。AI代理可向身份审计者出示该令牌，同时完成生物认证和任务授权验证，实现不可否认的人类控制与授权证明。实验基于人脸特征实现，达到零误匹配率下96%的真正匹配率，支持1024位代理令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着agentic AI系统（能够自主执行任务的AI代理）的快速发展，AI代理执行关键任务时缺乏明确的人类授权机制成为突出问题。生物识别作为最可靠的身份认证手段之一，有望解决AI代理的授权委托与责任归属问题，但目前尚缺乏将生物识别与AI代理身份绑定以实现可审计授权的系统化框架。

Method: BIND框架基于生物特征密码系统（biometric cryptosystem）的思想，在代理授权时将人类用户的生物特征数据与AI代理身份ID及任务约束权限范围进行安全绑定，生成令牌/标识符。该令牌可由AI代理出示给身份审计者（Identity Auditor），审计者同时执行生物认证、恢复代理ID和权限范围，实现实时用户认证和不可否认的授权证明。实现上采用基于人脸特征（深度神经网络提取）的模糊承诺（fuzzy commitment）结构，包括一个特征适配模块将实数特征嵌入转换为适合turbo纠错码的定长二进制表示。

Result: 实验验证了所提出的人脸密码系统的实际可行性，在零误匹配率（FMR=0）条件下达到96%的真正匹配率（TMR），并支持1024位代理令牌。

Conclusion: BIND框架通过生物特征密码系统将人类用户授权与AI代理身份绑定，为AI代理的责任归属和可审计授权提供了可行的技术方案，实验结果表明在实用安全性和效率方面均具可行性。

Abstract: The proliferation of agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems has raised serious questions about the accountability for tasks performed by AI agents. Ideally, an AI agent must not be allowed to perform critical tasks without explicit authorization by a human operator. Since biometric recognition is one of the most reliable approaches for authenticating individuals, it has the potential to enable authenticated delegation of authority to AI agents. In this work, we present a framework called BIND, which leverages ideas from the field of biometric cryptosystems, to securely bind biometric data of the human user to the AI agent identity (ID) and authority scope (task-specific constraints) at the time of agent authorization. This token/identifier can be presented by the AI agent to an Identity Auditor, who simultaneously performs biometric authentication and recovers the agent ID and scope, thereby enabling real-time user authentication and establishing a non-repudiable proof of human control and delegation of authority. We also provide a practical implementation of the proposed BIND framework based on face features extracted using standard deep neural network models. To facilitate this implementation, we propose a feature adaptation module that transforms real-valued feature embeddings into fixed-length binary representations suitable for a fuzzy commitment construct based on turbo error correcting codes. Experiments demonstrate the practical feasibility of the proposed face cryptosystem, achieving a True Match Rate of $96\%$ at zero False Match Rate and supporting $1024$-bit agent tokens.

</details>


### [21] [CLIP-CC-Bench: Evaluating Paragraph-Level Video Descriptions in Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04302)
*Mukhtiar Ali,Harsh Dubey,Sugam Mishra,Chulwoo Pack*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍CLIP-CC-Bench，一个用于评估视频语言模型长视频段落级描述能力的基准测试套件，基于5小时电影内容构建90秒片段，采用五种LLM嵌入模型的集成和粗细粒度两级语义匹配方法，评估了17个SOTA模型并提供标准化评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频语言模型评估主要聚焦于短视频片段和单句指标，缺乏对模型生成准确的长时、段落级描述能力的系统评估，存在评估空白。

Method: 构建基于5小时电影内容、90秒片段配专家段落级参考描述的CLIP-CC-Bench评估套件；集成五种SOTA LLM嵌入模型以减少单一模型偏差；采用粗粒度语义匹配和细粒度语义匹配两种互补方法；通过Borda聚合进行排名；使用评委间一致性和bootstrap排名稳定性量化协议内部可靠性。

Result: 评估了17个SOTA视频语言模型，报告了Borda聚合排名和平均分数，并通过评委间一致性和bootstrap排名稳定性验证了评估协议的内部可靠性。

Conclusion: CLIP-CC-Bench为长视频描述提供了实用评估框架，填补了现有短片段和仅QA基准的空白，并发布了标准化评估脚本、模型输出和聚合工具以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Benchmarking video-language models has largely focused on short clips and single-sentence metrics, leaving open whether current systems can generate accurate long-form, paragraph-level descriptions. We introduce CLIP-CC-Bench, an evaluation suite for long-form video description built from 5 hours of movie content segmented into 90-second clips, each paired with an expert-written paragraph-style reference. The evaluation suite employs an ensemble of five state-of-the-art LLM-based embedding models to increase reliability and mitigate single-model bias, and applies two complementary methodologies: (i) coarse-grained semantic matching and (ii) fine-grained semantic matching to compare model-generated descriptions against CLIP-CC-Bench references. Using this framework, we evaluate 17 state-of-the-art video-language models and report both their Borda-aggregated rankings and their average scores on CLIP-CC-Bench. We further quantify the protocol's internal reliability through inter-judge agreement and bootstrap ranking stability. We release standardized evaluation scripts, model outputs, and aggregation tools at https://github.com/Multimodal-Intelligence-Lab/CLIP-CC-Bench to support reproducibility. CLIP-CC-Bench provides a practical evaluation framework for long-form video description, filling a gap left by existing short-clip and QA-only benchmarks.

</details>


### [22] [iStructTab: Structured Feature Sequencing for Multimodal Learning of Image and Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04348)
*Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Md Younus Ahamed,Prashnna Gyawali,Gianfranco Doretto,Donald A. Adjeroh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GEDS（Graph-Enhanced Descriptor Sequencing）结构化特征排序算法，通过相似度图优化统计描述符并确定有效特征顺序，并将其集成到基于顺序感知记忆token的高效Transformer框架iStructTab中，在图文表格多模态基准上提升了预测性能与鲁棒性，减少特征分散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像与表格数据的多模态学习常因特征表示无效而受到损害，导致特征冗余、分散和泛化问题，现有方法缺乏对表格特征结构化排序的显式建模。

Method: 基于列排列问题（CPP）原理，通过相似度图计算精炼特征统计描述符，系统性地确定有效特征排序；在顺序感知的高效Transformer框架中引入顺序感知记忆token，并通过专用损失函数显式遵循推导出的特征排序。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上的实验表明，iStructTab有效减少了特征分散，提升了预测性能与鲁棒性，验证了结构化特征排序在多模态学习中的重要性。

Conclusion: 结构化特征排序对于提升图像与表格多模态学习的性能具有重要意义，GEDS与iStructTab框架为多模态特征表示与融合提供了一种有效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal learning of images and tabular data is often impaired by ineffective representations, resulting in redundancy, dispersion, and generalization problems. To tackle this challenge, we introduce Graph-Enhanced Descriptor Sequencing (GEDS), a structured feature sequencing algorithm grounded in principles from the Column Permutation Problem (CPP). GEDS refines statistical descriptors of the features through similarity graph-based computations, systematically determining an effective feature sequencing. We incorporate GEDS within an order-aware efficient transformer framework, utilizing order-aware memory tokens that explicitly adhere to the derived feature sequencing via a dedicated loss function. Experimental results across multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that iStructTab effectively minimizes feature dispersion, improving predictive performance and robustness, and highlighting the significance of structured feature sequencing in multimodal learning.

</details>


### [23] [Poly-OPD: Heterogeneous Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Capability-Selectable Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04349)
*Siming Fu,Haojun Xu,Ruizhe He,Zheming Fu,Hualiang Wang,Jie Huang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Mingchen Zhong,Weihu Huang,Xiaoxuan He,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Poly-OPD是一个框架，用于将多个异构教师模型（如FLUX.1-dev和Z-Image）的互补优势整合到一个紧凑的流匹配学生模型中（2.5B SD3.5-Medium）。通过像素桥接、DINOv2空间监督、梯度兼容性诊断的适配器组织和差距感知课程学习，Poly-OPD成功将GenEval从67.3提升至73.3（超越两个更大的教师模型），并将DrawBench HPSv3从9.34提升至11.35。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开源文生图模型各有所长：有的在偏好对齐的美学上表现优秀，有的在组合指令遵循上更忠实。然而，不同模型之间自编码器和噪声调度的差异使得将它们的优势相互转移变得困难。作者希望构建一个框架，能将多个异构教师模型的互补能力整合到单一紧凑的学生模型中。

Method: Poly-OPD包含四个核心组件：(1) 通过像素桥接进行在线策略蒸馏，将学生生成的图像用所选教师的编码器重新编码，并在教师噪声调度下从幅度匹配的噪声水平进行细化；(2) 在冻结的DINOv2空间中进行目标匹配，以实现跨不兼容潜在空间的监督；(3) 利用梯度兼容性诊断组织适配器——注意力LoRA模块在教师之间共享，而前馈适配器保持教师特定；(4) 采用差距感知课程学习，将更多训练分配到学生仍落后于教师的组合类别上，随着差距缩小动态转移训练重点。

Result: 将FLUX.1-dev和Z-Image蒸馏到2.5B SD3.5-Medium学生模型中，GenEval从67.3提升到73.3，不仅超越了学生基线，还超过了两个更大的教师模型；DrawBench HPSv3从9.34提升到11.35，成功将两个教师的互补优势整合到一个可切换的模型中。

Conclusion: Poly-OPD证明了通过像素桥接和DINOv2空间监督可以克服异构教师之间潜在空间不兼容的问题，实现多教师互补能力的有效整合。梯度兼容性诊断和差距感知课程策略进一步确保了在不同教学能力之间进行无干扰的蒸馏，最终在紧凑模型中获得超越更大教师的效果。

Abstract: Leading open text-to-image models often carry complementary strengths: one may lead on preference-aligned aesthetics while another follows compositional instructions more faithfully. However, differences in their autoencoders and noise schedules make it difficult to transfer these strengths across models. In this paper, we present Poly-OPD, a framework that can consolidate complementary strengths of heterogeneous teachers into a single compact flow-matching student. To bridge the incompatible latent spaces of different teachers, Poly-OPD performs on-policy distillation through a pixel bridge. Each student-generated image is re-encoded by a selected teacher's encoder and refined from a noise level matched by magnitude under the teacher's noise schedule. The resulting target is further matched to the student in frozen DINOv2 space, enabling supervision across incompatible latent spaces. To retain complementary capabilities without cross-teacher interference, Poly-OPD uses a gradient compatibility diagnostic to organize its adapters: attention LoRA modules are shared across teachers, whereas feed-forward adapters remain teacher-specific. During distillation, a gap-aware curriculum devotes more training to compositional categories where the student still falls short of the teacher. As each gap narrows, training shifts toward categories with larger remaining gaps. By distilling FLUX.1-dev and Z-Image into a 2.5B SD3.5-Medium student, Poly-OPD improves GenEval from 67.3 to 73.3, surpassing both larger teachers, and raises DrawBench HPSv3 from 9.34 to 11.35, consolidating both strengths within a switchable model.

</details>


### [24] [Image Classification Using CNN-QNN Hybrid Model with Optimized Correlated Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04379)
*Minseo Seong,Youngwook Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过优化CNN特征相关性来提升量子神经网络（QNN）图像分类性能的方法。与以往使用正交分解预处理不同，该方法有意引入与QNN物理特性更兼容的关联特征，利用量子纠缠表示关联态的优势。数学推导和蒙特卡洛模拟表明，特征间平均相关性为0.5时分类精度最优，为此引入相关性正则化项来调控CNN输出特征的相关矩阵。在CIFAR-10、Fashion-MNIST和雷达微多普勒三个数据集上的实验证实，适度中间相关性一致地提升了分类精度并降低了方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的CNN-QNN混合方法通常采用正交分解预处理特征，这忽略了QNN特有量子纠缠能力的优势。作者希望通过精心设计的特征相关性来对齐QNN的纠缠结构，从而超越经典神经网络无法实现的性能和稳定性。

Method: 该方法包括：(1) 在CNN输出上添加相关性正则化项，驱动特征相关矩阵的非对角元素趋近目标常数；(2) 通过QNN输出的数学推导和蒙特卡洛模拟确定最优平均相关性约为0.5；(3) 构建量子-经典混合模型，在三个二分类任务上评估不同相关性水平（低、高、未调控、中间）对分类性能的影响。

Result: 在CIFAR-10（汽车vs卡车）、Fashion-MNIST（衬衫vs外套）和雷达微多普勒（机器狗vs非机器人）三个数据集上，适度中间相关性一致地提升了分类精度，同时降低了精度方差。相比低、高或未调控的相关性设置，中间相关性（约0.5）表现最优，且无需修改量子电路。

Conclusion: 通过将CNN特征统计特性与QNN的纠缠结构对齐（即引入适度中间相关性），可以在不改变量子电路的情况下提升混合模型的分类精度和稳定性。该研究还表明，随着可用量子比特数量的增加，QNN有潜力超越经典分类器的性能。

Abstract: We propose a method to optimize the correlation among convolutional neural network (CNN) features that are used as inputs to quantum neural network (QNN) to enhance image classification accuracy. Unlike prior approaches that employ orthogonal decomposition as preprocessing, we intentionally introduce correlated features that are more physically compatible with QNN. This design leverages the QNN's inherent ability to exploit quantum entanglement for representing correlated states-an advantage unavailable to classical neural networks. We hypothesize that aligning feature correlations with the entanglement structure of QNN improves binary classification performance. Based on a mathematical derivation of QNN outputs, Monte Carlo simulations indicate that an average correlation between features of 0.5 yields optimal classification accuracy. To validate this finding, we evaluate a quantum-classical hybrid model on three tasks: CIFAR-10 (automobile vs. truck), Fashion-MNIST (shirt vs. coat), and radar micro-Doppler signatures (robotic dogs vs. non-robots). To regulate feature correlations, we introduce a correlation-regularization term on the outputs of the CNN, driving the off-diagonal entries of the feature correlation matrix toward a target constant. Across all datasets, inducing intermediate correlation consistently improved accuracy compared to low, high, or unregulated correlations, while also reducing classification accuracy variance. These results demonstrate that imposing moderate feature correlations-without modifying the quantum circuit-enhances classification accuracy and stability by aligning feature statistics with the QNN's entanglement structure. This study highlights the potential of QNN to surpass the performance of classical classifiers as more qubits become available.

</details>


### [25] [ReGround: Restoring Visual Grounding in Multi-Step Reasoning through Self-Diagnosis and Visual Re-Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04385)
*Lei Peng,Shuai Lv,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReGround框架，通过图像重新注入与目标化自诊断相结合，解决VLM在多步推理中逐渐丧失视觉锚定的问题，实验证明在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在多步推理过程中会逐渐丧失视觉锚定能力：推理链越长，后续推理步骤越依赖语言先验而非图像证据。论文识别出一个一致的基准级信号：注意力熵在Round 1下降，图像重新注入后回升。然而，简单的图像重新注入若无目标化诊断甚至可能损害性能，因此需要找到有效的重新检视视觉证据的方法。

Method: 提出ReGround两阶段框架。第一阶段通过能力引导（capability bootstrapping），利用同一模型家族中更强的变体仅在数据构建阶段提供诊断脚手架；第二阶段策略模型学习自主诊断，在推理时无需外部工具或架构修改即可实现有选择的视觉证据重新检视。该方法结合两个核心组件：图像重新注入和目标化自诊断。

Result: 在8个基准、2种VLM骨干网络上取得一致收益，尤其在视觉密集型多步推理任务上表现突出。相比工具增强基线，推理开销仅为适度增加。关键发现是：准确自诊断能带来数个百分点的大幅收益，且策略模型通过引导训练后保留大部分辅助增益。

Conclusion: ReGround验证了图像重新注入与自诊断质量是VLM视觉重新检视有效性的关键因素，诊断质量决定了重新检视是帮助还是损害。该方法无需架构修改或外部工具即可显著提升VLM在多步推理中的视觉锚定能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often lose visual grounding during multi-step reasoning: as reasoning chains grow longer, later inference steps rely increasingly on language priors rather than image evidence. We identify a consistent benchmark-level signature associated with this degradation: across 2,510 re-examined samples from four benchmarks, attention entropy over image tokens typically decreases during Round 1 and rises again after image re-injection.
  However, we find that effective visual re-examination requires two complementary ingredients: image re-injection and targeted self-diagnosis. Without targeted diagnosis, re-examination can even hurt performance, whereas accurate self-diagnosis yields substantial gains -- a swing of several points on key benchmarks, indicating that diagnostic quality is a key factor in whether re-examination helps or hurts in our setting. We present ReGround, a two-stage framework that teaches VLMs to self-diagnose grounding failures and selectively re-examine visual evidence, without architectural modifications or external tools. Through capability bootstrapping, a stronger variant from the same model family provides diagnostic scaffolding only during data construction, while the policy model learns to diagnose autonomously at inference time and retains most of the assisted gains.
  Experiments on eight benchmarks across two VLM backbones demonstrate consistent gains, especially on visually intensive multi-step reasoning tasks, while incurring only modest inference overhead relative to tool-augmented baselines. Project page: https://sespoir.github.io/reground-page/ . Code: https://github.com/sespoir/ReGround .

</details>


### [26] [Free-Lunch Augmentation by Revisiting Diffusion-Based Data Generation for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04394)
*Zijian Zhuang,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型数据增强的跨域小样本目标检测方法SITN，通过将域间隙分解为视觉间隙和语义间隙，分别采用减弱噪声添加和背景修补策略来合成有效训练数据，在多个跨域基准上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域小样本目标检测(CDFSOD)面临域间隙大和训练数据稀缺的双重挑战。现有扩散模型数据增强方法因域间隙过大而失效，生成的数据甚至导致性能低于原始图像。作者重新审视数据增强这一自然但未被充分探索的途径，并深入分析扩散模型在跨域场景下失效的根本原因。

Method: 提出SITN方法，将域间隙分解为两类分别处理：(1) 视觉间隙——通过向扩散模型添加减弱噪声来缓解其在专家域无法区分噪声与有用信息的问题，具体由Generation Module实现；(2) 语义间隙——利用背景语义在域间的间隙远小于前景语义这一发现，通过背景修补(background inpainting)来桥接，由Selection Module动态选择修补区域。

Result: 在6个CDFSOD数据集和4个跨域小样本分割(CDFSS)数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了该方法能合成有效训练数据，取得新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过将跨域间隙细分为视觉和语义两大类并分别施策，扩散模型数据增强可以在CDFSOD中发挥有效作用，证明了数据增强这一经典思路在极端跨域小样本场景下的可行性。

Abstract: Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection (CDFSOD) aims to transfer knowledge from data-rich upstream generic domains to downstream expert domains using scarce training data, where the significant domain gap and data scarcity make it an unsolved challenge. To address this problem, we revisit a natural yet underexplored approach in CDFSOD: data augmentation, by directly synthesizing data through diffusion models to supplement limited training samples. However, due to large domain gaps, we find that current diffusion methods cannot produce good results, leading to performance even lower than using the original images. To address these limitations, we divide the domain gaps into visual gaps and semantic gaps for separate analysis. For the visual gap, we find that the diffusion model cannot distinguish noise from useful information on expert domains, which can be mitigated by adding weakened noise. For the semantic gap, we find that the background semantics shows much smaller gaps between domains than foreground semantics, and we can bridge this gap by background inpainting. Based on the above analysis, we propose a method (Selective Inpainting with Tailored Noise, SITN) to dynamically take different strategies for downstream data synthesis based on their different gaps from the general domain, including a Generation Module for adding tailored noise and a Selection Module to dynamically select the inpainting regions. Extensive experiments on 6 datasets of CDFSOD and 4 datasets of cross-domain few-shot segmentation (CDFSS) validate that we can synthesize helpful data, achieving new state-of-the-art performance. Our codes is available at https://github.com/zzzzj311-droid/Free-Lunch-SITN

</details>


### [27] [CofactVLA: Deconfounding Vision-Language-Action Models via Counterfactual Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04396)
*Yan Zhang,Yinan Wu,Haoran Duan,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CofactVLA，一种基于双路径反事实去混杂图（DDG）的因果干预框架，用于解决VLA模型中的"视觉主导"（vision-override）现象。通过动态构建语言掩码的反事实分支，结合动作级正交投影引导（OPG）和特征级反事实协方差缩减（CCR）两种机制，有效隔离并中和视觉混杂因素。实验在多个仿真基准上达到SOTA，并在真实机器人实验中于分布外场景取得52.3%的绝对成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中虽然取得了显著进展，但存在严重的"视觉主导"现象：由于视觉流与语言指令之间的模态不平衡，模型倾向于过度拟合视觉混杂因素（如显著物体或熟悉布局）而非真正遵循语言指令，导致因果混淆。这种偏差阻碍了模型的泛化能力，本文旨在系统性缓解这一偏见。

Method: 1) 将动作生成过程形式化为双路径去混杂图（DDG），提出CofactVLA因果干预框架。2) 在单次前向传播中动态构建语言掩码的反事实分支，用于隔离视觉混杂因素。3) 引入两种协同机制：Action-Level Orthogonal Projection Guidance（OPG）通过几何投影将事实速度场投影离开反事实视觉偏差方向，提取纯语义意图；Feature-Level Counterfactual Covariance Reduction（CCR）通过惩罚协方差差值的正特征空间来显式抑制视觉捷径，同时保留因果语言意图。

Result: 在多个仿真基准上达到新的SOTA性能。在真实机器人实验中，CofactVLA在分布外场景下相比基线取得了52.3%的绝对成功率提升，验证了该方法在弥合泛化差距方面的因果有效性。

Conclusion: CofactVLA通过因果干预框架有效缓解了VLA模型中的视觉主导偏差，在仿真和真实机器人实验中均验证了方法的有效性，为VLA模型在分布外场景下的泛化提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have driven significant progress in robotic manipulation, yet they fundamentally struggle with the vision-override phenomenon. Driven by the severe modality imbalance between dense visual streams and sparse linguistic instructions, VLAs frequently fall prey to causal confusion. Instead of treating language as the primary causal driver, the policy entirely bypasses the original instruction by overfitting to spurious visual confounders, such as prominent objects or familiar layouts. To systematically alleviate this bias, we formalize the process of action generation as a Dual-path Deconfounding Graph (DDG) and propose CofactVLA, a novel causal intervention framework. By dynamically constructing a language-masked counterfactual branch within a single forward pass, CofactVLA isolates and neutralizes visual confounders through two synergistic mechanisms. First, Action-Level Orthogonal Projection Guidance (OPG) geometrically projects the factual velocity field away from the counterfactual visual bias during continuous flow matching, extracting the pure semantic intent. Second, Feature-Level Counterfactual Covariance Reduction (CCR) mathematically deconfounds latent representations by penalizing the positive eigenspace of the covariance difference, explicitly suppressing dominant visual shortcuts while preserving the causal language intent. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CofactVLA establishes a new state-of-the-art across diverse simulation benchmarks. Beyond simulation, real-world robot experiments demonstrate the causal efficacy of our method in bridging the generalization gap, yielding a 52.3\% absolute success rate gain under out-of-distribution scenarios.

</details>


### [28] [Faster-WAM: Efficient Inference-Time Future Conditioning for Robust World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04404)
*Weiheng Zhao,Haoyi Jiang,Xin Shi,Liu Liu,Fan Huang,Zhizhong Su,Wei Sui,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Faster-WAM，一种高效的未来条件世界动作模型，通过稀疏未来条件框架（SparseMoT和Interval KV-Fusion）在保持未来表示的同时避免昂贵的视频-动作交互计算，实现了性能与效率的更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）面临两难困境：联合式WAMs（Joint-WAMs）在推理时保留未来感知表示但计算成本过高，而高效替代方案则在推理时移除未来建模，可能损失时序推理带来的鲁棒性优势。作者重新审视未来表示在WAMs中的作用，发现推理时的未来条件对于分布偏移下的泛化至关重要。

Method: Faster-WAM提出稀疏未来条件框架，包括两个核心组件：1) SparseMoT用选择性视频-动作交互替代逐层融合，仅在紧凑的网络阶段子集上进行未来条件化；2) Interval KV-Fusion在不增加注意力复杂度的条件下聚合多深度未来表示。该框架计算一次未来表示并在动作去噪过程中选择性复用。

Result: 在分布偏移的LIBERO-Plus基准上，Faster-WAM将成功率从Fast-WAM的49.14%提升到73.57%，同时比Joint-WAM快2.21倍。在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0上达到最先进性能，并在真实世界操控中展现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 推理时的未来条件化对于WAMs在分布偏移下的泛化至关重要，Faster-WAM通过稀疏未来条件框架证明了可以在不牺牲未来表示鲁棒性收益的前提下大幅提升效率，实现了性能-效率权衡的实质性改进。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot manipulation by learning how the environment evolves beyond the current observation. However, existing approaches face a fundamental dilemma: Joint-WAMs preserve future-aware representations during inference but incur prohibitive computation costs, while efficient alternatives remove future modeling at inference time and may lose the robustness benefits of temporal reasoning. In this work, we revisit the role of future representations in WAMs and show that inference-time future conditioning is critical for generalization under distribution shifts. This observation motivates Faster-WAM, an efficient future-conditioning WAM that preserves future representations while avoiding expensive video-action interaction. Faster-WAM introduces a sparse future-conditioning framework that computes future representations once and selectively reuses them throughout action denoising. Specifically, we propose SparseMoT to replace ubiquitous layer-wise fusion with selective video-action interaction at a compact subset of network stages, and Interval KV-Fusion to aggregate multi-depth future representations without increasing attention complexity. Experiments demonstrate that Faster-WAM achieves a substantially better performance-efficiency trade-off than existing WAMs. On the out-of-distribution LIBERO-Plus benchmark, Faster-WAM improves success rate from 49.14% to 73.57% compared with Fast-WAM, while running 2.21$\times$ faster than Joint-WAM. It further achieves state-of-the-art performance on LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0, while demonstrating strong robustness in real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [29] [muSync-GS: Physics-Synchronized Driving Video Synthesis for Weather and Geometric Road Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04412)
*Yang Chen,Yicheng Zhu,Tao Li,Zilin Bian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: muSync-GS是一个物理同步的驾驶视频合成框架，可将恶劣天气和道路高程编辑与轮胎-路面摩擦、车辆动力学和自车相机运动耦合起来，在12个CarSim测试案例中实现了高精度的速度、俯仰角、滑移率和车轮法向载荷预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量驾驶数据对自动驾驶系统和生成式世界模型至关重要，但恶劣天气、低轮胎-路面摩擦制动和不平路面等稀有且安全关键场景的数据采集成本高且风险大。现有视频生成和3D高斯编辑方法仅能修改天气外观或道路几何，无法将这些编辑与轮胎-路面交互及车辆动力学耦合，导致编辑后的视频在路况条件改变时仍保留原始轨迹，物理不一致。

Method: muSync-GS提出一个物理同步框架：(1) 由降水导出的路面状况联合控制道路外观和轮胎摩擦系数；(2) 共享的道路高程剖面同时驱动可见道路几何编辑与车轴激励；(3) 校准的车辆模型预测速度、滑移率、法向载荷和俯仰角，用于构建自车相机轨迹和同步的物理标注。

Result: 在12个涵盖降水量、制动输入和道路剖面参数的CarSim测试案例中，模型实现了平均逐案例RMSE：速度0.0273 m/s、俯仰角0.0590度、滑移率0.0101、每轮法向载荷26.61 N。重建场景实验也验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: muSync-GS能够精确复现保留控制下的车辆响应，并将其与可控场景编辑和自车相机运动同步，从而生成物理一致的恶劣天气和道路高程危险场景下的驾驶视频。

Abstract: High-quality driving data are essential for autonomous-driving systems and generative world models. However, rare and safety-critical scenarios involving adverse weather, braking under low tire--road friction, and uneven road geometry are costly and risky to collect at scale. Existing video-generation and 3D Gaussian editing methods can modify weather appearance or road geometry, but typically do not couple these edits with tire--road interaction and vehicle dynamics. As a result, an edited video may retain its original trajectory even when the modified road condition should alter braking, wheel slip, load transfer, and ego-camera motion. We present muSync-GS, a physics-synchronized framework for driving video synthesis under adverse-weather and road-elevation hazards. A precipitation-derived road-surface condition jointly controls road appearance and tire friction, while a shared road-elevation profile drives both visible road-geometry editing and axle excitation. A calibrated vehicle model predicts speed, slip ratio, normal loads, and pitch for constructing the ego-camera trajectory and synchronized physical annotations. On 12 held-out CarSim cases spanning precipitation levels, brake inputs, and road-profile parameters, the model achieves mean case-wise RMSEs of 0.0273 m/s for speed, 0.0590 degrees for pitch, 0.0101 for slip ratio, and 26.61 N for per-wheel normal load. Together with the reconstructed-scene experiments, these results show that muSync-GS accurately reproduces vehicle responses under held-out controls while synchronizing them with controllable scene edits and ego-camera motion.

</details>


### [30] [Foreseeing the Invisible: Amodal Reconstruction of Leaf Fossil Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04423)
*Liuxiang Yue,Ailin Zhang,Ziyue Zhao,Yikun Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AmodalDINO，一个多头部密集预测模型，用于从单张RGB图像联合预测可见叶片、完整叶片（amodal）、主脉和细脉四个掩膜。无需可见掩膜输入，通过全量微调DINOv3 ViT-L/16并添加叶脉辅助头部，在合成化石图像上训练后达到95.0% Dice/90.5% IoU，可迁移到真实化石，且量化后可在浏览器中离线运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化石叶片极少完整保存，沉积岩会掩埋、断裂和侵蚀叶面，但古植物学研究高度依赖叶片完整形状和轮廓。因此需要将缺失组织的恢复视为amodal重建任务，以从残缺化石中重建完整叶片形态。

Method: 提出AmodalDINO多头部模型，从单一RGB图像同时预测四个掩膜（可见叶片、amodal完整叶片、amodal主脉、细脉）。两个关键改进：1）全量微调DINOv3 ViT-L/16并采用小学习率，而非冻结；2）在叶片头部旁附加叶脉辅助头部。训练仅使用合成化石图像。

Result: 合成数据训练后在验证集上达到95.0% Dice/90.5% IoU，并良好迁移到真实化石标本。缩减为两个头部后在KINS上达到85.05全mIoU/66.65遮挡mIoU，在COCOA-cls上达到80.90/38.15。4-bit量化后在浏览器离线运行，IoU达0.910。还加入了基于标尺的表面面积估算和活叶生成可视化。

Conclusion: AmodalDINO无需上游实例分割器即可联合预测可见与amodal区域，通过结构形状先验学习（利用DINOv3全量微调和叶脉辅助头部）有效解决化石叶片不完整重建问题，且模型轻量化、可离线部署，兼具实用性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Fossil leaves are rarely preserved whole -- sedimentary rock hides, breaks, and erodes the lamina, yet paleobotany depends on the complete shape and outline of the leaf. We cast the recovery of the missing tissue as amodal reconstruction and present AmodalDINO, a multi-head dense-prediction model that predicts four masks from a single RGB image: visible leaf, amodal complete leaf, amodal main vein, and fine veins. Unlike essentially all prior amodal work, AmodalDINO is given no visible mask. It predicts the visible and amodal regions jointly, so it needs no upstream instance segmenter at runtime. Two simple but effective changes adapt the model to the amodal segmentation task: fully fine-tune a DINOv3 ViT-L/16 at a small learning rate instead of freezing it, and attach auxiliary venation heads alongside the leaf heads. These two changes enable the model to learn the structural shape prior of leaves. Trained only on synthetic leaf fossil images, AmodalDINO reaches 95.0% Dice / 90.5% IoU on the validation set and transfers well to real fossil specimens. Stripped to two heads, the same recipe can run on two benchmark datasets, reaching 85.05 full mIoU / 66.65 occluded mIoU on KINS and 80.90 / 38.15 on COCOA-cls. The model is also practical: by quantizing to 4-bit weights, it runs entirely offline in a browser, matching the original model with an IoU of 0.910. We also add ruler-based calibration to estimate surface area, and a generative visualization of living leaves on local devices.

</details>


### [31] [Thinking with Anchors: Grounded and Efficient Document Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04424)
*Sichen Zhu,Yuchen Zhu,Wenzhuo Xu,Jason Kuen,Wanrong Zhu,Jing Shi,Xuan Shen,Quanyi Wang,Yiwei Wang,Yujun Cai,Bing Shuai,Qin Zhang,Yongxin Chen,Shilong Liu,Molei Tao,Jiuxiang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADOPD 2026 是 ADOPD 数据集的推理导向扩展，将页面分解任务转化为空间扎根的文档理解，通过引入人工清洗的描述、语义标签和基于文档区域的思维链（CoT）轨迹，将文本块、视觉实体、语义标签、边界框和多边形掩码统一为共享的视觉锚点词汇表，支持区域级语义标注、统一视觉-语言定位和密集计数三大能力，并提出了 Thinking-with-Anchors 流程以推动文档理解超越单纯定位迈向锚点扎根的文档智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档理解基准大多聚焦于定位页面元素，而真实世界的文档智能要求模型对区域语义、空间关系和视觉结构进行联合推理。作者旨在弥补这一缺口，将页面分解任务转化为推理导向的文档理解基准。

Method: ADOPD 2026 继承并扩展了 ADOPD 2024 数据集：为页面锚点添加人工清洗的标题、语义标签和扎根于文档区域的思维链（CoT）轨迹；将文本框、视觉实体、语义标签、边界框和多边形掩码统一为共享的视觉锚点词汇表；在此基础上构建三个关联能力（区域级语义标注、统一视觉-语言定位、密集计数），并提出 Thinking-with-Anchors 流程进行文档语义理解。

Result: 实验表明，现有最先进模型在基于 ADOPD 2026 派生的 DocCount 基准上的密集计数任务上仍表现不佳，揭示了标准布局基准所掩盖的长尾语义失败，凸显了 Thinking-with-Anchors 流程的必要性。

Conclusion: ADOPD 2026 通过将页面分解与可验证的视觉锚点推理相连接，提供了超越定位、面向锚点扎根文档智能的任务框架，推动文档理解向更高层次的语义推理发展。

Abstract: Existing document understanding benchmarks have largely focused on locating page elements, yet real-world document intelligence requires models to reason jointly about region semantics, spatial relations, and visual structure. We present ADOPD 2026, a reasoning-oriented extension of ADOPD that turns page decomposition into spatially grounded document understanding. ADOPD 2026 enriches page anchors inherited from ADOPD 2024 dataset with human-cleaned captions, semantic tags, and generated chain-of-thought (CoT) traces grounded to document regions. Instead of treating boxes, masks, and tags as independent supervision signals, we cast text blocks, visual entities, semantic labels, bounding boxes, and polygon masks as a shared vocabulary of visual anchors. This representation supports three connected capabilities. First, region-level semantic tagging asks models to identify document element types from both page context and local appearance, revealing long-tail semantic failures that standard layout benchmarks often hide. Second, unified vision-language grounding generates text regions and visual entities together with coordinates or polygonal outlines, transforming detection and segmentation outputs into structured anchors that can be reused by downstream reasoning systems. Third, current state-of-the-art models still struggle with dense counting tasks evaluated on DocCount, a benchmark derived from ADOPD 2026, highlighting the need for the Thinking-with-Anchors pipeline in document semantic understanding. By connecting page decomposition to verifiable visual-anchor reasoning, ADOPD 2026 provides a task framework that moves document understanding beyond localization toward anchor-grounded document intelligence.

</details>


### [32] [Predict, Then Retrieve: Cross-Instance Future-State Retrieval from Video Prefixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04426)
*Quynh Vo,Thong Nguyen,Vinh-Hien Do,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Anh-Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性状态检索（PSR）任务，要求模型观察视频前缀和关于物体未来状态的时间问题，从其他视频或图像中检索描绘该状态的实例。作者构建了包含四个数据集的基准，并提出轻量级检索器LFTR（使用冻结编码器预测问题与时间水平条件化的未来潜变量），在语义和视觉双空间中匹配。天花板分解表明真正的瓶颈在于预测而非感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务如动作预测仅预测标签、时刻检索仅定位已观察事件、视频生成仅合成像素，均无法处理"给定当前上下文，从其他实例中检索未来状态"这一跨实例组合需求。作者希望弥合预测与检索之间的鸿沟，并识别该任务的真正瓶颈所在。

Method: 1）定义PSR任务：给定视频前缀+关于物体未来状态的时间问题，从外部视频/图像库中检索匹配该状态的实例，支持多个时间水平（temporal horizons）。2）构建基准：基于四个数据集，采用分级人工验证的ground truth、难度分级和oracle上限。3）提出LFTR：轻量级检索器，使用冻结编码器，预测问题与时间水平条件化的未来潜变量，并在互补的语义空间和视觉空间中进行匹配。4）通过天花板分解评估预测器与oracle之间的差距。

Result: 天花板分解揭示明确瓶颈：一旦指定未来状态，真实未来状态高度可检索（oracle接近）但所有评估的预测器（包括可访问前缀帧的大型多模态语言模型）均远低于oracle。LFTR以显著更低的推理成本缩小了这一差距，消融实验表明其增益来自跨空间融合和困难负样本训练，而非潜变量展开（latent rollout）。

Conclusion: PSR任务的中心可学习挑战在于预测（forecasting）而非感知（perception）。作者发布了基准、代码和评估脚本以供社区使用。

Abstract: We introduce Predictive State Retrieval (PSR), a task in which a model observes a short video prefix and a temporal question about an object's future state, then retrieves instances from other videos or images that depict that state. Unlike action anticipation, which predicts a label, moment retrieval, which localizes an observed event within a video, or video generation, which synthesizes pixels, PSR combines anticipation with cross-instance retrieval across multiple temporal horizons. We construct a benchmark from four datasets with graded, human-validated ground truth, difficulty tiers, and an oracle ceiling. We also propose LFTR, a lightweight retriever with frozen encoders that predicts a question- and horizon-conditioned future latent and matches it in complementary semantic and visual spaces. A ceiling decomposition reveals a clear bottleneck: the true future state is highly retrievable once specified, whereas every predictor we evaluate, including a large multimodal language model with access to the prefix frames, remains far below the oracle. Thus, forecasting rather than perception is the central learnable challenge. LFTR narrows this gap at substantially lower inference cost, and ablations attribute its gains to cross-space fusion and hard-negative training rather than latent rollout. We release the benchmark, code, and evaluation scripts.

</details>


### [33] [UBLLIE: Unified Backlight and Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04429)
*Yasmin Yasin,Muhammad Usman,Ibrahim Radwan,Saeed Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的、无监督的背光与低光图像增强框架，基于CLIP引导的提示学习（prompt learning）进行语义监督，结合对称残差U-Net与空洞空间金字塔池化（ASPP）模块，通过CLIP语义相似度损失和迭代提示优化机制训练，在多个公开数据集上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 背光图像和低光图像常遭受严重的曝光失衡或全局欠曝光问题，影响视觉感知和下游任务。现有方法大多依赖成对的真实标注（ground-truth）数据进行监督训练，但在实际场景中难以获取配对数据，且两类退化（曝光不均与全局欠曝光）通常被分开处理，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 1) 基于CLIP引导的提示学习：使用学习的正/负文本提示对增强过程进行语义监督；2) 对称残差U-Net骨干网络，并引入ASPP模块以捕获多尺度上下文信息，实现空间异质光照下的自适应校正；3) 训练时采用CLIP语义相似度损失，并通过迭代提示优化机制不断精炼提示；4) 全程无监督，无需配对真值。

Result: 在BAID、Backlit300、LOL和VE-LOL-L等多个配对与非配对数据集上，该框架在保真度、感知质量和泛化性方面均一致优于现有有监督和无监督的SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为真实世界的多样化光照增强提供了稳健且可扩展的解决方案，同时强调了背光增强领域需要更强的基准测试协议。
