We use this resource to conduct a first exploratory evaluation of language models on their ability to understand and define queer slang under varying prompting conditions. Slang-Q is intended as a basis for studying how current models handle sensitive, community-specific language and whether they can provide accurate and reliable information about such forms of identity and linguistic expression.

</details>


### [167] [Preverbal Uninflected and Underived Roots in Mapudungun. Wuno and Its Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04869)
*Andres Chandia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过跨越四个世纪的历时语料分析，重新审视了马普切语中动词前无屈折、无派生词根的语法地位，提出了"韵律-正字法假说"来解释看似可变的黏着现象，最终支持Zúñiga的激进连接模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究对马普切语中wuno等动词前词根的分类存在分歧（助动词、情态前缀、动词前小词/复合动词干等），现有框架均无法充分解释这些成分的语法地位，需要基于历时证据重新评估。

Method: 采用语料库历时分析方法，覆盖1606年至今四个世纪的文本；对wuno、kim、shinge、pepi、kalli等成分进行词序（V1/V2）分布统计与分类；结合正字法演变与韵律停顿的化石化作解释。

Result: 发现三类不同分布特征：稳定的V1复合成分（kim、shinge）、受正字法变迁影响的波动V1成分（pepi、wuno）、以及真正的小词（kalli）。同时发现kim和kupa存在V2证例，确认其为完整词汇动词。

Conclusion: 早期传教士将韵律停顿转写为空格，后經使用者重新分析为句法边界，造成"可变黏着"的错觉；Zúñiga的激进连接模型因此是正确的语法分析框架，这对无前接触书面传统语言的描写研究具有方法论启示。

Abstract: This study examines the grammatical status of preverbal uninflected and underived roots in Mapudungun, with particular focus on wuno 'return/re-'. Through a critical review of scholarly classifications--auxiliaries (Smeets, 2008), modal prefixes (Longkon, 2011), and preverbal particles/complex verb stems (Zúñiga, 2006)--we demonstrate the limitations of existing frameworks. A diachronic corpus analysis spanning four centuries (1606-present) reveals that these elements exhibit three distinct profiles: stable V1 compounds (kim, shinge), volatile V1 rates reflecting orthographic shift (pepi, wuno), and a true particle (kalli). The discovery of V2 attestations for kim and kupa confirms their status as full lexical verbs. We propose a prosodic-orthographic hypothesis: apparent "variable binding" results from the fossilization of prosodic pauses transcribed by early missionaries as spaces, a convention later reanalysed by speakers as syntactic boundaries. The evidence supports Zúñiga's radical concatenation as the correct grammatical model, with implications for the study of languages with no pre-contact written tradition.

</details>


### [168] [A-SR: Self-Evolving Agentic LLMs for Symbolic Regression via Hierarchical Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04872)
*Wenxiao Zhao,Dong Liu,Kaiyi Xu,Feng Liu,Zhen Zhao,Fei Ben,Shu Wang,Wenhao Li,Yingnian Wu,Fenghua Ling,Haobo Li,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出A-SR，一个自进化的agentic框架，用于符号回归（从数据中发现闭式方程）。与传统LLM引导方法不同，A-SR将控制单元从"表达式编辑"转变为"角色条件化的证据视图"，通过协调协议路由、在线评估器-奖励角色策略和状态路由的过程记忆来协调公式发现。框架在两个时间尺度上自进化：运行内自适应搜索过程（不更新LLM参数），跨运行将记录轨迹蒸馏到开源LLM中作为角色条件化的提议先验。在LLM-SRBench的四个LSR-Synth科学域上，A-SR将Acc@0.01从25.79%提升到48.30%（Llama3.1-8B），A-SR-LoRA将Qwen3-4B结果从24.58%提升到38.29%，并在四个真实世界科学发现任务的8个指标中7个取得最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导的符号回归方法存在关键缺陷：它们依赖统一的提议循环，将所有异构搜索失败压缩为一个标量分数和单一提示，无法区分不同类型失败（如良构性错误、不可靠解、低产出路径）所蕴含的不同信息，从而限制了搜索效率和解的质量。

Method: A-SR是一个自进化的agentic框架，核心包含三个组件：(1) 协调协议之间的路由，决定搜索策略；(2) 在线评估器-奖励角色策略，评估器反馈同时刻画解的可靠性和产出性，更新角色级效用；(3) 状态路由的过程记忆，将精英motif、失败轨迹和有效性诊断路由到不同agent。框架在两个时间尺度自进化：运行内通过角色策略调整搜索过程而不更新LLM权重；跨运行通过轨迹蒸馏将经验编码为开源LLM的角色条件化提议先验。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench的四个LSR-Synth科学域上，A-SR在Llama3.1-8B上实现Acc@0.01从25.79%提升到48.30%；A-SR-LoRA使Qwen3-4B从24.58%提升到38.29%。在四个真实世界科学发现任务中，A-SR在8个报告指标的7个上取得最佳归一化均方误差（包括分布内和分布外）。

Conclusion: A-SR通过将搜索控制从表达式级细粒度编辑提升到角色条件化的证据视图级别，有效解决了统一提议循环中异构失败信息被压缩的问题。其双时间尺度自进化机制（运行内策略自适应与跨运行知识蒸馏）显著提升了符号回归的性能和泛化能力，验证了将agentic机制与LLM结合用于科学发现的潜力。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to discover closed-form equations from data, but existing LLM-guided methods often rely on a unified proposal loop that compresses heterogeneous search failures into a scalar score and a single prompt. We propose A-SR, a self-evolving agentic framework that shifts the control unit from expression edits to role-conditioned evidence views. A-SR coordinates formula discovery through routing among coordination protocols, an online evaluator-reward role policy, and state-routed process memory. During search, evaluator feedback characterizes reliability and productivity, updates role-level utilities, and routes elite motifs, failure traces, and validity diagnostics to different agents. The framework self-evolves at two timescales: within a run, it adapts the search process without updating LLM parameters; across runs, recorded trajectories can be distilled into open-source LLMs as role-conditioned proposal priors. Averaged over the four LSR-Synth scientific domains in LLM-SRBench, A-SR improves Acc@0.01 over baselines from 25.79% to 48.30% with Llama3.1-8B, while A-SR-LoRA improves the corresponding Qwen3-4B result from 24.58% to 38.29%. On four real-world scientific discovery tasks, A-SR obtains the best in-distribution or out-of-distribution normalized mean squared error on 7 of 8 reported metrics.

</details>


### [169] [Evaluation Pitfalls and Sparsity Limitations in LLM-based Confidence Estimates for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04899)
*Elena Merdjanovska,Omar Zaidan,Andreas Rücklé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在分类任务中的置信度verbalization存在严重稀疏性问题（如Qwen3-32B在SST-2上仅输出8个唯一置信度值），这会扭曲AUARC评估结果，并提出采用stepwise插值标准化评估，以及用token概率加权verbalized数字（verbalization logprobs）来缓解稀疏性，实现免费的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs用于分类任务时需要对预测进行置信度估计，但现有verbalization（语言化）方法产生极度稀疏的置信度输出，这既限制了实际用途，又会严重扭曲评估结果——不同插值方式会颠倒模型排名。因此需要统一评估标准并解决稀疏性问题。

Method: 1) 系统测量verbalization输出的稀疏性（在4个数据集和2个LLM上）；2) 对比线性插值与stepwise插值在AUARC评估中的影响；3) 提出verbalization logprobs方法——用token概率加权verbalized数字，在不增加推理成本的前提下获得更连续的置信度。

Result: Qwen3-32B在SST-2上只有8个唯一置信度值，其中超过一半恰好是95%。在stepwise插值下consistency sampling排名从最好跌至最差。Verbalization logprobs达到最佳AUARC，比vanilla verbalization高出2.3个百分点，且无额外推理成本。

Conclusion: 建议标准化stepwise插值作为AUARC评估基准以获得公平比较；verbalization logprobs有效缓解了置信度稀疏性，同时提升AUARC性能且不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Confidence estimation is essential when LLMs are used for classification, indicating when predictions can be trusted. However, common approaches such as verbalization produce extremely sparse outputs. For instance, Qwen3-32B verbalizes only eight unique confidence values on SST-2, with over half being exactly 95%, a pattern we observe consistently across four datasets and two LLMs. Besides limiting practical utility, we show that this sparsity critically affects evaluation: the choice of interpolation in area under the accuracy-rejection curve (AUARC) dramatically alters rankings, with consistency sampling dropping from best to worst under stepwise versus linear interpolation. We advocate for standardizing stepwise interpolation for a fairer comparison. Under such a fair evaluation, we find that weighting verbalized digits by token probabilities, a method we term verbalization logprobs, addresses sparsity and achieves the best AUARC (+2.3 points over vanilla verbalization) without incurring additional inference cost.

</details>


### [170] [Strengthening Target-Language Features: SAE-Based Steering for Multilingual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04904)
*Hongsheng Wang,Phlipp Koehn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种推理时多语言引导方法，利用预训练的稀疏自编码器识别并增强目标语言相关特征，无需参数更新和大量多语言训练数据，即可显著提升多语言大模型的跨语言性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型在不同语言上的性能差异显著，而现有的适应方法通常需要参数更新和大量多语言训练数据，成本高昂且不够灵活。

Method: 利用预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE），通过多语言平行句对比较SAE激活，选出与各目标语言相关的少量层特定特征，将其解码为引导信号注入模型的隐藏状态中，实现推理时的多语言引导，无需额外训练。

Result: 在Gemma-3-12B-it上的实验表明：XCOPA平均准确率提升10.9个百分点，XNLI提升5.3个百分点，MGSM提升1.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于SAE的推理时特征引导方法能够在不进行参数更新和大量数据训练的情况下有效增强模型的目标语言能力，为多语言大模型的跨语言性能提升提供了一种轻量级、可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models exhibit substantial performance differences across languages, while existing adaptation methods often require parameter updates and considerable multilingual training data. We propose an inference-time multilingual steering method that uses pretrained sparse autoencoders to identify and strengthen target-language-related features. Using multilingual parallel sentences, we compare SAE activations across languages and select a small number of layer-specific features associated with each target language. These features are decoded into steering signals and injected into the model's hidden states without additional training. Experiments with Gemma-3-12B-it show average accuracy improvements of 10.9 percentage points on XCOPA, 5.3 points on XNLI, and 1.9 points on MGSM.

</details>


### [171] [Does Out-of-Sight Equal Out-of-Mind in CoT Monitorability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04928)
*Pedro Ferreira,Wilker Aziz,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在潜在思维链（Latent CoT）推理模式下，大语言模型的可监控性（monitorability）是否得到保留。通过基于提示（hint）的干预实验，发现可监控性更多取决于任务属性（如正确答案是否约束推理过程）和模型内部访问级别，而非推理模式本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式思维链（CoT）通过可读的推理轨迹为LLM的决策提供监控窗口，但潜在CoT用少量连续状态替代显式token，降低了推理成本却移除了可读的推理轨迹，导致监控困难。虽然可以通过探测激活或将其语言化来替代监控，但替代方式能保留多少可监控性是未知的，需要系统研究。

Method: 采用基于提示（hint）的干预实验设置，以提示依赖（hint-reliance）作为监控目标，在数学推理和问答任务上，对比显式CoT、弱监督和强监督潜在CoT等多种推理模式下的监控器（monitors）表现。该设置模拟模型利用偏差性输入线索（如意外泄露的答案或用户声明的信念）而不加承认的行为。

Result: 研究发现，在该实验设置下，可监控性主要取决于任务属性和对模型内部结构的访问级别，而非推理模式本身。具体而言，正确答案是否约束支撑推理这一任务特性以及是否能够访问模型内部状态，比推理模式（显式CoT或潜在CoT）对监控效果的影响更大。

Conclusion: 潜在CoT并不必然损害可监控性；监控效果的好坏更多由任务约束条件和监控者对模型内部的访问权限决定。这一发现为在实际应用中设计监控策略提供了指导——不必因使用潜在CoT而过度担忧监控损失，而应关注任务特性和内部访问能力。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning offers a window into the decision-making of large language models (LLMs), which can be monitored for target behaviors by reading the reasoning trace, motivating work on CoT monitorability. Latent CoT approaches, however, replace the explicit tokens with a small number of continuous states, lowering inference costs but removing the readable trace this monitoring relies on. Monitoring then requires alternative access to the model, such as probing its activations or verbalizing the latent states back into text, but how much monitorability these alternatives preserve is unclear. We study this question with a hint-based intervention setup, a proxy for behaviors where models exploit biasing input cues, e.g., an inadvertently leaked answer or a belief stated by the user, without acknowledging them. Taking hint-reliance as the monitorability target, we compare monitors across reasoning modes, from explicit CoT to weakly- and strongly-supervised latent CoT, on math reasoning and question answering. We find that, in this setup, monitorability depends more on properties of the task (such as whether the correct answer constrains the supporting reasoning) and the level of access to model internals than on the reasoning mode.

</details>


### [172] [State2State: Environment-Derived Mid-Training for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04934)
*Xuanyu Lei,Yiqi Zhu,Chenliang Li,Kaiming Liu,Peng Li,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye,Ya-Qin Zhang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出State2State，一种环境派生中间训练方法，通过将探索到的环境状态转化为训练目标，使LLM智能体仅通过与环境的交互获得操作能力，无需外部任务指定或监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体训练方法（如SFT和在线RL）依赖外部专家轨迹或人工设计的任务与验证器，受到任务指定和监督信号的瓶颈限制，难以扩展训练数据的规模与多样性。作者希望探索一种无需外部任务指定即可训练智能体的环境学习范式。

Method: State2State从环境探索中派生训练目标：将探索的环境状态转换为目标状态，训练智能体从当前状态导航到指定目标状态；通过基于规则的状态匹配来验证任务是否成功完成，从而无需专家监督或人工任务设计即可提供可扩展且可验证的训练目标。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上的实验表明，State2State作为独立的环境学习阶段在大多数设置下能提升智能体性能；作为下游强化学习的初始化，它能进一步提升最终性能和学习效率，并展现出跨环境泛化的证据。

Conclusion: State2State证明了一种无需外部任务指定的环境学习范式是可行的，通过将环境状态本身转化为可验证的训练目标，能够有效提升LLM智能体的能力，并可作为下游RL的良好初始化基础。

Abstract: Training LLM agents commonly relies on supervised fine-tuning from expert trajectories or online reinforcement learning over human-specified tasks with handcrafted verifiers. Though effective, both remain bottlenecked by externally specified tasks and supervision signals, limiting the scalability and diversity of agent training. We study an environment learning paradigm in which agents acquire interaction and manipulation capabilities solely through environment interaction, without externally specified tasks. We propose State2State, an environment-derived mid-training method that converts explored environment states into training objectives, challenging agents to reach a specified target state. By deriving tasks from environment exploration and verifying success through rule-based state matching, State2State provides scalable and verifiable training objectives without expert supervision or manual task design. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that State2State improves agent performance as a standalone environment-learning stage in most settings. As initialization for downstream RL, it further improves final performance and learning efficiency, with promising evidence of cross-environment generalization.

</details>


### [173] [Reading Between the Frames: Interpreting Implicit and Non-literal Meaning in Social Media Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04939)
*Yang Wang,Yanan Ma,Yiqi Liu,Zi Yan Chang,Chi-Li Chen,Chia-Yi Hsiao,Tyler Loakman,Aline Villavicencio,Chenghao Xiao,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DrivelHub+基准，一个包含1,000个社交媒体视频的数据集，用于评估视频-语言模型能否推断视频中隐含的、非线性的、修辞层叠的语用意义——即表面看似无意义但承载刻意交际意图的内容。该基准从解释（生成自然语言语用理解）和表征（推理即检索，视频-文本双向检索）两个维度评估当前模型，旨在衡量多模态感知与语用理解之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体视频的深层含义往往超越其可见动作、字幕或语音，仅通过多模态线索与文化语境的交互才能实现幽默、反讽或讽刺等效果。现有视频理解任务聚焦于识别和描述，缺乏对隐含语用推理能力的系统评测，因此需要一个针对语境化多模态推理的基准来诊断当前模型的局限。

Method: 构建包含1,000个社交媒体视频的基准数据集，每个视频配有以人为撰写的隐含叙事解释标注。评估采用两个视角：(1) 解释任务——模型需用自然语言阐述视频的语用理解；(2) 表征任务——将推理建模为检索（reasoning-as-retrieval），测试视频-文本和文本-视频双向检索中模型表征能否将视频与其对应的隐含叙事对齐。

Result: 基准提供了诊断性设定，用于衡量多模态感知与语用理解之间的差距，揭示当前视频-语言模型在从"描述所呈现内容"到"推断所表达含义"这一能力上的不足。

Conclusion: DrivelHub+为视频-语言模型在隐含语用推理能力上的评估提供了首个系统性基准，表明当前模型在超越表面内容理解、捕捉文化语境和多模态修辞层面含义方面仍存在显著差距。

Abstract: Social media videos often communicate meanings that go beyond their visible actions, captions, or speech. A mundane clip may become humorous, ironic, or satire only through the interaction of multimodal cues and cultural context, making such content a difficult test case for video-language models. In this paper, we introduce \textit{DrivelHub+}, a benchmark for evaluating whether models can infer the implicit, non-linear, and rhetorically layered meanings of social media videos that appear nonsensical on the surface but convey deliberate pragmatic meanings. DrivelHub+ consists of 1,000 videos collected from social media, each annotated with a human-written implicit narrative explanation. Unlike conventional video understanding tasks focused on recognition or description, we present a benchmark that targets contextual multimodal reasoning. We evaluate current video-language models from two perspectives: explanation, where models must explain the pragmatic comprehension of a video in natural language; and representation, where we adapt reasoning-as-retrieval to test whether model representations align videos with their corresponding implicit narratives in both video-to-text and text-to-video retrieval. Our benchmark provides a diagnostic setting for measuring the gap between multimodal perception and pragmatic comprehension, asking whether current models can move beyond describing what is shown to inferring what is meant.

</details>


### [174] [Protoreasoning in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04980)
*Eduardo Valle,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"原型推理"（protoreasoning），一种简单形式的链式思维（Chain of Thought），可让仅约100万参数的微型Transformer实现逐步推理，从而在低成本模型上深入研究推理的泛化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型表现出令人印象深刻的逐步推理能力，但我们尚不了解其泛化本质——模型究竟是学习了通用算法还是"启发式集合"。这类问题难以在计算密集的前沿模型和不透明数据上进行研究，因此需要更小、更可控的模型平台。

Method: 在远低于自然语言能力阈值的模型规模上，定义了基于Dyck语言（正确嵌套括号的句子）的推理友好任务。让微型Transformer（约1M参数）生成链式思维形式的中介推理轨迹（称之为protoreasoning），并对比有无该轨迹的泛化表现，同时通过消融实验分离轨迹内容与额外token的影响。

Result: protoreasoning轨迹显著缩小了分布外（out-of-distribution）泛化差距；消融实验证实，推理轨迹的内容本身（而非仅仅是额外token数量）才是带来性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: 微型Transformer能够利用简单的链式思维形式进行逐步推理，且在极小规模下即可研究推理泛化机制；protoreasoning带来的提升源于推理内容而非数据量扩充，为理解LLM推理泛化的本质提供了可操控的实验平台。

Abstract: We show that tiny transformers can profitably employ a simple form of Chain of Thought, which we call protoreasoning, allowing us to study step-by-step reasoning on ~1M-parameter models and opening up opportunities for much more detailed experimentation and analysis than is feasible for larger models. Current Large Language Models exhibit impressive step-by-step reasoning, but we have yet to understand its generality, i.e., when and how LLMs learn genuinely general algorithms rather than "bags of heuristics." Such questions are hard to settle on compute-intensive frontier models trained on opaque data. To work at model scales far below the threshold for natural-language competence, we define reasoning-friendly tasks on Dyck languages (sentences of correctly nested brackets). We find that protoreasoning traces substantially close the out-of-distribution generalization gap, and ablations confirm that the trace's content, not merely its extra tokens, drives the gain.

</details>


### [175] [DelusionEval: Measuring Delusion-Linked Behaviors in AI Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05004)
*Jared Moore,Andrea Mock,Yifan Mai,Jacy Reese Anthis,Ryan Louie,William Agnew,Ashish Mehta,Kevin Klyman,Percy Liang,Nick Haber,Eric Lin,Desmond C. Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DelusionEval评估协议，用于测试大语言模型在真实用户对话中促进用户妄想行为的倾向。基于18名参与者、589条对话历史（12,591条消息）的测试发现，模型诱发妄想行为的倾向与模型规模、发布日期或推理链无关，但延长上下文会显著增加有害行为发生率（如自杀意念下未劝阻自残率从30.0%升至41.1%），所有主流模型家族均表现出可观的妄想关联行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康专业人士担心LLM可能通过"妄想螺旋"对用户造成心理伤害，且随着LLM聊天机器人的广泛使用，迫切需要基于真实心理伤害案例构建评估框架，以量化模型在促进用户妄想方面的风险。

Method: 开发DelusionEval评估协议，用589条来自18名经历过妄想和心理伤害的真实用户的对话历史（12,591条消息）提示各模型，测试其表现出与促进用户妄想相关行为的倾向；系统性地改变上下文长度（额外前置350条消息），并跨模型家族（GPT、Claude等）及模型属性（规模、发布日期、推理能力）进行比较分析。

Result: 模型展示妄想关联行为的倾向与模型大小、发布日期或测试时推理无关；延长上下文显著增加妄想关联行为率；例如未劝阻自残行为率从30.0%升至41.1%。所有模型家族均表现出可观的妄想关联行为率，且同一家族内更晚、更大或推理能力更强的模型并非在所有行为类别上一致更优。

Conclusion: 研究结果对LLM潜在心理影响提出严重关切，强调需要基于真实人机交互的更严谨研究，并呼吁业界关注上下文长度对LLM安全评估的重要性。

Abstract: Mental health professionals have raised concerns about risks of psychological harm from interaction with large language models (LLMs), including "delusional spirals" in which concerning human and LLM behaviors reinforce each other over time. With growing public use of LLM-powered chatbots, there is an urgent need to build evaluations grounded in real-world episodes of psychological harm experienced by users. We developed DelusionEval, an evaluation protocol that tests a model's tendencies to exhibit behaviors linked to promoting user delusions. We prompt each model with 589 unique conversation histories from 18 participants, comprising 12,591 messages from users who experienced delusions and psychological harm. We find that the tendency of an evaluated LLM to exhibit delusion-linked behavior does not reliably correlate with model size, release date, or the presence of test-time reasoning. However, extending the context of prior messages substantially increases rates of delusion-linked behaviors, providing evidence for the importance of context in LLM safety evaluation. For example, the rate of failing to discourage self-harm when the user expresses suicidal ideation increases from 30.0% to 41.1% when an additional 350 messages are prepended to the conversation history. All model families (e.g., GPT, Claude) exhibit substantial rates of delusion-linked behaviors. Within families, later, larger, or higher-reasoning models are not uniformly better across all behavior categories. Our results raise concerns regarding the potential psychological impact of LLMs and the need for more rigorous studies of real-world human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [176] [OneDayAgent: Towards a Long-Horizon Harness for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05013)
*Jingsheng Zheng,Xinyuan Fang,Jintian Zhang,Zhengke Gui,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OneDayAgent是一个面向开放式日常请求的长时程智能体执行框架（harness），它能将任务分解为有界子任务、在上下文压力下维护执行记忆，并对最终交付物进行验证和修复。在AgentIF-OneDay基准的104个任务上，使用GLM-5.2后端取得了0.821的整体分数，刷新了SOTA，且该框架无需调参即可泛化到三个模型家族的五个后端LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正越来越多地被应用于跨工作、学习和生活的开放式日常请求，这些任务具有长时间跨度、跨环境和多模态的特点，要求智能体在大量步骤中保持目标和约束，同时导航异构工具和附件。先前工作仅解决了单一失效模式（如目标漂移、状态丢失、上下文溢出），缺乏研究单一harness能否同时管理这些问题并在不同后端上保持有效性的工作。

Method: OneDayAgent将开放式请求转化为受管理的执行过程：1）将任务分解为有界子任务；2）在上下文压力下维护执行记忆；3）对最终交付物进行验证和修复。

Result: 在AgentIF-OneDay的104个任务上，配备GLM-5.2后端的OneDayAgent取得了0.821的整体分数，创下新的SOTA；同一harness可跨越三个模型家族的五个后端LLM运行，无需调参即可泛化，尽管不同模型在同一工作流下会表现出不同的执行风格。

Conclusion: OneDayAgent证明了一个统一的harness能够联合管理目标漂移、状态丢失和上下文溢出等多种失效模式，并在不同后端LLM上表现出良好的泛化能力，为长时程跨环境多模态的智能体任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to open-ended everyday requests that span work, study, and life. These tasks are long-horizon, cross-environment, and multimodal, forcing the agent to preserve goals and constraints across many steps while navigating heterogeneous tools and attachments. While prior work has addressed individual failure modes such as goals drift, states loss, and context overflow, whether a single harness can manage them jointly and remain effective across backends has received less study. We present OneDayAgent, a long-horizon harness for autonomous agents. OneDayAgent turns an open-ended request into a managed execution process that decomposes tasks into bounded subtasks, maintains execution memory under context pressure, and verifies and repairs the final deliverable. We evaluate OneDayAgent on AgentIF-OneDay across 104 tasks. With the GLM-5.2 backend, OneDayAgent sets a new state of the art with an overall score of 0.821. The same harness runs across five backend LLMs from three model families, indicating the harness generalizes across backends without tuning, even as different models induce distinct execution styles under the same workflow.

</details>


### [177] [Language Models Generalize to Human-like Word Order Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05028)
*Amanda Popadich,Shane Steinert-Threlkeld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验表明，语言模型在训练语料中完全移除含多个修饰语的名词短语后，仍能一致地偏好"scope同态"（scope-homomorphic）的修饰语顺序，即与人类学习者相同的归纳偏好，且这种偏好无法用PMI（点互信息）等关联强度指标来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言习得中的核心问题是：语言偏误是否能从通用学习机制在欠完整（underdetermined）输入中涌现出来？人工语言学习（ALL）研究表明人类学习者会可靠地泛化超出给定证据，特别是偏好scope同态的名词短语修饰语顺序。作者希望探究语言模型是否在类似条件下表现出相同的行为偏好，从而为理解语言偏误的涌现机制提供计算层面的证据。

Method: 作者构建了一个受控学习环境：在训练语料中移除所有包含多个修饰语的名词短语，消除关于修饰语排序的直接证据；随后在包含多个修饰语的句子上评估模型。实验中使用了三种规模的模型进行训练与测试，并利用点互信息（PMI）分析名词-修饰语关联强度，以探究偏好来源。

Result: 三种规模的模型在从未见过多修饰语训练样本的情况下，均一致地偏好scope同态的修饰语顺序；该偏好的强度因修饰语类型而异。PMI虽然反映了已知的修饰语排序模式，但无法解释模型的排序偏好，说明模型的泛化行为并非源于简单的词级关联统计。

Conclusion: 语言模型能够从欠完整的输入中恢复出类似人类的语言归纳偏误，这一发现为研究此类偏误背后的机制提供了受控的实验框架，同时也表明这类语言学偏误可能是通用学习机制在数据不足条件下运作的自然产物。

Abstract: A central question in language acquisition is whether linguistic biases can emerge from general learning mechanisms operating over underdetermined input. Artificial Language Learning (ALL) studies have shown that human learners reliably generalize beyond the evidence provided, including by preferring scope-homomorphic noun phrase modifier orders. In this work, we investigate whether language models exhibit the same bias under similar conditions. We create a controlled learning environment in which models are trained on a corpus where all noun phrases containing multiple modifiers have been removed, eliminating direct evidence about modifier ordering, and are then evaluated on multiple modifier sentences. Across three model sizes, we find that they consistently prefer scope-homomorphic orders despite never observing them during training. These preferences vary in strength by modifier type. To investigate the source of these preferences, we examine noun-modifier association strength using pointwise mutual information (PMI). While PMI reflects known modifier-ordering patterns, it does not explain the models' ordering preferences. These findings demonstrate that LMs can recover human-like linguistic generalizations from impoverished input and provide a controlled framework for investigating the mechanisms underlying such biases.

</details>


### [178] [Provable Limits and Certified Deferral for Verbalized Uncertainty in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05064)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究小型开源语言模型在私有/离线/成本敏感场景下的风险可控延迟（deferral）机制，探讨口头化置信度能否支持风险受限的模型-人工切换。作者在三个模型家族（0.5B到14B参数）的11个指令微调模型上，对ARC-Challenge和TruthfulQA进行了25,168次局部预测评估。理论结果界定了校准能力的极限，实证显示多数模型对在温度缩放的不可行性边界附近徘徊，Platt缩放可将ECE降至0.02，但仅有三个模型-任务对在20%风险预算下获得认证自主权，10%预算下为零。此外还发现并修复了TruthfulQA多选题形式的答案排序伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开放权重语言模型日益在私有、离线、成本敏感的环境中部署，关键问题不再仅是该模型回答什么，还包括何时应转交人类处理。作者希望探究口头化置信度能否支持风险可控的延迟决策，从而为小模型的安全使用提供可认证的保证。

Method: 理论层面推导出三条结论限制校准的效用：(1) 严格单调校准保持风险-覆盖率边界和错误检测AUROC不变；(2) 温度缩放无法校准那些置信度保持在0.5以上而准确率却跌破0.5的模型；(3) Clopper-Pearson过程将200题的校准集转化为在i.i.d.部署假设下的有限样本风险证书。实证层面在11个指令微调模型（0.5B-14B参数）上对ARC-Challenge和TruthfulQA进行评估，共25,168次局部预测，并检验Platt缩放、温度缩放等校准方法对认证自主率的影响。同时修复了TruthfulQA多选题形式中答案排序伪影问题。

Result: 22个模型-任务对中有8对在温度缩放不可行性下限的一个百分点以内。Platt缩放将ECE降至最低0.02，但在20%风险预算下仅有三个模型-任务对被授予认证自主权，10%预算下则为零。

Conclusion: 校准赋予置信度以语义，而认证延迟（certified deferral）决定小模型何时可安全使用。口头化置信度经过合适校准后可以支持风险受限的延迟，但在严格风险预算下，多数小型模型的校准置信度仍不足以获得认证自主权。

Abstract: Small open-weight language models increasingly run in private, offline, and cost-sensitive settings, where the key deployment question is not only what a model answers but when it should defer to a human. We study whether verbalized confidence can support risk-controlled deferral, evaluating eleven instruction-tuned models from three families, 0.5B to 14B parameters, on ARC-Challenge and TruthfulQA with 25,168 local predictions. Three theoretical results delimit what calibration can provide: strictly monotone calibration preserves the risk-coverage frontier and error-detection AUROC; temperature scaling cannot calibrate models whose confidence stays above one half while accuracy falls below it; and a Clopper-Pearson procedure converts a 200-question calibration set into a finite-sample risk certificate under an i.i.d. deployment assumption. Empirically, eight of 22 model-task pairs hit the temperature-scaling infeasibility floor within one percentage point of the predicted bound. Platt scaling reduces ECE to as low as 0.02, yet certified autonomy at a 20% risk budget is granted to only three model-task pairs and to none at 10%. We also identify and repair an answer-ordering artifact in the multiple-choice form of TruthfulQA. Calibration gives confidence semantics; certified deferral determines when small models are safe to use.

</details>


### [179] [German parties shifted towards intuition-based rhetoric after the far right's parliamentary breakthrough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05075)
*Peer Saleth,Segun T. Aroyehun,Fabio Carrella,Christoph M. Abels,Stephan Lewandowsky,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了2015-2025年间德国政治精英在推特（450万条推文）和议会演讲（59,170场）中的言论，发现直觉导向的语言逐渐增多，右翼行为者的"证据减直觉（EMI）"得分持续最低。2017年极右翼政党AfD进入议会后，整个议会的EMI得分显著下降，而推文上的下降则更为平缓，表明极右翼的可见性与精英话语中"真相观"的改变存在关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误信息的扩散被视为对民主协商的威胁，但政治精英关于真相的修辞承诺如何随着民粹主义行为者的崛起而改变，此前尚不清楚。该研究旨在填补这一空白，量化精英话语中证据导向与直觉导向修辞的演变趋势。

Method: 使用验证过的分布式词典表示法（validated distributed dictionary representation）来测量证据导向型和直觉导向型修辞；分析450万条推文和59,170场议会演讲，覆盖2015-2025年间的德国政治精英。

Result: 在两个场域中，直觉导向的语言都变得更加突出；右倾行为者的证据减直觉（EMI）得分持续最低。2017年AfD进入议会与整个议会EMI得分的急剧下降相吻合，而推特上的下降则更为渐进。

Conclusion: 研究发现极右翼的可见性与欧洲多党制民主国家精英话语中对待真相方式的变化之间存在关联，揭示了民粹主义进入主流政治场域可能对精英论述风格产生的系统性影响。

Abstract: The spread of misinformation is widely perceived as a threat to democratic deliberation, yet how political elites' rhetorical commitments to truth shift alongside the rise of populist actors remains poorly understood. Analysing 4.5 million tweets and 59,170 parliamentary speeches by German political elites between 2015 and 2025, we measure evidence-based and intuition-based rhetoric using a validated distributed dictionary representation. Across both arenas, intuition-based language has become more prominent, and right-leaning actors consistently exhibit the lowest Evidence Minus Intuition (EMI) scores. The parliamentary entry of the extreme-right Alternative for Germany (AfD) in 2017 coincides with sharp downward shifts in EMI across the broader chamber, while a more gradual decline is observed on Twitter. These findings document an association between far-right visibility and a changing approach to truth in elite discourse in a multiparty European democracy.

</details>


### [180] [Same Formulas, Different Semantics: Do Language Models Follow Modal Logic Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05097)
*Réemi Andrieu,Damien Sileo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了配对模态逻辑问题来评估语言模型的语义遵循能力，发现五个近期模型中四个在直接提示下低于仅依赖条件基线的表现，但启用推理模式后DeepSeek V4 Flash从4.4%提升至88.1%，表明模态语义判断强烈依赖于推理模式和模型身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评测语言模型在模态逻辑推理中是否遵循给定的语义假设（如世界可及性、对象存在域），而不是默认采用某种熟悉的逻辑系统。由于同一推理在不同模态系统下可能成立或失效，需要构造能区分模型是否真正遵循所陈述语义的测试。

Method: 构造配对模态问题，前提和猜想相同但框架（frame）或域（domain）条件不同，利用自动定理证明验证相反标签；通过平衡核心设计防止语义条件单独泄露答案；在直接提示和推理模式下分别测试五个近期模型。

Result: 直接提示下四个模型低于仅条件基线；DeepSeek V4 Flash在推理模式下从4.4%提升至88.1%；当框架条件省略时模型结果多有分歧，各自适配不同的常见逻辑系统。

Conclusion: 语言模型遵循模态语义的能力高度依赖推理模式和模型身份，直接提示下多数模型未能遵循指定的模态语义；同时发布了公式、oracle工件、反模型和响应数据以供进一步研究。

Abstract: Reasoning about necessity and possibility depends on assumptions about accessibility between worlds and about which objects exist at each one. The same inference may therefore hold under one modal system and fail under another. Evaluating language models on such problems requires testing whether their judgments follow the stated semantics rather than a familiar logic. We construct paired modal problems with identical premises and conjecture but different frame or domain conditions; automated reasoning verifies opposite labels. A balanced core prevents the semantic condition alone from revealing the answer. On this core, four of five recent models perform below the condition-only baseline under direct prompting. Yet enabling reasoning mode raises DeepSeek V4 Flash from 4.4% to 88.1% on unchanged prompts. Following stipulated modal semantics thus depends strongly on inference mode as well as model identity. When frame conditions are omitted, models often agree but fit different familiar logics best. We release the formulas, oracle artifacts, countermodels, and responses.

</details>


### [181] [Spoken Function Calling: A New Perspective on Spoken Language Understanding for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05126)
*Yuezhang Peng,Yuxin Liu,Changfeng Gao,Zhifu Gao,Xiangang Li,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Spoken Function Calling (SFC)，一种基于结构化规则定义的口语语义理解新视角，超越传统闭集SLU，通过构建SFC-Bench数据集并评估/后训练LLM和LALM，实验证明SFC在语义提取精度上优于传统SLU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SLU虽然能在有监督微调后有效处理闭集任务的用户语义提取，但由于规则定义模糊，难以利用上下文学习处理开放域任务。这种局限性阻碍了任务型对话系统在人机交互中的灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出SFC框架，以结构化规则定义优化语义理解。具体包括：1）基于传统SLU数据集整理和扩展口语函数集合；2）构建多智能体系统合成SFC-Bench数据集；3）评估LLM和LALM的SFC能力；4）通过后训练增强LALM的SFC能力。

Result: 实验表明SFC表现优于传统SLU，显著提升了LLM和LALM的语义提取精度。

Conclusion: SFC作为一种新的语义理解范式，通过结构化规则定义有效克服了传统SLU在开放域任务中的局限，为面向任务的对话系统提供了更优的语义理解方案。

Abstract: Spoken Language Understanding (SLU) is the core component of task-oriented dialogue systems and a pivotal link in achieving seamless human-agent interaction. While traditional SLU can effectively extract user semantics for closed-set tasks after in-domain supervised fine-tuning, it faces significant challenges in leveraging in-context learning for open-domain tasks due to its ambiguous rule definitions. This work proposes Spoken Function Calling (SFC), a novel semantic understanding perspective that optimizes semantic understanding with structured rule definitions, to evolve beyond traditional closed-set SLU. Specifically, we curate and extend a suite of spoken functions based on traditional SLU datasets, construct a multi-agent system to synthesize the SFC-Bench dataset, evaluate the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Audio Language Models (LALMs), and enhance the SFC capabilities of LALMs through post-training. Experiments demonstrate that SFC outperforms traditional SLU, substantially enhancing the semantic extraction accuracy for LLMs and LALMs.

</details>


### [182] [Toward Skill-Native LLMs: Skill Entropy for Benchmarking and Training Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05139)
*Yinghui He,Ling Yang,Jiarui Liu,Yongjin Yang,Lechen Zhang,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Mengdi Wang,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跨技能长程推理问题，引入Skill Entropy作为衡量技能切换难度的指标，构建Skill^2-Bench基准（覆盖558个技能、9个领域），并提出Skill-Entropy RL框架，通过在训练中同时预测答案和技能序列并引入技能熵奖励，显著提升模型在跨技能长程任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型需要在一个推理链中切换不同技能（如数学推导后规划调度），但现有基准通常只评估单一技能，缺乏衡量模型技能切换能力的原理性方法。作者希望同时从评估和训练两侧填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 提出Skill Entropy度量不同技能间切换的难度；2) 构建Skill^2-Bench基准，覆盖558个技能、9个可验证的开领域域，每个任务分配任务级技能熵分数并划分为三个难度等级；3) 提出Skill-Entropy RL框架，模型在每步不仅预测答案还预测所用技能，奖励由步骤级正确性和技能序列与金标序列对齐的技能熵奖励组成。

Result: 在8个前沿模型和4个开源模型上评估发现技能切换差距（高熵任务准确率下降）。在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上Skill^2-Bench得分从34.4%提升至68.4%，在Qwen3-1.7B上从14.6%提升至40.1%，优于竞争基线。该流水线可应用于OpenR1-Math等现成训练数据。

Conclusion: 技能熵不仅可作为基准难度标尺，还能转化为可复用的训练信号，有效提升模型在跨技能长程推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning in recent LLMs demands that the model switch between distinct skills inside a reasoning chain, such as first doing a math derivation, then using the result to plan a schedule. We call such problems cross-skill long-horizon tasks: multi-step tasks whose steps require different reasoning skills and depend on earlier outputs. Existing benchmarks often evaluate individual skills, lacking a principled way to measure how well a model switches between skills. We address this gap from both the evaluation and training sides. We introduce Skill Entropy, a measure of the difficulty of switching from one skill to another. We then propose Skill^2-Bench, a benchmark of cross-skill long-horizon tasks built over 558 skills across 9 verifiable and open-ended domains. Each task is assigned a task-level skill-entropy score and grouped into three difficulty levels. Evaluating 8 frontier and 4 open-source models on Skill^2-Bench reveals a skill-switching gap: accuracy decreases on higher-entropy tasks. We then turn skill entropy from a benchmark scale into a training signal. We propose Skill-Entropy RL, an RL framework where the model predicts not only the answer at each step but also the skill used to produce it. The reward combines step-level correctness with a skill-entropy reward that measures the alignment between the model-predicted skill sequence and the gold skill sequence. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct and Qwen3-1.7B, Skill-Entropy RL improves the Skill^2-Bench score from 34.4% to 68.4% and from 14.6% to 40.1%, respectively, outperforming competitive baselines. The same pipeline can be applied to off-the-shelf training data such as OpenR1-Math, indicating that skill entropy is a reusable training signal. Code available at: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Skill-Entropy-RL

</details>


### [183] [Reasoning Core: Designing Broad Procedural Data for Completion-Supervised Reasoning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05148)
*Damien Sileo,Valentin Lacombe,Dimitri Kachler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Core——一个包含50个程序化推理数据生成器的集合，覆盖数学、逻辑、规划等领域，并通过语义评分器、难度控制和任务评估器来提升生成质量。在匹配的完成监督协议下，Reasoning Core在3B模型基准上取得了最优平均分。研究还发现语义有效性并不能单独保证训练效用，并展示了结合模型辅助审查、人工裁决和回归测试的审计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化生成器能够大规模产生可验证的推理问题，但其作为完成监督微调数据的价值尚未被充分探索。现有程序化推理数据集在训练效用方面研究不足，且生成过程中的正确性问题（生成、渲染、目标和评分之间的不匹配）常被忽视。

Method: 构建Reasoning Core集合，包含50个覆盖数学、逻辑、规划、状态跟踪、形式语言、结构化数据、游戏、因果性和代码的生成器，配备语义评分器、难度控制和任务评估器。采用匹配的完成监督协议（matched completion-supervised protocol）在四种基座模型设置和多种训练时长下与Procedural Warmup、Reasoning Gym和SynLogic进行对比。同时设计了结合模型辅助审查、人工裁决和回归测试的审计流程。

Result: 在主要3B模型对比中，Reasoning Core在DROP、LogiQA和ARC-Challenge上取得最高平均分，超过了基线（无程序化数据）和所有三种替代程序化数据集合。任务级分析表明语义有效性并不能单独保证训练效用，紧凑目标和校准难度是重要的设计因素。审计流程揭示了各数据集合中生成、渲染、目标和评分之间的微妙不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 程序化生成本身并不保证数据正确性，需要系统的审计与质量保障流程。Reasoning Core及其生成数据、审计材料均已公开，为该领域后续研究提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Procedural generators produce useful verifiable reasoning problems at scale, but have received less attention as data for completion-supervised fine-tuning. We introduce Reasoning Core, a collection of 50 generators spanning mathematics, logic, planning, state tracking, formal languages, structured data, games, causality, and code, with semantic scorers, difficulty controls, and task evaluators. Under a matched completion-supervised protocol, we compare Reasoning Core with Procedural Warmup, Reasoning Gym, and SynLogic across four base-model settings and multiple training durations. In the primary 3B comparison, Reasoning Core achieves the highest mean scores on DROP, LogiQA, and ARC-Challenge, exceeding both the baseline without procedural data and all three alternative procedural collections. Task-level analyses show that semantic validity alone does not ensure training utility, highlighting compact targets and calibrated difficulty as important design factors. We ran audits combining model-assisted review, human adjudication, and regression testing. Applied throughout Reasoning Core development and to the other collections, they reveal subtle mismatches among generation, rendering, targets, and scoring, a reminder that procedural generation alone does not guarantee correctness. The library, generated datasets, and audit material are publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Simulation-Based Imaging: Learning Acoustic Inverse Problems from Simulated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04145)
*Luke Bodmer,E. Bruce Pitman*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出模拟仿真成像（SBI）框架，利用完全基于模拟数据训练的机器学习模型作为声学逆问题的实时求解器，实现无损声学成像。通过高保真非连续Galerkin正向求解器生成训练数据，2D卷积神经网络直接从144个边界传感器测量值学习到32×32×32体素的三维内部重建。模型无需包含物数量或几何形状的先验知识即可可靠恢复位置和尺寸，在5%加性测量噪声下重建误差仅退化13%，仅需24个传感器（全阵列的17%）即可达到全覆盖质量4%以内的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声学成像设备依赖复杂昂贵的硬件和逆问题求解算法，计算开销大、实时性差。现有方法需要先验知识且对噪声敏感，难以实现低成本、便携、可部署的成像系统。本文旨在通过软件层面的深度学习替代硬件复杂性，实现经济高效的无损声学成像。

Method: 采用高保真节点非连续Galerkin（nodal Discontinuous Galerkin）正向求解器在单位立方体域内随机化包含物几何结构生成大规模训练数据；使用2D卷积神经网络学习从边界压力测量到三维内部体素重建（32×32×32）的直接映射；网络在144个边界传感器的条件下训练，无需包含物数量或几何形状的先验假设。

Result: 训练后的模型能可靠恢复包含物的位置和尺寸；在5%加性测量噪声下重建误差仅退化13%；仅使用24个传感器（占144个全阵列的17%）时，重建质量仍可达到全覆盖精度的4%以内。

Conclusion: SBI框架证明了基于纯模拟数据训练的深度学习模型可作为可行的概念验证成像设备，其复杂性从硬件转移到软件层面，为低成本、便携、可部署的成像系统开辟了道路。

Abstract: We introduce Simulation-Based Imaging (SBI), a framework for non-destructive acoustic imaging in which machine learning models trained entirely on simulated data serve as real-time solvers for the acoustic inverse problem. A high-fidelity nodal Discontinuous Galerkin forward solver generates large training datasets by randomizing inclusion geometry within a unit-cube domain; a 2D convolutional neural network then learns a direct mapping from boundary pressure measurements to a 32 by 32 by 32 voxel reconstruction of the interior. The trained model reliably recovers inclusion position and size from 144 boundary sensors with no prior knowledge of inclusion count or geometry. Reconstruction error degrades by only 13% under 5% additive measurement noise, and just 17% of the sensor array (24 of 144 sensors) suffices for quality within 4% of full coverage. These results establish SBI as a viable proof-of-concept imaging device whose complexity resides in software rather than hardware, opening a path toward cheap, portable, deployable imaging systems.

</details>


### [185] [Trie-Constrained Token Prediction with Hierarchy-Aware Semantic Alignment for HS Code Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04464)
*Minseop Kim,Taekhyun Park,Kikun Park,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIE-HSA方法，将trie约束的令牌预测与层级感知语义对齐（HSA）相结合，使小语言模型（SLM）在商品文本的HS编码预测任务中既能输出结构合法的编码，又能捕捉HS码的层级语义，在码头真实数据上显著超越零样本推理和现有最强基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HS编码预测在国际贸易和港口物流中至关重要，大语言模型（LLMs）虽语言理解能力强但计算成本高，难以部署在集装箱码头等资源受限环境；小语言模型（SLMs）虽为可行替代方案，但容易生成无效HS编码，且忽略商品文本与HS编码之间的层级语义关系。

Method: 提出TRIE-HSA框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）trie约束的令牌预测——利用HS分类体系构建trie结构，约束SLM仅生成合法的编码数字；（2）层级感知语义对齐（HSA）——将商品文本表示与HS编码的层级结构进行语义对齐，使模型理解编码间的层级关系。

Result: 在从运营中集装箱码头采集的数据上进行大量实验，TRIE-HSA将平均HS6准确率相比零样本推理提升了49.96个百分点，并超过最强的任务特定基准11.94个百分点。结果表明，使用参数少于100亿的模型即可实现准确且结构合法的HS编码预测。

Conclusion: TRIE-HSA为无法支持大规模LLM部署的港口物流场景提供了实用且高性能的HS编码预测方案，证明了小规模模型在结合适当约束与对齐策略后能够达到实用水平的预测效果。

Abstract: Harmonized System (HS) code prediction (HSP) from commodity text is essential to international trade, and its importance continues to grow in port logistics. For the purposes of such prediction, recently, large language models (LLMs) have been actively investigated, owing especially to their strong language-understanding capabilities. However, their high computational cost limits deployment in constrained environments such as container terminals. Small language models (SLMs) offer a practical alternative, but their smaller scale makes them prone to generating invalid HS codes and to overlooking the hierarchical semantics between commodity text and HS codes. To address these limitations, this study proposes TRIE-HSA, which combines trie-constrained token prediction with hierarchy-aware semantic alignment (HSA). This method constrains the SLM to predict only valid digits under the HS taxonomy and aligns commodity text representations with the hierarchical structure of HS codes. In extensive experiments on data collected from an operational container terminal, TRIE-HSA improved average HS6 accuracy by 49.96 %p over zero-shot inference and exceeded the strongest task-specific benchmark by 11.94 %p. These results demonstrate that accurate and structurally valid HSP is achievable with fewer than 10 billion parameters. Therefore, TRIE-HSA offers a practical basis for deployment of HSP in port logistics operations that cannot support largescale LLMs.

</details>


### [186] [Benchmarking Multi-fidelity Neural Operators on Complex PDE Problems with Non-trivial Fidelity Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04708)
*Ghifari Adam Faza,Kemas Zakaria,Pramudita Satria Palar,Keivan Shariatmadar,Lavi Rizki Zuhal,Hans Hallez,David Moens*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种多保真度神经算子策略（两步法、残差法、中间法、迁移学习），在四个PDE测试用例上（包括两个标准网格问题、一个改进的Darcy流、一个非稳态烟雾流入）的表现。核心发现是：直接在HF模型中输入LF预测的方法在大保真度差异下容易产生误差放大，而迁移学习通过LF预训练权重初始化HF模型，始终能提升精度，是一种可靠的多保真度PDE代理建模方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解PDE控制的物理问题计算成本高昂，限制了高质量HF数据获取，而神经算子通常需要大量数据。多保真度学习通过结合丰富的低频数据和有限的HF样本来解决这一问题。现有研究多聚焦于网格分辨率差异导致的LF-HF不同，但真实场景（如CFD中RANS与LES模型之间）的差异更为复杂，需要更真实地评估多保真度策略在显著模型差异下的表现。

Method: 对四种多保真度神经算子策略（两步法、残差法、中间法、迁移学习）进行系统评估。设计了四个PDE测试用例：两个标准网格问题、一个具有LF-HF控制方程差异的改进Darcy流、一个具有时间动态的非稳态烟雾流入。特别引入了两个新测试用例来模拟超越简单网格分辨率差异的非平凡LF-HF失配。

Result: 直接向HF模型输入LF预测的方法在大LF-HF差异下容易产生误差放大，尤其在自回归设置中。相比之下，迁移学习（用LF训练权重初始化HF模型）通过提供由HF数据精化的鲁棒先验，始终能提升精度。

Conclusion: 迁移学习应被视为一种可靠且高效的多保真度PDE代理建模方法，特别是在LF-HF模型之间存在复杂系统差异的现实场景中。

Abstract: Solving PDE-governed physical problems is computationally expensive, limiting the availability of high-fidelity (HF) data for training neural operators, which typically require large datasets. Multi-fidelity learning addresses this by combining abundant low-fidelity (LF) data with limited HF samples. To evaluate several multi-fidelity neural operator strategies, including two-step, residual, intermediate, and transfer learning, we conduct a systematic assessment across four PDE test cases: two standard grid-based problems, a modified Darcy flow with LF-HF governing equation discrepancies, and an unsteady smoke inflow with temporal dynamics. Most prior studies focus on grid-based LF-HF differences, but real-world discrepancies, such as those between RANS and LES models in CFD, can be more complex. To capture these more challenging scenarios, we introduce two new test cases specifically designed to emulate nontrivial LF-HF mismatch beyond simple grid-resolution differences, enabling a more realistic assessment of multi-fidelity strategies under substantial model discrepancies. We find that methods directly feeding LF predictions into HF models are prone to error amplification under large LF-HF discrepancies, particularly in autoregressive settings. In contrast, transfer learning, which uses LF-trained weights to initialise the HF model, consistently improves accuracy by providing a robust prior refined with HF data. These results highlight the importance of establishing transfer learning as a reliable approach for efficient multi-fidelity PDE surrogate modelling.

</details>


### [187] [scikit-rom: An Open-Source Python Platform for Teaching and Prototyping Projection-Based Reduced-Order Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04960)
*Suparno Bhattacharyya,Ali Syed,Jian Tao,Jean C. Ragusa*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 scikit-rom，一个基于 scikit-fem 构建的开源 Python 库，旨在使基于投影的降阶建模（ROM）全流程透明、可修改，并适合交互式笔记本探索。该库提供模块化问题模板，支持快照生成、降阶基构造、降阶算子组装、在线 ROM 求解、超降阶（支持DEIM、S-OPT、ECSW和ECM四种策略）以及定量精度评估，并通过四个递进式示例覆盖线性/非线性、静/瞬态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影式降阶建模虽已成为加速参数密集型工程仿真的核心技术，但其多阶段工作流（快照生成、SVD/POD基构造、Galerkin投影、离线-在线分解、超降阶、误差评估）难以教学和原型验证。现有软件框架要么将各阶段抽象在高层次接口之后，要么依赖难以检查和修改的编译式单片求解器栈，对研究生和早期研究者不友好。

Method: scikit-rom 采用轻量级有限元后端 scikit-fem 构建，提供模块化问题模板及统一工作流中的专用设施：快照生成、降阶基构造、降阶算子组装、在线ROM求解、超降阶（支持DEIM、S-OPT、ECSW、ECM四种策略）以及定量精度评估。库还提供四个从全阶模拟到ROM再到超降阶模型构建的递进式教学示例。

Result: scikit-rom 作为一个开源库发布，源代码与示例托管于 GitHub，文档位于 scikitrom.github.io。其设计面向研究生教学、强化研讨班和降阶建模社区的研究原型开发。

Conclusion: scikit-rom 通过提供透明、可修改的完整投影式ROM流水线，降低了降阶建模的教学和原型开发门槛，为降阶建模社区贡献了一个适合教学和研究的开源工具。

Abstract: Projection-based reduced-order modeling (ROM) has become a cornerstone technique for accelerating parameter-intensive engineering simulations, yet the methodology remains challenging to teach and prototype. The difficulty stems from its multi-stage workflow, which encompasses snapshot generation, SVD/POD basis construction, Galerkin projection, offline-online decomposition, hyper-reduction, and error assessment. Existing software frameworks either abstract these stages behind high-level interfaces or depend on compiled, monolithic solver stacks that are difficult for students and early-stage researchers to inspect or modify. This paper introduces scikit-rom, an open-source Python library designed to make the complete projection-based ROM pipeline transparent, modifiable, and suitable for interactive notebook-based exploration. Built on the lightweight finite element backend scikit-fem, scikit-rom provides modular problem templates and dedicated facilities for snapshot generation, reduced-basis construction, reduced-operator assembly, online ROM solution, hyper-reduction, and quantitative accuracy assessment within a unified workflow. Hyper-reduction is supported through four strategies: the Discrete Empirical Interpolation Method (DEIM), S-OPT sampling, the Energy-Conserving Sampling and Weighting (ECSW) scheme, and ECM-style cubature construction. Four progressive worked examples guide the reader from full-order simulation to ROM and hyper-reduced model construction across linear, nonlinear, static, and transient problem classes. The library is intended for graduate-level instruction, intensive workshops, and research prototyping in the reduced-order modeling community. Source code and examples are available at github.com/suparnob100/scikit-rom, with documentation at scikitrom.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Sublogarithmic Swap Regret in Multiplayer General-Sum Games via Hybrid Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04149)
*Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非耦合动力学，使每个玩家在一般和多人博弈中仅产生 O(nm²√(log m log T)) 的交换悔恨，首次实现次对数级别的个体保证，并证明其收敛到近似关联均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多人一般和博弈中，交换悔恨决定了非耦合学习动力学收敛到关联均衡的速率。在全信息反馈下，当所有玩家遵循相同动力学时，此前的最高保证是随时间跨度 T 对数增长的。作者希望突破这一对数界限，获得更优的个体交换悔恨保证。

Method: 核心算法选择是将 Blum-Mansour 约简与乐观跟随正则化领导者（optimistic FTRL）结合，使用一个混合正则化器，分别加权负 Shannon 熵和对数障碍（log-barrier）：熵用于控制乐观预测误差，对数障碍通过其 Bregman 散度控制转移矩阵的移动。此外，提出了一个新的马尔可夫链平稳分布敏感性定理，既不涉及混合参数也不涉及最小转移概率，从而将控制传递到实际博弈策略上，并简化了分析，无需局部范数或自相容性论证。

Result: 每个玩家仅产生 O(nm²√(log m log T)) 的交换悔恨，首次在设置中实现次对数个体保证；时间平均乘积分布是 O(nm²√(log m log T)/T) 的近似关联均衡。该保证还被对抗鲁棒变体（针对任意效用序列额外保证 O(nm²√(log m log T)+√(mT log m))）和无视时间跨度变体（无需预先知道 T）所保留。

Conclusion: 这项工作通过创新的混合正则化器和马尔可夫链敏感性分析，首次在多人一般和博弈的非耦合学习动力学中实现了次对数交换悔恨，并提供了更简单且更强的理论分析，同时支持对抗鲁棒性和无地平线设置。

Abstract: Swap regret governs the rate at which uncoupled learning dynamics converge to correlated equilibria in multiplayer general-sum games. Under full-information feedback, the best previous guarantee when every player follows the same dynamics grows logarithmically in the horizon $T$. We construct uncoupled dynamics under which every player incurs only $O(nm^2\sqrt{\log m\log T})$ swap regret, where $n$ is the number of players and $m$ bounds the number of actions per player. To our knowledge, this is the first sublogarithmic individual guarantee in this setting, and it implies that the time-averaged product distribution of play is an $O(nm^2\sqrt{\log m\log T}/T)$-approximate correlated equilibrium. The key algorithmic choice is to combine the Blum--Mansour reduction with optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader using a hybrid regularizer that separately weights negative Shannon entropy and the log-barrier: the entropy controls the optimistic prediction error, whereas the log-barrier controls the transition-matrix movement through its Bregman divergence. A new sensitivity theorem for stationary distributions of Markov chains, which involves neither mixing parameters nor the smallest transition probability, transfers this control to the played strategies and yields a simpler analysis without local-norm or self-concordance arguments. The guarantee is preserved by an adversarially robust variant that additionally ensures $O(nm^2\sqrt{\log m\log T}+\sqrt{mT\log m})$ swap regret against arbitrary utility sequences, and by a horizon-free variant that requires no prior knowledge of $T$.

</details>


### [189] [From Compensation Design to Budget-Feasible Mechanisms: A Constant Approximation for Subadditive Valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04337)
*Ioannis Anagnostides,Kshipra Bhawalkar,Christopher Liaw,Aranyak Mehta,Grigoris Velegkas,Weiqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文在预算可行机制设计领域取得重大突破，大幅改进了多种估值函数下的近似比上界，并通过补偿设计框架和自界函数平滑引理解决了子加性估值下的常数近似多项式时间机制这一长期开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预算可行机制设计由Singer提出，但上下界之间仍存在巨大差距。特别是子加性估值下是否存在常数近似多项式时间机制是Dobzinski、Papadimitriou和Singer提出的长期开放问题，他们猜想常数近似需要指数次需求查询。

Method: 提出了一个简单而统一的框架，基于非真实间接机制（补偿设计）。通过势能论证为边际贡献支付规则的补偿设计建立了常数价格稳定性界，并将其转化为真实直接机制。对子加性估值，核心贡献是一个新的平滑引理：每个子加性函数可以被自界函数在2倍因子内逼近。

Result: 在没有计算约束下：(1) 单调子模估值达到3近似（原3.798），非单调子模达到e+1（原9.742）；(2) XOS估值达到e+1（原28），大市场确定性e；(3) 子加性估值达到2e+1（原33），大市场确定性2e。在多项式时间内使用需求查询，子加性估值实现常数近似，优于此前O(log log n)的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了子加性估值下常数近似的长期开放问题，推翻了指数查询下界的猜想。平滑引理具有独立价值，解决了多赢家选举中在子加性估值下2e近似核心的存在性问题。

Abstract: Budget-feasible mechanism design is a classic framework introduced by Singer, but there is still a wide gap between existing upper and lower bounds. In this paper, we significantly advance the state of the art. First, without computational constraints, we show that there exists a budget-feasible universally truthful mechanism with the following approximation ratios:
  - $3$ for monotone submodular valuations and $e+1$ for nonmonotone submodular valuations, improving over $3.798$ and $9.742$, respectively.
  - $e+1$ for XOS valuations, improving over $28$. In large markets, our approximation can be improved deterministically to $e$.
  - $2e+1$ for subadditive valuations, improving over $33$. In large markets, our approximation can be improved deterministically to $2e$.
  Moreover, for subadditive valuations, we obtain a constant-approximation mechanism that runs in polynomial time using demand queries. This improves over the previous best approximation of $O(\log \log n)$, resolving a long-standing open problem going back to Dobzinski, Papadimitriou, and Singer, who conjectured that a constant approximation requires exponentially many demand queries. We obtain these results through a simple and unifying framework based on non-truthful indirect mechanisms, recently coined compensation design. In particular, through a potential argument, we establish constant price-of-stability bounds for compensation design based on marginal-contribution payment rules, which we then translate into truthful direct mechanisms. For subadditive valuations, the core of the argument is a new smoothing lemma showing that every subadditive function can be approximated within a factor of $2$ by a self-bounding function. This is also of independent interest, readily addressing an open question in multiwinner elections by showing the existence of a $2e$-approximate core even under subadditive valuations.

</details>


### [190] [From Cake-Cutting and Necklace-Splitting to Fair Division of Indivisible Items](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04340)
*Max Dupré la Tour,Ayumi Igarashi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个存在性转移框架，将连续公平分割定理转化为路径上不可分割物品分配的EFk类型保证。结合连通蛋糕分割定理和等基数项链分割定理，获得了任意素数幂个数的智能体下的连通分配结果，包括EF1保证、共识公平性保证（consensus up to n goods and n chores），以及EF2分配与均衡束大小等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续公平分割（如蛋糕分割）已有丰富理论，但不可分割物品的分配缺乏有效的归纳方法。作者希望建立一座桥梁，使得连续域中成熟的理论（如envy-freeness、consensus划分定理）能够直接转化为离散路径上不可分割物品分配的EFk类型保证，尤其针对非可加偏好（non-additive valuations）这一此前缺乏理论支撑的场景。

Method: 作者引入一个存在性转移框架（existential transfer framework），将连续公平分割定理的结论翻译为路径上不可分割物品的分配保证。具体采用两种工具组合：(1) 连通蛋糕分割定理，在相同估值和任意估值（智能体数为素数幂）下获得EF1连通分配；(2) Jojić等人的等基数项链分割定理，构造任意素数幂束数下每个束由至多n个区间组成且满足共识性质的分配。同时可在额外代价（一个区间和一个物品）下叠加无嫉妒约束。

Result: 获得了以下主要结果：(1) 在相同估值及任意估值（智能体数为素数幂）下存在连通EF1分配；(2) 对于素数幂个束和n个任意估值函数，存在每个束为至多n个区间并集且满足consensus up to n goods and n chores的分配，这是首个针对非可加估值超越对半情形的EFk类型共识保证；(3) 当智能体数为素数幂时，单调估值实例存在EF2分配且各束大小差异至多为2。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将连续公平分割理论推广到不可分割物品领域，首次为非可加估值下的共识公平分割提供了EFk类型保证，并为无嫉妒与均衡束大小约束开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We give an existential transfer framework for converting continuous fair division theorems into guarantees for indivisible items arranged on a path. This allows continuous envy-freeness and consensus results to translate directly into EF$k$-type guarantees for indivisible allocations.
  Combining this method with connected cake-cutting theorems, we obtain connected allocations satisfying envy-freeness up to one good and one chore for identical valuations and for arbitrary valuations when the number of agents is a prime power.
  Combining this method with the equicardinal necklace-splitting theorem of Jojić et al., we show that, for any prime-power number $r$ of bundles and $n$ arbitrary valuation functions, there exists an allocation in which every bundle is the union of at most $n$ intervals, and the bundles satisfy consensus up to $n$ goods and $n$ chores. This result is the first EF$k$-type guarantee for consensus fair division with non-additive valuations beyond the halving case.
  Envy-freeness constraints can be imposed simultaneously at the cost of one additional interval and one additional item in each guarantee. As a consequence, when the number of agents is a prime power, every instance with monotone valuations admits an EF$2$ allocation whose bundle sizes differ by at most two.

</details>


### [191] [Towards Decentralized Searcher Competition in MEV Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05011)
*Roozbeh Sarenche,Yunwen Liu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文从搜索者（searcher）竞争视角研究MEV市场中的中心化问题，提出异质模型刻画搜索者在机会覆盖和执行效率上的差异，并引入Shapley加权Jain公平指数和期望奖励HHI两个度量指标。在标准第一价格赢者通吃拍卖存在集中化和不公平问题的基础上，提出一种入口过滤的Shapley封顶拍卖机制，并通过贝叶斯安全约束对抗Sybil攻击和验证者共谋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MEV中心化研究多聚焦于构建者（builders）、验证者（validators）和区块构建市场，忽略了搜索者竞争层面的中心化问题。搜索者层面的经济权力持续集中同样会削弱竞争、降低开放性，违背无需许可协议的去中心化目标，因此需要从搜索者角度系统分析拍卖机制设计对公平性、去中心化和安全性的影响。

Method: 构建异质搜索者模型（差异维度：机会覆盖与执行效率），提出两个度量指标：Shapley加权Jain公平指数（衡量奖励与边际贡献的匹配程度）和期望奖励HHI（衡量长期奖励集中度）。以标准第一价格赢者通吃拍卖为基准分析，进而提出入口过滤的Shapley封顶拍卖机制；针对无需许可环境中的Sybil攻击（复制/降级策略）和验证者-搜索者共谋，引入贝叶斯安全约束进行机制设计。

Result: 分析表明，在搜索者异质性下，第一价格拍卖会奖励排名优势（rank dominance）而非边际贡献，导致奖励集中且贡献调整后公平性显著降低。提出的Shapley封顶拍卖机制能够在贝叶斯安全约束下更公平、更广泛地在合格的高质量提交中分配搜索者奖励，缓解Sybil偏差和验证者-搜索者共谋。

Conclusion: MEV搜索者竞争中的拍卖设计是影响市场中心化程度的关键因素；第一价格赢者通吃机制天然偏向排名优势，而Shapley封顶的入口过滤机制可同时兼顾公平性、去中心化与安全性，为无需许可区块链环境下的搜索者激励设计提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Centralization in maximal extractable value (MEV) markets is a significant concern for blockchain systems, as persistent concentration of economic power can weaken competition, reduce openness, and undermine the decentralization goals of permissionless protocols. While much of the existing analysis has focused on builders, validators, and block-building markets, this paper studies centralization from the perspective of searcher competition. We develop a heterogeneous model in which searchers differ in opportunity coverage and execution efficiency, and we analyze how auction design affects fairness, decentralization, and security among searchers competing for the same MEV opportunity.
  To evaluate searcher competition, we introduce two metrics: a Shapley-weighted Jain fairness index, which measures whether rewards are proportional to searchers' marginal contributions, and an expected-reward Herfindahl-Hirschman Index (HHI), which measures concentration in long-run searcher rewards. Using these metrics, we first analyze the standard first-price, winner-take-all auction as a benchmark. Our analysis shows that, under searcher heterogeneity, first-price competition can reward rank dominance rather than marginal contribution, leading to concentrated rewards and weaker contribution-adjusted fairness.
  Motivated by these limitations, we propose an entry-filtered Shapley-capped auction mechanism that distributes searcher rewards more fairly and broadly among admitted high-quality submissions. Designing such a mechanism in a permissionless blockchain environment is challenging: searchers may create Sybil identities by submitting copied or degraded versions of the same execution strategy, and validators may collude with searchers to increase joint payoff. We address these concerns through Bayesian security constraints for copied-code Sybil deviations and validator-searcher coalition deviations.

</details>


### [192] [Dimensions of Power: A Systematic Guide to Power Indices for Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05031)
*Filip Naudot,Arunavo Ganguly,Timotheus Kampik,Vicenç Torra,Christopher Blöcker*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文系统性地组织了博弈论中的权力指数在AI可解释性归因任务中的应用，将其划分为三个归因维度（单玩家、集合基、基数基），并研究了各维度下权力指数的形式化性质与适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权力指数正越来越多地被用于AI系统输出归因以实现可解释性，但如何为特定解释任务选择合适的权力指数是一个研究不足的问题。

Method: 作者沿三个归因维度（单玩家、集合基、基数基）组织权力指数，对现有的权力指数进行综述与推广，分析其满足的形式化原则，补充文献中缺失的性质证明，并通过具体实例展示不同维度与指数选择对归因结果的影响。

Result: 证明了移动到基数基设定会移除玩家身份信息但保留了一些指数级别的区分能力；提供了文献中缺失的性质证明；给出了实践中为不同应用场景选择合适权力指数的指导建议。

Conclusion: 不同归因维度和权力指数的选择会显著影响AI归因结果，作者为从业者提供了一套系统化的选择框架和指导原则。

Abstract: Power indices, originating in cooperative game theory, quantify each player's influence on the outcome of a given game. Originally designed to distribute profits or costs among players and to analyse the fairness of voting systems, power indices have recently gained prominence as methods for attributing outputs of AI-based systems to inputs, thus facilitating explainability. However, selecting the appropriate power index for a given explanation task is an understudied problem. To address this, we organise power indices along three attribution dimensions: single-player, set-based, and cardinality-based. For each dimension, we review the corresponding power indices, generalise existing ones where applicable, and analyse which formal principles they satisfy. We provide proofs for properties that are missing in the literature and show that moving to the cardinality-based setting removes player-identity information while preserving some index-level distinctions. Using concrete examples, we illustrate how the choice of dimension and index affects the resulting attributions in practice, and offer guidance for practitioners seeking to select a suitable power index for a given application context.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [A Long-Run Persistence Theory for AI Systems under the Redundancy-Adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04012)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于冗余调整的人工年龄评分（AAS）的长程持久性框架，证明AI系统可以经历无限循环运行而结构年龄保持有界，即无需经历无界结构老化即可持续存在，并建立了包括负担持久性、零负担持久性、振荡持久性和累积终端负担在内的渐近体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越需要在重复的交互、适应和更新循环中运行，而非孤立的一次性输出。这引出一个基本理论问题：AI系统能否无限持久而不产生无界的结构老化？现有AAS仅是静态评估指标，无法刻画系统在重复运行中的长期行为，因此需要发展一个循环级的功能性框架来分析长期持久性。

Method: 将AAS从静态评估指标扩展为循环级泛函，在每次循环中通过加权、冗余感知的对数惩罚函数定义结构年龄。在此基础上定义渐近体制的层级结构（负担持久性、零负担持久性、振荡持久性、累积终端负担），并建立比较排序、灵敏度界限、分量稳定下的收敛性、有限全变差下的持久性、阻尼循环间扰动下的几何稳定性以及非退化冗余条件下的零负担刻画等理论结果。

Result: 主要结果证明无限循环延续不需要无界结构老化：AI系统可以经历无限多个循环而结构年龄保持有界；在更强的正则条件下边际老化消失；在最强体制下循环级负担收敛到零。同时建立了多个渐近体制及其理论性质（排序、灵敏度、收敛和稳定性）。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析长期人工持久性提供了形式化基础，将其视为有界结构负担问题而非不可避免的累积恶化，为理论上理解AI系统在无限交互循环中的持续运行能力提供了严格的数学依据。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly expected to operate over repeated cycles of interaction, adaptation, and update rather than through isolated one-shot outputs. This raises a fundamental theoretical question: can an AI system persist indefinitely without incurring unbounded structural aging? This paper develops a long-run persistence framework for AI systems based on the redundancy-adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS). The model extends AAS from a static evaluative measure into a cycle-level functional that generates an age sequence across repeated operation. At each cycle, structural age is defined through a weighted, redundancy-aware logarithmic penalty over component consistency levels. Within this framework, cycle-level age is shown to be well defined and uniformly bounded, thereby excluding explosive pointwise aging. On this basis, the paper defines a hierarchy of asymptotic regimes, including burdened persistence, zero-burden persistence, oscillatory persistence, and cumulative terminal burden. It also establishes comparative ordering, sensitivity bounds, convergence under componentwise stabilization, persistence under finite total variation, geometric stabilization under damped inter-cycle perturbations, and a zero-burden characterization under nondegenerate redundancy conditions. The main result is that indefinite cyclic continuation does not require unbounded structural aging: an AI system may pass through infinitely many cycles while its structural age remains bounded, while under stronger regularity conditions its marginal aging vanishes and, in the strongest regime, its cycle-level burden converges to zero. The framework thus provides a formal basis for analyzing long-run artificial persistence as a problem of bounded structural burden rather than inevitable cumulative deterioration.

</details>


### [194] [The LLM Proposes, the Executive Disposes: A Self-Verifying Agent Instrument that Dissociates Commitment Drift from Binding Drift in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04066)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构性验证的长时程智能体仪器：确定性执行器持有全部信念，语言模型仅能提交类型化提案，主张通过预先注册的预测与代码观测的匹配才被接纳。该仪器通过每次运行自失效机制和渲染不可见的影子参考来实现结构性验证，并报告了一个关于目标放弃失败的单变量消融结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体的自身状态和自我报告不可信，传统的基于自述的事后验证（post-hoc verification）不可靠。作者希望构建一种验证方式，使验证在结构层面而非事后层面完成，从而提供可信任的智能体开发方法论。

Method: 构建了一个"智能体仪器"：(1) 确定性执行器（Executive）持有全部信念，语言模型只能提交类型化提案（typed proposals）；(2) 主张（claim）只有在动作前预注册的预测被代码观测匹配后才被接纳；(3) 每次运行在per-organ写错误、渲染大小或盐化金丝雀回声（salted-canary-echo）下限被突破时自动失效；(4) 使用渲染不可见的影子参考（shadow reference）在每个消融单元中编译完整系统本会承诺的计划，从而在机制被移除的单元中仍能定义漂移指标。基于此仪器进行单变量消融实验（三个种子每单元，最多394个参考节拍每运行，所有运行均有效门控）。

Result: (1) 前八次架构运行中有四次因失效机制被标记无效，每次均定位到真实缺陷；(2) 消融承诺机制（commitment mechanism）使目标放弃率从0.00翻转为1.00，而绑定错误保持平坦0.00；(3) 消融绑定修复时，绑定通道未以每节拍漂移形式重现——因为绑定是代码拥有的，失败类别被结构性吸收，其残余仅在上游一层以假设形成崩溃的形式出现；(4) 任务效能为零（52次门控运行在ARC-AGI-3上零级别完成），作为预注册的结构性挫败因素（structural defeater）被完全披露。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于一种智能体开发的验证方法论，以及该方法论可测量的漂移分解（drift decomposition）。通过将验证嵌入结构而非依赖事后检查，该仪器实现了对自身科学性的验证，即使任务本身效能为零，其核心价值在于提供了一种结构性、可失效、可分解的验证框架。

Abstract: How do you verify a long-horizon agent when its own state and self-reports are exactly what you cannot trust? We present an agent instrument built so that verification is structural rather than post-hoc. A deterministic Executive owns all belief; a language model may only file typed proposals, and a claim is admitted only when a prediction pre-registered before acting is matched against observation by code. Two properties make the instrument a verifier of its own science, not just of the agent: every run invalidates itself when per-organ write-error, render-size, or salted-canary-echo floors are breached (four of the first eight architecture runs were invalidated, each localizing a real defect); and a render-invisible shadow reference compiles the plan the full system would have committed in every ablation cell, so drift metrics are defined even where the mechanism under test has been removed. Using this instrument we report a clean, single-variable result on a failure every long-horizon agent suffers: ablating the commitment mechanism flips goal-abandonment from 0.00 to 1.00 while binding error stays flat at 0.00 (three seeds per cell, up to 394 reference beats per run, every run gated valid). The binding channel, by contrast, does not reappear as per-beat drift when its repair is ablated -- because binding is code-owned, the failure class is structurally absorbed, its only residue appearing one layer upstream as a collapse in hypothesis formation. We report these under full disclosure that task efficacy is null (zero level completions across 52 gated runs on ARC-AGI-3), pre-registered as a structural defeater. The contribution is a verification methodology for agent development and the drift decomposition it makes measurable.

</details>


### [195] [Monte Carlo Tree Search for Table-to-Multimodal Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04071)
*Teng Lin,Zhiyang Zhang,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MCTS-Report，一种基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的多模态表格到报告生成框架，将报告生成分解为原子动作并通过LLM动态推理逐步构建，配合多维奖励函数和MMRBench基准，在结构化完整性、数值准确性、图表-文本对齐和洞察新颖性上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖固定的线性流水线和孤立子任务处理，难以联合优化事实准确性、视觉质量和叙述连贯性这三个关键维度，导致结构化表格数据到专业多模态报告（含文本分析和可视化图表）的自动生成效果不佳。

Method: MCTS-Report框架将表到报告的生成建模为结构化搜索空间上的渐进构建过程，将报告生成分解为章节规划、可视化任务识别、图表生成、洞察组织和叙述细化五类原子动作，每个动作由LLM基于当前报告状态动态推理执行；在MCTS过程中存储逐步推理轨迹至节点以实现上下文感知的连贯构建；设计多维奖励函数联合评估数值事实一致性（通过SQL）、图表质量、图表-文本对齐和结构完整性，并加入多样性惩罚抑制重复图表和前置条件检查剪枝无效动作；同时构建了包含六个领域真实表格的MMRBench基准。

Result: 在MMRBench上的实验表明，MCTS-Report在结构化完整性、数值准确性、图表-文本对齐和洞察新颖性方面均显著优于强基线方法，总评分达到77.9。

Conclusion: MCTS-Report通过将报告生成建模为MCTS驱动的渐进构建过程，有效解决了现有线性流水线方法无法联合优化多维度质量指标的问题，为多模态数据智能报告生成提供了新的范式，并配套了高质量基准MMRBench。

Abstract: Automatically generating professional multimodal reports comprising both textual analysis and visual charts from structured tabular data is a critical challenge in data intelligence. Existing methods suffer from fixed linear pipelines and isolated subtask processing, which hinder joint optimization of factual accuracy, visual quality, and narrative coherence. To address these issues, this paper proposes MCTS-Report, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-driven framework that formulates multimodal table-to-report generation as a progressive construction process over a structured search space. The core idea is to decompose report generation into atomic actions, including chapter planning, visualization task identification, chart generation, insight organization, and narrative refinement, each executed by an LLM based on dynamic reasoning conditioned on the current report state. We use an LLM to generate step-by-step reasoning and actions during MCTS, storing the reasoning trajectory in each node for context-aware, coherent report construction. To guide the search, we design a multi-dimensional reward function that jointly evaluates numerical fact consistency (via SQL), chart quality, chart-text alignment, and structural completeness, while incorporating a diversity penalty to suppress repeated charts and a precondition check to prune invalid actions. We also construct MMRBench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising real-world tables from six domains, paired with expert-refined reference report structures and verifiable key insights. Experiments on MMRBench demonstrate that MCTS-Report significantly outperforms strong baselines across structural completeness, numerical accuracy, chart-text alignment, and insight novelty, achieving a 77.9 overall score.

</details>


### [196] [FinProBench: Evaluating Financial AI Agents with Role-Grounded Rubrics Derived from Professional Deliverables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04077)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinProBench基准和Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC)流水线，用于专业金融AI智能体的评估。RGRC从从业者实际交付物中提取隐性标准构建评估指标，在57个职业、1723个交付物上进行验证。结果显示，对于原型丰富的常规角色，Prompt-only方法几乎与RGRC相当（89.2% vs 90.7%），但对于角色专业化角色，RGRC显著优于Prompt-only（99.1% vs 78.0%）。人工交付物在评估中排名第一（73.7分），角色级指标复用可将每任务构建成本降低6.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估金融AI智能体的指标方法通常从任务提示或模型输出中派生标准，忽视了从业者交付物中才能体现的隐性专业标准。需要一种能捕捉真正专业工作中的隐性质量标准的方法。

Method: 提出FinProBench基准和RGRC流水线。RGRC包含四个阶段：交付物收集（Deliverable Collection）、能力提取（Competency Extraction）、标准综合（Rubric Synthesis）和验证（Validation）。将57个职业按交付物类型分为30个先验丰富的常规角色和27个先验稀少的角色专业化角色。基准包含覆盖57个职业、8个金融子行业、161种交付物类型的1723个精选交付物，初始评估集包含20个完整任务覆盖20个角色。

Result: RGRC指标能捕捉隐性标准并区分质量等级。对常规角色，Prompt-only几乎匹配RGRC（89.2% vs 90.7%）；对角色专业化角色，RGRC大幅领先（99.1% vs 78.0%）。使用异构LLM评审和角色级指标时，人工交付物平均排名第一（73.7 vs 70.3、70.2、69.6），四个系统95%置信区间重叠但各有互补优势。角色级指标复用使每任务构建成本降低6.7倍。

Conclusion: 该研究证明当专业标准在模型先验中有充分体现时，提示工程可以近似评估指标的效果；但超出模型先验的专业标准需要基于从业者交付物的专业落地作为支撑。RGRC在角色专业化任务中优势显著，且通过角色级指标复用大幅降低构建成本。

Abstract: Evaluating financial AI agents requires criteria aligned with real professional work. Existing rubric methods typically derive criteria from task prompts or model outputs, overlooking tacit standards visible only in practitioner deliverables. We introduce FinProBench, a benchmark for professional financial tasks, and Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC), a reusable pipeline that derives rubrics from deliverables produced by practitioners in the same role. RGRC comprises four stages: Deliverable Collection, Competency Extraction, Rubric Synthesis, and Validation. Its rubrics capture tacit standards, distinguish quality levels, and transfer across tasks within a role. Before analysis, we classified 57 occupations by deliverable genre into 30 prior-rich conventional roles and 27 prior-sparse role-specialized roles. Across all roles, Prompt-only nearly matches RGRC for conventional roles (89.2% vs. 90.7%), but RGRC substantially outperforms it for role-specialized roles (99.1% vs. 78.0%). This split indicates that prompt engineering can approximate rubrics when conventions are well represented in model priors, while professional grounding is essential for standards beyond those priors. FinProBench is built from 1,723 curated deliverables spanning 57 occupations, 8 financial sub-industries, and 161 deliverable types, and releases an initial evaluation set of 20 complete tasks covering 20 roles in 7 sub-industries. With heterogeneous LLM judges and role-level rubrics, human deliverables rank first on average (73.7 vs. 70.3, 70.2, and 69.6 out of 100), while all four systems show overlapping 95% confidence intervals and complementary strengths. Reusing rubrics at the role level reduces estimated per-task construction effort by 6.7 times relative to authoring each rubric from scratch.

</details>


### [197] [FinPerMA: A Theory-Informed, Event-Grounded Personalized-Memory Benchmark for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04095)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinPerMA，一个基于事件驱动的个性化记忆基准，用于评估大语言模型代理能否在长期金融咨询场景中维护和更新用户模型。基于276个真实投资者画像和2,994个问题，实验发现七个前沿LLM及七种记忆配置均远未饱和（整体准确率约0.47，选择题约39%），且基于摘要的记忆常丢失偏好信号，简单检索在冲击事件后反而优于专门构建的记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化记忆基准主要测试事实记忆或在弱约束的模型生成轨迹上进行评估，忽视了事件驱动的偏好适应性。在高风险领域（如金融咨询）中，LLM代理需要长期维护和更新个体化的用户模型，但这一能力尚不明确，因此需要一个能够严格评估事件驱动偏好适应的基准。

Method: FinPerMA的生成流水线结合了三个阶段：（1）确定性、基于理论驱动的冲击规则（theory-informed impact rules）；（2）受控的LLM叙述生成；（3）自动化质量筛选。此外设立Post-Shock检查点，用于隔离判断代理是否已将重大事件整合进其持久用户模型中。数据集包含276个画像（personas）和2,994个问题。

Result: 在七个前沿LLM和最多七种记忆配置的评估中，没有任何全上下文配置在整体准确率上超过约0.47，或在多选题上超过约39%。属性分析显示，基于摘要的记忆通常能保留事实细节但丢失个性化所需的偏好信号，因此简单检索方法在冲击事件后反而优于专用的记忆系统，且这一差距在冲击后进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 当前LLM代理在事件驱动的个性化记忆上远未饱和，尤其是摘要型记忆在偏好捕捉上存在系统性缺陷。这意味着需要开发更有效的记忆机制来捕获和更新用户的偏好变化，而非仅关注事实保留，FinPerMA为此类研究提供了标准化的评估平台。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used as personalized assistants in high-stakes domains such as financial advising, yet it remains unclear whether they can maintain and update an individualized user model over long horizons. Existing personalized-memory benchmarks primarily test factual retention or rely on weakly constrained model-generated trajectories, leaving event-driven preference adaptation underexplored. We introduce FinPerMA, an event-grounded benchmark that evaluates personalized memory against frozen longitudinal investor trajectories. Its generation pipeline combines deterministic, theory-informed impact rules, controlled LLM narration, and automated quality screening; a Post-Shock checkpoint isolates whether an agent has integrated a material event into its persistent user model. On 2,994 questions from 276 personas, seven frontier LLMs and up to seven memory configurations remain far from saturated: no full-context configuration exceeds approximately 0.47 overall accuracy or approximately 39% on multiple-choice questions. Attribution analysis shows that summary-based memory often preserves factual details while losing the preference signals needed for personalization; simple retrieval can therefore outperform purpose-built memory systems, with the gap widening after shocks.

</details>


### [198] [BrainBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Comprehensive EEG Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04156)
*Yangxuan Zhou,Sha Zhao,Yuning Chen,Chen Wu,Jiquan Wang,Shijian Li,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的基准测试集，用于评估大语言模型在脑电图（EEG）综合分析方面的能力。该基准涵盖17个数据集、多种任务类型和大量真实数据实例，通过数值、分类、集合、序列、语义和伪影多种验证方式全面评估系统的科学报告输出能力。作者评估了多个代表性LLM在自主代码执行（CodeAct）和结构化智能体分析（BrainAgent）两种范式下的表现，结果表明模型的EEG能力高度依赖于模型本身及其操作化方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EEG分析评估主要集中在孤立的解码任务或特定系统的演示上，缺乏对LLM在完整EEG理解流程（从自然语言指令、信号处理、定量证据到科学解释）中能力的量化评估。作者提出"综合EEG理解"这一概念，并指出现有评估不足以衡量LLM在此方面的真实水平。

Method: 构建统一基准，包含四个子集（基础分析、睡眠评估、神经认知评估、生理整合），覆盖17个数据集和多种任务类型。评估框架支持数值、类别、集合、序列、语义和伪影六种输出验证。在两种执行范式（CodeAct自主代码执行和BrainAgent结构化智能体分析）下对多个代表性LLM进行超过10万次执行的大规模评估。

Result: 不同模型在不同子集、难度级别和执行范式下的表现差异显著，表明EEG能力取决于模型选择及其操作化方式。基准提供了可复现的测试平台，代码和基准数据即将发布。

Conclusion: 该基准为LLM在综合EEG理解任务中的能力提供了系统性的量化评估框架，填补了现有评估的空白，并为未来LLM-based EEG分析研究提供了可复现的测试平台。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis extends beyond assigning predefined labels to recordings; it requires workflows connecting natural-language instructions, signal processing, quantitative evidence, and scientific interpretation. We term this capability \emph{comprehensive EEG understanding}. Existing evaluations, however, primarily target isolated decoding tasks or system-specific demonstrations, leaving the competence of large language models (LLMs) insufficiently quantified. We introduce \benchmarkname{}, a unified benchmark for comprehensive, instruction-conditioned EEG understanding. It comprises four subsets---Foundational Analysis, Sleep Assessment, Neurocognitive Assessment, and Physiological Integration---covering 17 datasets, \numcases{} tasks, and over \numinstances{} real-data instances. Given an instruction and EEG recordings with optional physiological signals, a system must perform the analysis and produce a scientifically grounded report and, when required, artifacts. Outputs are assessed through numerical, categorical, set, sequence, semantic, and artifact validation. We evaluate \nummodels{} representative LLMs across more than 100K executions under two paradigms: autonomous code execution with CodeAct and structured agentic analysis with BrainAgent. Results vary substantially across models, subsets, difficulty levels, and execution paradigms, showing that EEG competence depends on the model and its operationalization. \benchmarkname{} provides a reproducible testbed for advancing LLM-based EEG understanding. The code and benchmark will be released soon, with evaluation results continuously updated.

</details>


### [199] [Adversarially Robust Abductive Fusion of Pre-trained Transformer-based Perception Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04190)
*Mario Leiva,Yue Ma,Qinru Qiu,Gerardo Simari,Paulo Shakarian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需领域知识的元认知层用于多模型检测器融合：通过利用向量空间几何（每个模型训练嵌入构建的标签向量池）生成误差检测规则，在分布偏移下达到与领域知识规则相当的精度（F1相差0.002以内）。在15个天气偏移的航拍基准和6个ViT检测器上，该方法在干净数据上与多数投票持平（F1差0.005以内），但在协调标签翻转攻击下显著优于所有多数投票基线（90%翻转率时0.42 vs 0.35 F1，相对提升22%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练感知模型在新环境中因分布偏移而精度下降，且简单的模型组合（如多数投票）无法恢复精度——这些组合器在召回和精度之间权衡，且对协调性失败很脆弱。先前的元认知方法依赖手工设计的领域知识线索（如物体尺寸先验、分割掩码），这些线索无法迁移到真正的新场景。作者希望在没有领域知识的情况下学习元认知层。

Method: 1) 标签向量池（LVP）：利用每个模型自身的训练嵌入构建标签向量池，从检测结果相对于训练决定的原型的几何关系中推导误差检测规则，完全无需领域知识。2) 神经符号框架：几何规则与领域知识规则共享同一逻辑框架，可互补使用。3) 一致性诱导的溯因问题：将多个不完美ViT检测器的融合形式化为测试时求解的溯因问题，通过精确整数规划（IP）及多项式时间启发式算法求解。

Result: 在15个天气偏移测试集和6个ViT检测器的航拍基准上：误差检测规则与领域知识规则F1差距在0.002以内；干净数据上与最强多数投票变体F1差距在0.005以内；在90%标签翻转率的协调攻击下平均F1为0.42，优于MV-Plurality的0.35（相对提升22%）；当翻转率超过0.4时，在每个测试集上都取得最高F1。

Conclusion: 元认知层无需领域知识即可通过向量空间几何学习，达到与领域知识规则相当的性能，同时保留了神经符号框架的灵活性和与领域知识互补的能力。该方法在协调攻击场景下显著优于所有多数投票基线，展现了更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deploying pre-trained perception models in novel environments degrades their accuracy under distributional shift, and assembling them alone does not recover it: combiners such as majority voting trade recall for precision and are brittle to coordinated failures. Prior metacognitive methods learn logical rules that flag a model's errors, but rely on hand-authored domain-knowledge cues (object-size priors, segmentation masks) that do not transfer to genuinely novel scenes. We show that this metacognitive layer can be learned without any domain knowledge by exploiting vector-space geometry: per-model Label Vector Pools (LVP), built from each model's own training embeddings, yield error-detection rules from the geometry of detections relative to training-determined prototypes, reaching parity with domain-knowledge rules to within $0.002$ every F1 on test set. Because the approach remains neurosymbolic, these geometric rules share a single logical framework and can still be complemented by domain knowledge when available. We frame the fusion of multiple imperfect ViT-based detectors as a consistency-based abduction problem solved at test time by an exact Integer Program (IP) and a polynomial-time heuristic. On an aerial-imagery benchmark of 15 weather-shifted test sets and six ViT detectors, our domain-knowledge-free layer matches the strongest majority-vote variant on clean data (within $0.005$ F1) and, unlike every majority-vote baseline, retains its performance under a coordinated label-flipping attack: at a $90\%$ flip rate it averages $0.42$ F1 versus $0.35$ for MV-Plurality (a $22\%$ relative gain) and attains the highest F1 on \emph{every} test set once the flip rate exceeds $0.4$

</details>


### [200] [MatrAIx: Simulating the World with 8.3 Billion Persona Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04205)
