Result: CoPlan在居家养老护理规划场景中进行了演示，系统支持自适应护理团队招募、基于角色的论证审查、最终护理方案生成，以及通过调度代理实现的实际后续跟进，展示了可争议的护理规划界面的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了一个可争议的护理规划界面，并提出了可信赖人机护理规划的设计框架，该框架保持了人类能动性和临床问责性。

Abstract: AI-supported care planning can help clinicians, patients, caregivers, and care teams coordinate complex decisions across clinical, functional, psychosocial, and environmental needs. However, many AI systems present recommendations as fixed outputs, limiting stakeholders' ability to inspect, challenge, and revise plans when they conflict with clinical judgment, patient values, or real-world feasibility. We present CoPlan - a Co-Intelligent and Contestable Interface for Human-AI Care Planning. CoPlan uses a multi-agent workflow in which specialized AI agents generate candidate interventions and supporting or challenging arguments, while human care planners can accept, reject, modify, or add arguments before final plan generation. Through this design, CoPlan combines co-intelligence, in which humans and AI agents contribute complementary expertise, with contestability, where recommendations remain open to inspection, revision, and justification. We demonstrate CoPlan in an aging-in-place care planning scenario. The system supports adaptive care team recruitment, role-based argument review, final care plan generation, and practical follow-up through scheduling agents. This work contributes a contestable care planning interface and a design framing for trustworthy human-AI care planning that preserves human agency and clinical accountability.

</details>


### [232] [OctoLong: Mid-Training On Cross-Repository Code Contexts Enhances Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05141)
*Indraneil Paul,Falko Helm,Goran Glavaš,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OctoLong，一种通过AST解析器、语言服务器后端和包管理器递归检索代码引用的上下文工程流水线，用于构建数百万token长度的依赖丰富代码上下文。基于此训练的OctoLong-Instruct系列长上下文开放语言模型（600M到14B参数），在50B token混合语料（含6.2B OctoLong代码上下文）上进行上下文扩展中训练和10B token指令微调。实验表明，仅用12%的OctoLong数据替换传统上下文扩展语料，即可在长程检索、长期状态跟踪、仓库级代码理解和下游智能体任务上取得显著提升，同时增强短上下文编码场景中的API使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文语料以书籍、学术文章和代码仓库为主，这些资源有限且往往缺乏长距离依赖关系，难以满足语言模型对长上下文能力日益增长的需求。

Method: 提出OctoLong上下文工程流水线：集成AST解析器、语言服务器后端和包管理器，递归检索代码引用以构建依赖丰富的百万token级代码上下文。训练方式为上下文扩展中训练（约50B token混合语料，含6.2B OctoLong代码上下文），随后进行约10B token的指令微调。

Result: 与18个先进的开源长上下文语言模型对比，仅用12%的OctoLong数据替换传统上下文扩展语料即可在长程检索、长期状态跟踪、仓库级代码理解和下游智能体任务上获得显著增益，同时增强短上下文编码场景中的API使用能力。

Conclusion: 依赖丰富的代码上下文是一种高效且可扩展的长上下文训练语料来源，能替代传统有限的长上下文资料，提升模型在多种长上下文任务和编码场景中的表现。

Abstract: Context lengths of language models (LMs) have dramatically increased, driven by the demands for in-context learning, self-improvement, and long-horizon agentic workflows. Existing long-context corpora, however, are dominated by books, academic articles, and code repositories, which are finite resources and often scarce in long-distance dependencies. In this work, we introduce OctoLong, a context engineering pipeline that instruments an AST parser, a language server backend, and a package manager to facilitate the recursive retrieval of code references, enabling the curation of dependency-rich code contexts of millions of tokens in length. We then train OctoLong-Instruct, a suite of capable long-context open LMs, derived from base models ranging in size from 600M to 14B parameters, via context-extension mid-training on a ~50B-token mixture containing ~6.2B tokens of OctoLong code contexts, followed by ~10B tokens of instruction tuning. Our training ablations and experimental evaluations against 18 state-of-the-art open-weight long-context LMs show that supplanting just 12% of traditional context-extension corpora with OctoLong data yields substantial gains in long-range retrieval, long-term state tracking, repository-level code understanding, and downstream agentic tasks, while also enhancing API usage in short-context coding scenarios.

</details>


### [233] [Argus: A General-Purpose Agentic Runtime for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05144)
*Boxiu Li,Zimo Wen,Yijia Fan,Junxiang Lei,Sufeng Guo,Jiaao Wu,Ruize Tang,Mukai Li,Yifei Shen,Xiaoyu Chen,Wanbo Zhang,Runjing Gu,Yifei Gao,Yuheng Wu,Xuyao Huang,Zelong Zhao,Jiachen Zhang,Shibo Hu,Hangxi Guo,Yilin Chen,Yuzhe Zhang,Fan Yang,Chuan Wen,Xian Zhang,Xuanhe Zhou,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Argus——一个持久化、自我演化的智能体运行时系统，通过Manager、Planner、Engineer和Reviewer四角色协作在持久项目状态上执行有界任务。系统分离稳定用户意图与操作目标/约束/验证标准，仅在接受角色审查和任务原生验证后吸纳记忆、技能、流程、验证器等。模型权重固定，自我演化通过持久运行时状态和控制策略实现。在SWE-Bench Pro上达到约78%（相较于Direct Copilot的59%），token使用量为1.41倍；AARRI-Bench达76.8%；数学数据综合领先28.0分。演化后成熟任务减少21%输入token和15%工作时间，并展示34次验证器恢复和22次严格审查循环救援。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野推理需要能够持续运行的智能体运行时：当证据支持当前方法时保持，当测量显示失败、隐藏约束或目标误设时转向。现有系统缺乏持久性和自适应能力，无法在长时间任务中积累经验并自我修正。

Method: Argus由Manager、Planner、Engineer和Reviewer四个角色组成，在持久项目状态上执行有界任务。系统区分稳定用户意图与操作目标/约束/验证标准，通过角色所有审查和任务原生验证来接纳记忆、技能、程序、验证器、路由决策和拒绝的路由。模型权重固定，自我演化通过持久运行时状态和控制策略实现，并在操作员拥有的升级点之间自主执行。

Result: 在七个GPT-5.5基准竞技场中：SWE-Bench Pro约78%（Direct Copilot为59%），token总量为1.41倍；验证门控自我演化后，成熟SWE-Bench任务减少21%的solve-input tokens和15%活跃工作流时间，记录34次验证器恢复和22次严格审查循环救援；AARRI-Bench达76.8%；数学数据综合领先28.0分；GPU内核和语言模型训练结果具有竞争力。真实部署中：RWKV6内核优化已合并上游、数学战役保留伪路线和证明支持的前沿更新、六条论文管线完成254个任务并执行16次阶段回滚。

Conclusion: 固定权重、自我演化的harness能够修正、恢复并积累经过验证的方法，同时为未来的监督学习和强化学习产生结构化轨迹。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning requires an agentic runtime that can persist when evidence supports its current approach and pivot when measurements reveal failure, hidden constraints, or a misspecified objective. We present Argus, a persistent, self-evolving runtime in which Manager, Planner, Engineer, and Reviewer execute bounded missions over durable project state. Argus separates stable user intent from operational objectives, constraints, and verification criteria, and admits memories, skills, procedures, verifiers, routing decisions, and rejected routes only after role-owned review and, when available, task-native verification. Model weights remain fixed; self-evolution occurs through persistent runtime state and control policy, with autonomous execution between operator-owned escalation points. Across seven GPT-5.5 benchmark arenas, Argus achieves about 78% on SWE-Bench Pro versus 59% for Direct Copilot while using 1.41 times the aggregate tokens. After verification-gated self-evolution, mature SWE-Bench waves use 21% fewer solve-input tokens and 15% less active workflow time per task than startup waves, while recording 34 verifier recoveries and 22 strict review-loop rescues. Argus also reaches 76.8% on AARRI-Bench and a 28.0-point gap on mathematical data synthesis, with competitive GPU-kernel and language-model-training results. Beyond benchmarks, an optimized RWKV6 kernel was merged upstream; a multi-day mathematics campaign retained falsified routes and proof-backed frontier updates; and six paper pipelines completed 254 missions with 16 stage rollbacks. These results show that a fixed-weight, self-evolving harness can revise, recover, and accumulate verified approaches while producing structured trajectories for future supervised and reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [234] [Similarity All The Way Up: Multilingual Generalization in LLMs Relies on Language-Level Similarity Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22699)
*Supantho Rakshit,Adele Goldberg,Henry Conklin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型（LLMs）在多语言泛化能力上的表现及其原因。作者从认知科学的视角出发，认为成功的泛化源于在相似性空间中恰当的表示。研究发现LLMs的潜在表示能够较好地恢复印欧语系家族的层级相似性结构，将同一亚系的语言在表示空间中聚在一起；此外，模型反映语言相似性结构的程度与其在XNLI多语言自然语言推理基准上的表现呈正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在多样任务中能力不断增强，其（无法）泛化的能力仍然难以量化，且在有限领域之外缺乏深入理解。特别是，LLMs在多语言情境下（即英语之外、训练数据中占比较少的语言）的泛化能力表现不佳。作者希望通过认知科学的理论来理解何使某些模型在多语言泛化上表现更好。

Method: 作者从认知科学中"成功的泛化源自于相似性空间中恰当表示"的观点出发，考察LLMs的潜在表示能否捕捉不同语言之间的层级相似性结构。具体而言，他们检验模型的表示是否恢复印欧语系家族的树状层级结构，并将模型反映语言相似性结构的程度与其在XNLI基准上的性能进行相关性分析。

Result: 研究发现：（1）LLMs的潜在表示在很大程度上恢复了印欧语系家族的层级结构，同一亚系的语言在表示空间中紧密聚集；（2）模型反映语言相似性结构的程度与其在XNLI多语言自然语言推理基准上的表现存在相关性。

Conclusion: 该研究将经典的相似性驱动泛化理论扩展到了大规模模型场景，表明那些能够将相似语言表示得相似的模型，在跨语言泛化方面表现更好。这为理解LLMs多语言泛化能力的差异提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) grow more capable across diverse tasks, their (in)ability to generalize remains difficult to quantify and poorly understood beyond limited domains. In particular, LLMs are known to struggle generalizing multilingually, to languages outside of English, and that are poorly attested in their training data. To understand why this may be, and what enables some models to perform better than others, we turn to a long history of work across the cognitive sciences, arguing that successful generalization derives from appropriate representations in similarity space. We look at how well LLMs' representations capture the hierarchical similarity structure between distinct languages. Strikingly, we show LLMs' latent representations largely recover the hierarchical structure of the Indo-European language family tree -- grouping languages that are members of the same subfamily closely together in representation space. Furthermore, we show that the degree to which models reflect the similarity structure of languages correlates with their performance on XNLI, a multilingual natural language inference benchmark. This extends classic work on similarity-driven generalization at scale, showing how models that represent similar languages similarly generalize better from one language to another.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

### [235] [Tail-Calibrated Soft-Output GRAND for Finite-Memory Noise-Effect Posteriors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04068)
*Behrooz Razeghi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出Tail-Calibrated SOGRAND算法，针对二进制加性信道中具有有限记忆能量的硬判决噪声效应后验分布，通过有限状态递归计算后验权重、尾质量和位级APP LLR，并利用随机码本占用概率p_q与环境后验尾质量T_q的乘积估计未查询的码本受限分母，实现ML解码且给出可证明的尾放弃边界和估计器统计性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相关感知GRAND方法虽然利用了局部依赖，但其停止规则和软输出规则并非从有限记忆后验尾部推导而来；SOGRAND虽能给出随机码本APP估计，但缺乏针对相关噪声效应后验的有限记忆算法来计算后验权重、配分函数、尾质量和位级尾部边量。

Method: 针对二进制加性信道，假设环境硬判决噪声效应后验（以接收软信息为条件）可由有限记忆能量表示。解码器按非递减后验能量枚举候选噪声效应，按GRAND方式查询码本成员资格，通过有限状态递推计算后验权重与尾质量，并估计未查询的码本受限分母为p_q×T_q，其中T_q为环境后验尾质量，p_q为剩余随机码本占用概率。

Result: 在精确枚举且不放弃的条件下，首个列出的码字为（定义后验能量的似然模型下的）ML解码。证明了环境后验尾放弃边界，以及随机码本缺失列表估计量的条件无偏性、方差与集中界。同一后验尾分解可给出块级APP估计、缺失列表概率和位级APP LLR。

Conclusion: Tail-Calibrated SOGRAND为有限记忆噪声效应后验提供了完整的软输出解码框架，包括ML保证、可证明的放弃边界和具有统计保证的缺失列表估计，同时支持块级和位级软信息输出。

Abstract: In guessing random additive noise decoding (GRAND), memory in the hard-decision noise effect changes the likelihood order of candidate noise effects. In soft-output decoding, the same memory also affects the finite-block quantity determining the missing-list probability: the codebook-restricted posterior mass outside the current list. Existing correlation-aware GRAND methods exploit local dependence without interleaving, but their stopping and soft-output rules are not derived from finite-memory posterior tails. Soft-output GRAND (SOGRAND) derives random-codebook a posteriori probability (APP) estimates for GRAND lists, but does not provide finite-memory algorithms for posterior weights, partition functions, tail masses, or bitwise tail marginals for correlated noise-effect posteriors. We introduce Tail-Calibrated SOGRAND for binary additive channels whose ambient hard-decision noise-effect posterior, conditioned on received soft information, is represented by a finite-memory energy. The decoder enumerates candidate noise effects in nondecreasing posterior energy, queries codebook membership as in GRAND, computes posterior weights and tail masses by finite-state recursions, and estimates the unqueried codebook-restricted denominator as $p_qT_q$, where $T_q$ is the ambient posterior tail mass and $p_q$ is the remaining random-codebook occupancy probability. With exact enumeration and no abandonment, the first listed codeword is ML under the likelihood model defining the posterior energy. We also prove an ambient posterior-tail abandonment bound and, separately, conditional unbiasedness, variance, and concentration bounds for the random-codebook missing-list estimator. The same posterior-tail decomposition gives blockwise APP estimates, missing-list probabilities, and bitwise APP log-likelihood ratios (LLRs) for finite-memory noise-effect posteriors.

</details>


### [236] [Geometry-Informed Optimization of Binary RIS Configurations for Communication and Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04133)
*Angelos Gkekas,Alexandros I. Papadopoulos,Petros Andreas Pantazopoulos,Antonios Lalas,Konstantinos Votis,Christos Liaskos*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文揭示了1-bit RIS（可重构智能表面）的指数级配置空间并非无结构。通过将1-bit RIS优化重述为带符号信道向量和的范数最大化问题，证明了最优配置必须由向量投影到公共方向上的符号决定。该几何特征化导致不同的算法结果：MIMO系统中开发了几何指导的采样方法，SISO系统中通过二维角度划分将候选集降至N+1个配置，可实现多项式时间的全局最优求解。该原理还统一应用于ISAC场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际RIS通常仅支持少量离散相位态（如1-bit），直接优化需在2^N个二进制配置中搜索，计算复杂度呈指数爆炸。作者希望揭示该配置空间的内在结构，从而避免暴力搜索，为离散RIS优化提供理论依据和高效算法。

Method: 将1-bit RIS优化重述为带符号信道向量之和的范数最大化问题，证明全局最优配置必由向量在公共方向上的投影符号所诱导。基于信号空间维度分别处理：MIMO系统中利用几何结构限制候选配置并设计采样方法；SISO系统中利用二维角度划分完整刻画候选集，通过多项式时间枚举（至多N+1个配置）恢复全局最优；并将同一二元优化原理应用到ISAC场景。

Result: SISO系统中，全部2^N个配置中至多N+1个需要评估即可恢复全局最优；MIMO系统中仅需评估结构上可容许的配置。该几何框架统一了通信增强与目标定位两类功能下的1-bit RIS优化问题。

Conclusion: 1-bit RIS的配置空间具有可被几何结构表征的内在规律，该发现为通信和感知场景提供了统一的高效优化框架，大幅减少了所需评估的配置数量。

Abstract: Practical Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (RISs) often support only a small number of phase states, making their configuration inherently discrete. For a 1-bit RIS with $N$ elements, direct optimization requires searching among $2^N$ binary configurations. This work shows that this exponential configuration space is not unstructured. By reformulating 1-bit RIS optimization as the maximization of the norm of a signed sum of channel-dependent vectors, we prove that every globally optimal configuration must be induced by the signs of their projections onto a common direction. This geometric characterization restricts the class of configurations that can contain global optima and leads to different algorithmic consequences depending on the signal-space dimension. For general Multiple-Input-Multiple-Output (MIMO) systems, we develop a geometry-informed sampling method that evaluates only structurally admissible configurations. For Single-Input-Single-Output (SISO) systems, the same principle reduces to a two-dimensional angular partition, allowing the complete candidate set to be characterized and the global optimum to be recovered through polynomial-time enumeration, by evaluating at most $N+1$ out of the $2^N$ configurations. Finally, we apply the same binary optimization principle to an Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) scenario, where communication enhancement and target localization reduce to the same underlying geometric problem. The proposed framework therefore provides a unified approach for exploiting the structure of practical 1-bit RIS configurations across communication and sensing functionalities.

</details>


### [237] [Dimension Rigidity and Projective Geometry of Trace-Product Switchings of the Gold Cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04261)
*Oleksandr Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了Gold几乎完美非线性（APN）函数 $x\mapsto x^3$ 在偶数维上的自然标量迹积切换（scalar trace-product switching）。非平凡切换仅出现在 $n=4,6,8$ 维，对应的可容许系数分别为非零迹零元素、乘法阶为9的六个元素和 $\mathbb{F}_4^{*}$；对所有 $n\geq10$ 的偶数维，无非零系数可容许。此外，作者在8维中分类了由 $\mathbb{P}^{1}(\mathbb{F}_4)$ 规范化的秩二扩展，并给出了全局APN类在其Gold中心附近的局部组织结构的完整描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究APN函数的切换（switching）机制旨在揭示有限域上向量布尔函数的结构性质，并通过在已知Gold APN函数附近寻找等价或新的APN类，进而探索APN函数的全局分类与维度刚性（dimension-rigidity）。此前Arshad的博士论文给出了 $n=6,8$ 维的原始系数列表，但缺乏内在描述和统一证明，本文旨在补全这一理论空白。

Method: 作者采用加法特征（additive-character）估计来排除无限多偶数维（$n\geq10$）的非平凡切换，并对 $n=10,12$ 进行精确的有限"桥"验证。对于 $n=8$ 的秩二扩展，利用 $\mathbb{P}^{1}(\mathbb{F}_4)$ 归一化和二进迹选择器（binary trace selector）在每个非基点接受两个系数值，得到8个被接受的标记切换；再通过一个与中心无关的低秩导数判据，将每个秩 $r$ 候选者归约为 $2^r-1$ 次预计算的禁忌集合成员判定。

Result: 非平凡切换仅在 $n=4,6,8$ 时存在：$n=4$ 的非零迹零元素、$n=6$ 的乘法阶为9的六个元素、$n=8$ 的 $\mathbb{F}_4^{*}$。所有 $n\geq10$ 的偶数维无切换。8维中的8个被接受的标记切换恰好形成两个扩展仿射（extended-affine）类，即两个CCZ类；全局APN类为已知，结果刻画了其在Gold中心附近的局部组织。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了Gold三次APN函数在偶数维上迹积切换的完全分类，给出了统一的维度刚性定理，排除了所有 $n\geq10$ 偶数维上该切换机制的可能性，并精确刻画了低维情形下所得APN类的局部结构。

Abstract: We completely classify a natural scalar trace-product switching of the Gold almost perfect nonlinear function $x\mapsto x^3$ in every even dimension. Nontrivial switchings occur only for $n=4,6,8$: the admissible coefficients are, respectively, the nonzero trace-zero elements, the six elements of multiplicative order nine, and $\mathbb{F}_4^{*}$. For every even $n\geq10$, no nonzero coefficient is admissible. The infinite range is excluded by additive-character estimates on a Fermat cubic, with exact finite bridges for $n=10,12$. The raw coefficient lists for $n=6,8$ appeared earlier in Arshad's dissertation; our contribution is their intrinsic description, a proof uniform in the dimension, and the resulting dimension-rigidity theorem. We also classify normalized rank-two extensions in dimension eight by $\mathbb{P}^{1}(\mathbb{F}_4)$. A binary trace selector accepts two coefficient values at each non-base projective point, and the eight accepted marked switchings form exactly two extended-affine, hence two CCZ, classes. A centre-independent low-rank derivative criterion reduces each rank-$r$ candidate to $2^r-1$ membership tests in precomputed forbidden sets. The global APN classes reached are known; the results describe their local organization around the Gold centre and rule out this switching mechanism in all larger even dimensions.

</details>


### [238] [Bernoulli--Strang--Fix Conditions: Approximation and Prediction by Sampling Kantorovich Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04727)
*Sreya T,A. Antony Selvan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了向量值生成函数与周期非均匀采样集下的Bernoulli–Strang–Fix条件及其广义版本，用于建立采样Kantorovich算子的精确和渐近多项式再现性质，并证明该算子可有效用于信号预测，最后通过高斯函数和B样条的数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Bernoulli–Strang–Fix条件适用于标量生成函数与均匀采样，而实际应用中采样点往往是非均匀周期性的，生成函数也可能具有向量值结构。作者希望将这一理论框架推广到更一般的设定，从而为采样Kantorovich算子的多项式复现、逼近性质乃至信号预测提供更广泛的理论基础。

Method: 首先定义向量值生成函数φ和周期非均匀采样集X下的Bernoulli–Strang–Fix条件及其广义形式；利用这些条件推导采样Kantorovich算子关于给定阶数的精确与渐近多项式再现性质；进而分析算子的逼近性质与收敛行为；最后将算子应用于从有限个过去局部平均采样中进行信号预测，并通过高斯函数和B样条进行数值验证。

Result: 建立了在向量值生成函数和周期非均匀采样集下采样Kantorovich算子的多项式再现及近似性质的理论结果；证明了该算子不仅能用于逼近，还能作为有效的预测算子，在有限历史局部平均采样的条件下实现信号预测；数值实验验证了理论结论的有效性。

Conclusion: 作者成功将Bernoulli–Strang–Fix条件推广至向量值生成函数与周期非均匀采样框架，既完善了采样Kantorovich算子的逼近理论，又开辟了其在信号预测中的新应用方向，理论与数值结果相互印证。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the Bernoulli--Strang--Fix conditions and their generalized versions for a vector-valued generator $\varphi=(\varphi_0,\dots,\varphi_{ρ-1})$ and a periodic nonuniform sampling set $X$. We use these conditions to establish exact and asymptotic polynomial reproduction properties of sampling Kantorovich operators associated with $(\varphi,X)$ up to a prescribed degree. We analyze the approximation properties and convergence behavior of these operators in detail. Furthermore, we demonstrate their application to signal prediction from a finite number of past local average samples, showing that sampling Kantorovich operators can also serve as effective prediction operators. Finally, we present numerical examples based on Gaussian functions and B-splines to illustrate and validate the theoretical approximation and prediction results.

</details>


### [239] [Minimal Binary Linear Codes of Dimension n+4 from Partial Spreads and Their Dual Access Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04889)
*Apurba Sarkar,Kalyan Hansda,Makhan Maji*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于特殊布尔函数类的最小二进制线性码的通用构造方法，实现维度为n+4的新码族，利用有限域中部分扩散的几何性质确定其权重分布和权重枚举器，并给出最小性判定的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小线性码在秘密共享方案、安全多方计算和密码学中具有重要应用，构造新的最小码族并突破现有理论限制（如Ashikhmin-Barg条件）对先进通信系统具有重要意义。

Method: 采用通用构造方法，从特殊布尔函数类出发构建维度为n+4的二进制线性码；利用有限域中部分扩散（partial spreads）的几何性质推导显式权重分布；系统性地推导码最小性的充要条件。

Result: 确定了所构造码的显式权重分布和权重枚举器；推导了这些码为最小码的充要条件；证明该码族产生的最小码在结构上违反Ashikhmin-Barg条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的构造方案产生了一类明确权重分布、结构上违反Ashikhmin-Barg条件的最小二进制线性码，对先进密码学和通信系统具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Minimal linear codes have significant applications in secret sharing schemes, secure multi-party computation, and cryptography. In this paper, we propose a generic construction of a new family of minimal binary linear codes with dimension n+4 from a special class of Boolean functions. By leveraging the geometric properties of partial spreads in finite fields, we determine the explicit weight distribution and weight enumerator of the constructed codes. Furthermore, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for these codes to be minimal, and establish that the proposed family yields minimal codes that structurally violate the well-known Ashikhmin-Barg condition, making them highly desirable for advanced communication systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出C²MOE框架，通过一致性与互补性引导的专家混合机制，解决多模态情感识别中模态缺失导致的性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别（MERC）依赖完整的多模态输入，但实际场景中常因传输错误或用户行为导致模态缺失，严重损害模型性能。现有方法通过跨模态一致性学习增强鲁棒性，但忽略了模态互补性，导致重建结果存在偏差。

Method: C²MOE将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性两个组成部分：一致性通过最大化跨模态可预测性捕获，互补性通过最大化模态间条件熵保留。在此基础上，设计双分支预测机制：一致性分支通过最小化不确定性对齐联合分布，互补性分支通过熵最大化利用模态特有线索。最后采用可学习的重加权模块动态分配各专家输出的重要性权重，实现自适应融合。

Result: 在多个MERC基准数据集上的大量实验表明，C²MOE在各种缺失模态设置下均持续超越现有最优方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 同时考虑模态一致性与互补性对于不完整多模态情感学习至关重要，基于信息论框架的分解与动态加权融合策略能够有效提升模态缺失场景下的情感识别性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [241] [Unscented KalmanNet: a hybrid deep learning filter with calibrated posterior covariance for nonlinear state estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04201)
*Minhyeok Ko,Abdollah Shafieezadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unscented KalmanNet (UKN)，一种混合递归估计器，在保留UKF显式sigma点协方差递推的基础上，通过两个学习组件（NoiseNet和GainNet）分别预测有界乘性噪声协方差修正和有界增益残差修正，并采用校准感知的训练目标同时优化估计精度与协方差校准。在三个合成系统和UZH-FPV真实飞行数据上，UKN在状态估计误差上均取得最优表现，相比UKF在合成案例中RMSE降低26.4%-49.7%，在11架次飞行交叉验证中位置和速度RMSE分别降低22.4%和34.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统UKF在未知、时变噪声统计和模型失配条件下，估计精度和协方差校准会严重退化。现有学习型滤波器大多基于扩展卡尔曼滤波器构建，要么舍弃显式协方差输出，要么学习不确定性但无法纠正由模型失配引起的增益偏差。因此需要一个既能保持UKF显式协方差递推结构，又能通过学习组件同时提升精度并纠正增益偏差的混合滤波器。

Method: UKN在UKF框架上引入两个结构不同的学习组件：(1) NoiseNet预测时变的过程和测量协方差，作为固定基线的有界乘性修正，并保证正定性；(2) GainNet对解析卡尔曼增益施加有界残差修正。训练目标采用校准感知的损失函数，结合状态误差与协方差一致性、新息一致性项，并通过自适应权重联合优化精度与校准。

Result: 在三个合成系统和UZH-FPV真实飞行数据上，UKN在全部四个示例中取得最低的聚合状态估计误差；合成案例中相比UKF的RMSE降低26.4%-49.7%；11架次留一序列交叉验证显示位置和速度RMSE分别降低22.4%和34.3%；同时UKN的折间变异性最低，归一化NEES和经验覆盖率最接近标称值。

Conclusion: 通过在UKF中嵌入NoiseNet和GainNet两个有界学习组件，并配合校准感知的训练目标，UKN在保持显式协方差递推的同时显著提升了非线性状态估计的精度和协方差校准能力，在合成与真实数据上均优于UKF、KalmanNet和Bayesian KalmanNet基线方法。

Abstract: State estimation for nonlinear dynamical systems is commonly performed with the Unscented Kalman filter (UKF), which propagates the state moments through deterministic sigma points and reports a posterior covariance at every step. In practice, however, unknown and time-varying noise statistics and model mismatch degrade both estimation accuracy and covariance calibration. Existing learned filters improve accuracy but are largely built on the extended Kalman filter and either forgo an explicit covariance or learn uncertainty without correcting mismatch-induced gain bias. This paper introduces the Unscented KalmanNet (UKN), a hybrid recursive estimator that augments the UKF with two structurally distinct learned components while preserving its explicit sigma-point covariance recursion. NoiseNet predicts time-varying process and measurement covariances as bounded multiplicative corrections to fixed baselines, guaranteeing positive definiteness, while GainNet applies a bounded residual correction to the analytical gain. A calibration-aware training objective combines state error with covariance- and innovation-consistency terms through adaptive weights, jointly optimizing accuracy and calibration. UKN is benchmarked against UKF, KalmanNet, and Bayesian KalmanNet on three synthetic systems and UZH-FPV real-flight data. It achieves the lowest aggregate state-estimation error in all four examples and reduces RMSE by $26.4$-$49.7\%$ compared to UKF in the synthetic cases. Leave-one-sequence-out cross-validation over 11 flights shows $22.4\%$ and $34.3\%$ reductions in mean position and velocity RMSE, respectively. UKN also yields the lowest fold-to-fold variability, with normalized NEES and empirical coverage closest to nominal values among the other filters.

</details>


### [242] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对次优势(极小极大)超度量算子的稳定性问题，发展了一套ℓ₀型稳定性理论，揭示了稀疏编辑通过最小生成树传播的机制，给出每编辑暴露割分数与树状全局包络，证明了Hamming-Lipschitz界及其最优性，并在深度学习嵌入图上验证了其作为层级表示脆弱性诊断工具的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典超度量稳定性理论基于ℓ∞或Gromov-Hausdorff度量，无法刻画稀疏扰动（仅改变少数几对距离）对超度量结果的影响。需要一种更适合稀疏扰动的稳定性理论框架，以理解单链接聚类对个别噪声的鲁棒性边界。

Method: 发展ℓ₀型稳定性分析框架：证明稀疏编辑仅沿最小生成树传播；引入每编辑暴露割分数指标和树状全局包络；推导Hamming-Lipschitz界限用于界定可改变的超度量条目数量；同时给出严格割分离下树边界的精确可达性证明，以及离树编辑的Θ(n²)超度量条目改变族；提出条件近可加性原理处理多重编辑。

Result: 证明了稀疏编辑仅通过最小生成树传播；给出尖锐的每编辑暴露割分数和树状全局包络；获得Hamming-Lipschitz界；严格割分离下树边界的精确可达性得到证明；构造了单次离树编辑影响Θ(n²)条目的一族显式实例；提出了多重编辑的条件近可加性原理；深度学习嵌入图上的实验验证了结构分数作为层级表示脆弱性诊断的有效性。

Conclusion: ℓ₀型稳定性理论为次优势超度量算子提供了比经典ℓ∞视角更精确的稀疏扰动分析工具，其树几何依赖性本质上是不可避免的。该框架可应用于层级表示（如深度学习嵌入）的脆弱性诊断与鲁棒性评估。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>


### [243] [Physics-informed reduced-order modelling with equivariant spectral submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04239)
*Georg Maierhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等变谱子流形（eSSM）约简方法，利用全阶模型的对称性来加速SSM计算，在保持模型鲁棒性的同时大幅提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱子流形（SSM）约简是构建非线性降阶模型的有效数学方法，能捕捉超出线性技术（如DMD）能力范围的动力学。然而，SSM的计算尤其对高维系统而言代价高昂，亟需一种能够加速计算的方案。

Method: 作者首先从数学上证明SSM本质上是等变子流形，其相关图表和约简动力学继承了适当的诱导群作用；在此基础上开发了等变SSM约简算法，将全阶模型的对称性显式纳入约简过程。

Result: 在多个基准问题（包括科学通用任务框架中的测试）上验证了该方法，结果表明相比传统SSM方法，eSSM实现了显著更快的计算速度，同时提高了模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 等变SSM约简是一种有效利用系统对称性加速非线性降阶建模的新框架，兼具计算效率与模型鲁棒性优势，为高维动力系统的SSM计算提供了实用且原理完备的路径。

Abstract: Spectral submanifold (SSM) reduction has emerged as a mathematically principled route to reliable nonlinear reduced-order models, capturing dynamics beyond the reach of linear techniques such as Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD). The computation of SSMs, however, remains computationally expensive, particularly for high-dimensional systems. In this work, we introduce equivariant spectral submanifold (eSSM) reduction, a novel extension of the SSM framework that explicitly incorporates symmetries of the full-order model into the reduction process. We establish the mathematical foundations of this approach by showing that SSMs are naturally equivariant submanifolds and that the associated charts and reduced dynamics inherit the appropriate induced group actions. Building on this framework, we develop a novel equivariant SSM reduction algorithm that exploits these symmetries to achieve substantially faster computations while also improving model robustness. We demonstrate the advantages of this approach on several benchmark problems including a test from the Common Task Framework for Science.

</details>


### [244] [A Trust-region Framework for Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04026)
*Oluwasegun A. Somefun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个信任区域（trust-region）框架来理解和分析自适应矩估计优化机制（如Adam），该框架将每个权重的更新步长限制在由p阶矩约束（p∈[2,4]）控制的信任区域内。由此推导出一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制，其中p=4时涉及峰度类估计。该通用机制（Gmake）在统一框架下诠释了矩估计归一化、学习率调度、动量作为谱低通滤波以及算子级谱归一化。在GPT2-124M上的实验表明，当信任区域约束较弱时四阶矩实现收益最大，而随着约束增强，二阶矩实现变得更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩估计方法在深度学习中被广泛使用，但其内部工作机理和设计选择（如使用二阶矩而非更高阶矩）尚缺乏统一的理论解释。作者希望构建一个理论框架，从信任区域优化的角度统一理解各类归一化机制，并探索更高阶矩估计（如四阶矩/峰度）是否以及在何种条件下能带来收益。

Method: 提出信任区域框架：将每个权重的更新步长约束在一个由p阶矩约束（p∈[2,4]）所控制的信任区域内。基于该推导，得到一族学习率机制，结合二阶矩估计与归一化p阶矩估计。当p=4时涉及峰度类似估计，形成了Gmake通用框架。该框架将矩估计归一化、学习率调度、动量（谱低通滤波）和算子级谱归一化统一到一个共同框架中。实验使用GPT2-124M在FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories数据集上训练验证。

Result: 实验表明，四阶矩（p=4）实现在信任区域约束较弱时提供最大收益；当引入逐步增强的信任区域控制后，二阶矩（p=2）实现变得更具竞争力，往往达到略低于对应四阶矩实现的验证损失。

Conclusion: 该信任区域框架为理解自适应矩估计优化器提供了统一视角。结果表明，高阶矩（如四阶/峰度）的优势依赖于信任区域约束的强度——在弱约束下高阶矩更优，在强约束下二阶矩充分。这为选择不同阶数的矩估计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation. The general mechanism, referred to as \textsc{Gmake}, provides a unified interpretation of normalization by moment estimation, learning-rate scheduling, spectral lowpass filtering as momentum, and operator-level spectral normalization within a common trust-region framework. Experiments on GPT2-124M trained on FineWeb-Edu and TinyStories suggest that the fourth-moment realization provides its greatest benefit when trust-region constraints are weak. As progressively stronger trust-region controls are introduced, the second-moment realization becomes increasingly competitive, often achieving slightly lower validation loss than its corresponding fourth-moment realization.

</details>


### [245] [Learning to Resolve Neutron Resonances with Fully Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04027)
*Nataly R. Panczyk,Athanasios Stamatopoulos,Josef Svoboda,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨用全卷积神经网络（FCN）自动检测透射谱中的中子共振，以增强传统R-Matrix代码。模型在7个透射谱（2个评估谱+5个实验谱）上达到93%分类准确率，但进一步分析表明该指标高估了泛化能力，模型无法可靠泛化到未见过的同位素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R-Matrix代码在拟合中子透射数据时依赖先验评估并需要大量人工干预，而透射数据复杂且噪声大，传统峰识别方法难以处理，因此需要自动化方法来加速后处理过程并减少人为偏差。

Method: 采用全卷积神经网络（FCN）对透射谱中的每个点进行分类，区分其属于共振区或非共振区。训练数据包括2个评估谱和5个实验谱共7个透射谱，并在先前PHYSOR 2026分析基础上增加了额外的训练数据。

Result: 模型在训练数据上达到约93%的分类准确率，但进一步分析表明该指标夸大了模型的泛化能力。尽管加入了更多训练数据，模型仍不能可靠地泛化到此前未见过的同位素。

Conclusion: 当前方法泛化能力有限。未来工作应：（1）评估更大、更多样化的训练数据集是否能产生可泛化的模型；（2）将中子共振的已知物理特征融入模型以改善性能。

Abstract: This work investigates the feasibility of augmenting traditional R-Matrix codes with a robust machine learning framework for automatically detecting neutron resonances in transmission spectra. Neutron transmission data are often complex and noisy, making them difficult to analyze using traditional peak-identification methods. The state-of-the-art R-Matrix codes currently used by physicists to fit these data often depend on prior evaluations and require substantial manual effort. This preliminary study demonstrates a method for accelerating the post-experimental processing of neutron transmission data and reducing bias associated with dependence on prior evaluations. We employ a fully convolutional neural network to classify individual points as belonging to resonance or non-resonance regions in seven transmission spectra---two evaluated and five experimental. Although the model achieves classification accuracies in the range of 93\%, further analysis shows that this metric overstates its ability to generalize. Building on our prior analysis in PHYSOR 2026, we find that, despite the inclusion of additional training data, the method does not generalize reliably to previously unseen isotopes. To address these limitations, future work should evaluate whether a larger and more diverse training dataset can produce a generalizable model and should incorporate known physical characteristics of neutron resonances to improve model performance.

</details>


### [246] [Lindblad-Inspired Multi-Timescale Reservoir Computing with Separable Rotation and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04028)
*Jyotiranjan Beuria,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受Lindblad方程启发的多时间尺度回声状态网络（Echo-State Network），将循环动力系统分解为精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态，使旋转（相位混合）与衰减（记忆遗忘）成为独立的设计变量，并提供直接的全局稳定性保证，无需事后谱半径调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回声状态网络的储备池通常在单一随机循环矩阵中同时承担信号混合、记忆保持和稳定性控制，这些目标相互冲突。现有的结构化设计虽改进了拓扑、范数保持、泄漏或深度，但缺乏对可逆混合与不可逆遗忘的独立模态控制，也缺乏直接的全局稳定性保证。作者希望将开放系统动力学原理与结构化状态空间建模相结合，解决这一局限。

Method: 提出Lindblad启发的多时间尺度储备池：循环算子由精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态（damped rotational modes）组装而成。旋转控制相位混合，衰减控制记忆损失，二者为独立设计变量。正交模态混合保持正态性，衰减谱直接决定回声状态稳定性裕度，无需事后谱半径缩放。方法涵盖旋转、泄漏、深度、正交、循环等多种结构设计，并在十个对齐的随机种子上与标准、泄漏、深度、正交、循环、下一代储备池及压缩训练的门控循环单元进行比较。

Result: 在基准测试中，所提方法在有界NARMA-20上取得最佳固定储备池性能，在Lorenz-63上取得最低平均误差，在线性记忆任务上与最强结果持平，并在其余多项基准上保持广泛竞争力。消融研究表明旋转增加状态多样性，耗散提供受控遗忘并改善预测条件数。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可解释的循环架构，其中信号混合、记忆保持和稳定性是明确且可独立调节的设计变量，架起了开放系统动力学与结构化状态空间建模之间的桥梁。

Abstract: Echo-state networks enable efficient temporal learning by fixing the recurrent dynamics and training only a linear readout. However, conventional reservoirs typically accommodate signal mixing, memory retention, and stability within a single random recurrent matrix. Existing structured designs improve topology, norm preservation, leakage, or depth, but generally do not provide separate modal control of reversible mixing and irreversible forgetting together with a direct global stability guarantee. We introduce a classical Lindblad-inspired multi-timescale reservoir that bridges open-system dynamical principles with structured state-space modeling. The recurrent operator is assembled from exactly discretized damped rotational modes, so rotation and decay become independent design variables governing phase mixing and memory loss. Orthogonal mode mixing preserves normality, while the decay spectrum directly determines the echo-state stability margin without post-hoc spectral-radius rescaling. We evaluate the method over ten aligned seeds against standard, leaky, deep, orthogonal, cycle, and next-generation reservoirs, together with a compact trained gated recurrent unit, across linear memory, nonlinear recurrence, chaotic forecasting, delayed logic, and real sensor calibration. Across the benchmark suite, the proposed reservoir achieves the best fixed-reservoir performance on bounded NARMA-20 and the lowest mean error on Lorenz-63, matches the strongest linear-memory result, and remains broadly competitive across broad range of benchmarks. Ablation studies show that rotation increases state diversity, whereas dissipation provides controlled forgetting and improves predictive conditioning. The resulting framework offers an interpretable recurrent architecture in which mixing, memory, and stability are explicit and independently tunable design variables.

</details>


### [247] [DASyR-LLM: Domain-Aware Symbolic Regression with LLMs for Kinetic Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05120)
*Roberto Aliaga Medina,Paulina Quintanilla,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的符号回归（SR）框架，用于自动化化学动力学模型发现。该框架将LLM嵌入迭代SR算法中，LLM在每个迭代中扮演两个角色：对最佳SR候选模型进行定性物理化学批判，以及基于SR生成模型和化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。在四个复杂度递增的模拟案例（涵盖多相催化和生物过程系统）中，该框架相比最先进的SR框架减少了41.7%-79.3%的迭代次数，且LLM在超过一半的引导运行中直接提出了正确的模型结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学工程中的动力学模型发现是一个核心挑战，准确的速率表达式对理解和控制化学及生物过程至关重要。符号回归（SR）作为数据驱动方法虽能识别可解释的动力学模型，但通常不利用领域知识，常探索物理化学上不合理的模型。大语言模型（LLM）提供了将领域专业知识注入搜索过程的可行途径，因此作者开发了LLM引导的SR框架以实现自动化、领域感知的动力学模型发现。

Method: 作者提出LLM-guided符号回归框架，将LLM模块嵌入迭代SR算法中。每轮迭代中LLM执行两个角色：(1)对最佳SR候选模型进行定性物理化学批判，评估其物理化学合理性；(2)基于SR生成的模型和嵌入的化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。该框架在四个复杂度递增的模拟案例上进行评估，涵盖多相催化和生物过程系统，并与最先进的SR框架进行对比。

Result: LLM引导框架相比最先进的SR框架减少了41.7%-79.3%的迭代次数来识别真实模型，且LLM在超过一半的引导运行中直接提出了正确的模型结构。在实际应用中每次迭代对应一次湿实验，这大幅减少了实验工作量。两种方法在独立验证集上的预测性能相当，所有案例R²>0.98。消融实验表明SR组件和LLM规模都对性能有贡献，较小规模的LLM仍能大部分保持发现效率。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM能够有效地将领域知识注入科学模型发现过程，显著提高了动力学模型发现的效率和物理化学合理性，为构建全自动化、领域感知的动力学建模流水线奠定了基础。

Abstract: Kinetic model discovery is a central challenge in chemical engineering, as accurate rate expressions are essential for understanding and controlling chemical and biological processes. Symbolic regression (SR) has emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for identifying interpretable kinetic models, but usually operates without domain knowledge, often exploring physicochemically implausible models. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for injecting domain expertise into this search. Here, we introduce an LLM-guided SR framework, embedding an LLM module within an iterative SR algorithm for automated kinetic model discovery. The LLM performs two roles at each iteration: (1) a qualitative physicochemical critique of the best SR candidates, and (2) the proposal of new candidate rate expressions guided by the SR-generated models and embedded chemical knowledge. Our framework is evaluated on four in silico case studies of increasing complexity, spanning heterogeneous catalysis and bioprocess systems. Results show the LLM-guided framework reduces iterations to identify the ground-truth model by $41.7-79.3\%$ versus a state-of-the-art SR framework, with the LLM directly proposing the correct model structure in over half of the guided runs. In practical settings, where each iteration typically requires a new wet-lab experiment, this translates into a substantial reduction in experimental effort. Predictive performance on an independent validation set is equivalent between both approaches, with $R^2>0.98$ in all case studies. Ablation studies indicate that both the SR component and the LLM scale contribute to this performance, with a reduced-size LLM largely retaining discovery efficiency. These findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively inject domain knowledge into scientific model discovery, paving the way toward fully automated, domain-aware kinetic modelling pipelines.

</details>


### [248] [An Explainable LLM Agent Layer for Open-World Anomaly Detection in Oil Wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04041)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,Thales Miranda de Almeida Vieira,Eduardo Toledo de Lima Junior,William Wagner Matos Lira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在OWL（开放世界学习）油井异常检测管线下游部署了一个大型语言模型（LLM）智能体层，作为已发布的感知方法的补充而非替代。该智能体接收结构化传感器指标和上游分类/新颖性断言，输出自然语言理由、置信度排序的评判以及合并后的新颖性命名。在3W数据集的989个井段上，该智能体在九类分类中达到35.1% top-1/63.9% top-3，七类验证中71.7% top-2（准确率0.91），新颖性检测89.7%，并在7个隐藏类中的5个上实现了稳定的簇命名。目的是弥合OWL管线在实际部署中面临的解释性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OWL油井异常检测管线能够回答"发生了什么"（what happened），但无法解释"模型为什么这样认为"（why）或"操作员下一步该做什么"（how/what next），且无法为新发现的新颖性聚类提供人类可读的名称。这种解释性差距阻碍了OWL管线在运营场景中的实际部署。

Method: 将LLM智能体层置于OWL管线下游，采用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts模型通过NVIDIA NIM服务。智能体接收结构化传感器指标和上游分类或新颖性断言，执行四项核心功能：(1)证据支持时确认上游决策；(2)用传感器可审计的自然语言论证决策；(3)上游标签不合理时标记分歧；(4)为新颖性簇生成合并的人类可读名称。

Result: 基于3W数据集989个真实井段的三个研究：九类分类top-1为35.1%、top-3为63.9%（95%CI [56.9,70.4]）；七类探测验证top-2为71.7% [64.8,77.6]，精确率0.91 [0.84,0.95]；新颖性检测89.7% [87.0,91.9]，7个隐藏类中5个实现了稳定簇命名。

Conclusion: 该LLM智能体并非独立的分类器，而是一个"同伴"（companion）角色：确认上游决策、提供可审计的传感器依据、标记不合理标签、命名新颖性簇。其最终目标是弥合OWL管线在运营部署中阻碍落地的解释性差距。

Abstract: Open-World Learning (OWL) pipelines for oil well anomaly detection have recently been shown to combine autoencoder-based detection, multiclass classification, and Mahalanobis-based novelty detection on the public 3W dataset. These pipelines answer \textit{what happened}, but they do not explain \textit{why the model believes it} or \textit{what the operator should do next}, and they do not put a human-readable name on the novelty clusters they discover. This paper evaluates a Large Language Model (LLM) agent layer placed downstream of the OWL pipeline, designed as a \textbf{companion} to the published upstream methods rather than a replacement. Using the Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts model served via NVIDIA NIM, the agent receives structured sensor metrics and upstream classification or novelty assertions, and returns natural-language justifications, confidence-ranked critiques, and consolidated names for detected novelties. Across three studies spanning 989 real well-file segments from the 3W dataset, the agent achieved $35.1\%$ top-1 / $63.9\%$ top-3 (95\% CI [56.9, 70.4]) classification on all nine classes, $71.7\%$ top-2 validation [64.8, 77.6] with precision $0.91$ [0.84, 0.95] across 7 probed classes, and $89.7\%$ novelty detection [87.0, 91.9] with stable cluster naming on 5 of 7 hidden classes. The agent is not a standalone classifier. Its role is to: (1) confirm upstream decisions when sensor evidence supports them, (2) justify decisions in sensor-grounded language operators can audit, (3) flag disagreement when upstream labels are implausible, and (4) name novelties so that clustered unlabeled events arrive at the engineer with a consolidated human-readable label. The goal is to close the explainability gap that currently blocks deployment of OWL pipelines in operational settings.

</details>


### [249] [Spend Bits Where Queries Look: KV Cache Vector Quantization with Attention-Preserving Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04074)
*Samuel Fernández-Menduiña,Amir Ziashahabi,Eduardo Pavez,Antonio Ortega,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出NOVA-KV方法，将KV缓存量化建模为变换编码问题，从校准统计中推导出闭式最优变换，并利用等体积分组实现固定位宽的向量量化，在每元素2比特下大幅恢复标量量化方法在长上下文检索任务中损失的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码每一步都需读取KV缓存，加载缓存的时间超过计算注意力本身，导致吞吐率受限于内存带宽。缩小缓存尺寸可同时提升解码速度和推理吞吐。现有2比特量化方法虽依赖正交变换，但要么是数据无关的，要么未从失真准则推导变换；且多基于随机或Hadamard旋转（只均衡方差而非压缩能量）和固定宽度标量量化器（低码率下次优）。

Method: 将KV缓存量化形式化为变换编码问题，失真定义为注意力乘积的误差。在高分辨率模型假设下，从校准统计量推导键和值的闭式最优变换。关键发现是最优键变换非正交，且满足广义Parseval关系：注意力感知失真等价于变换域的MSE。因此可直接在变换后的键系数上使用MSE最优的向量量化器。为满足固定宽度布局，将系数分组为等体积分区，使等大小码本在高分辨率模型下达到变速率最优。该方法命名为NOVA-KV。

Result: 在每元素2比特的设置下，NOVA-KV在可比吞吐率下恢复了标量量化方法在长上下文检索准确性中丢失的大部分精度。

Conclusion: 通过从注意力感知失真准则出发推导最优变换，并利用等体积分组的向量量化方案，NOVA-KV在固定每token比特数的约束下实现了更好的KV缓存量化性能，优于依赖正交旋转和标量量化的现有方法。

Abstract: Long-context LLM decoding reads the key-value (KV) cache at every step. Loading it takes longer than computing attention over it, so throughput is bandwidth-bound. Hence, reducing the cache size can raise both decoding speed and serving capacity. The challenge is to reduce cache size while preserving the attention products, keeping reconstruction cheap, and using a fixed per-token bit count. At two bits per element, the most competitive methods rely on orthogonal transforms. However, existing techniques are either data-oblivious or use the query statistics without deriving the transform from a distortion criterion. Moreover, they rely on transforms built on top of random or Hadamard rotations, which equalize variances across entries rather than compacting energy, and fixed-width scalar quantizers, which are suboptimal at low rates. In this paper, we formulate KV cache quantization as a transform coding problem in which distortion is the error in the attention products. We derive closed-form optimal transforms for keys and values from calibration statistics, under a high-resolution model. We show that the optimal key transform is not orthogonal and satisfies a generalized Parseval relation: the attention-aware distortion becomes mean-squared error (MSE) in the transform domain. Thus, we can use MSE-optimal vector quantizers applied directly to the transformed key coefficients. To meet the fixed-width layout requirement, we show that grouping coefficients into equal-volume partitions makes equal-size codebooks attain the variable-rate optimum under the same high-resolution model. At two bits per element, our method, termed NOVA-KV, recovers most of the long-context retrieval accuracy lost by scalar quantization methods at comparable throughput.

</details>


### [250] [Tactus: Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition from Low-Cost Pressure Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04043)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tactus是一个基于电阻式压力阵列触觉传感器构建的开源模型，仅凭压力数据即可回答文本查询。在STAG基准上达到0.771±0.062 top-1准确率，超过监督式CNN（0.76），且无需训练分类头。核心配方是小数据：187条训练记录、144k无标注帧的掩码自编码器预训练、以及传感器自身校准仿射变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电阻式压力阵列是最廉价、出货量最大的触觉传感器，但现有触觉表征学习研究几乎全部集中在成像式光学传感器（如凝胶变形成像）上。作者希望通过纯压力数据实现文本查询应答，填补该低成本传感器在触觉表征学习方面的空白。

Method: 使用掩码自编码器（MAE）在144k无标签同传感器帧上进行预训练，结合传感器自身的校准仿射变换，仅用187条训练记录即可在STAG基准（27个物体、留出录音）上匹配甚至超过监督式闭合集CNN的性能。模型无训练分类头，直接生成文本查询对应的表征。

Result: 在STAG基准上top-1准确率0.771±0.062（4次运行），top-3为0.935，匹配并有时超过监督式闭合集CNN（0.76）。错误集中在少数接触歧义类别，与文本目标几何无相关性（Spearman rho≤0.05，702个类别对），能容忍改写和裸名称查询（差异在1个百分点内），两张不同帧可恢复八帧准确率的89%。负面结果同样报告：跨传感器预训练池化无增益、视觉协同训练反而降低触摸性能、未正确归一化的输入管线静默丢弃97%动态范围。

Conclusion: Tactus证明电阻式压力阵列虽分辨率低，但在小数据条件下仍可通过MAE预训练与校准仿射实现可与光学传感器方法竞争的文本查询触觉表征学习。传感器自身校准贡献超过所有架构改动之和。模型已开源发布。

Abstract: Resistive pressure arrays are the cheapest and most widely shipped tactile sensors, yet tactile representation learning has concentrated on optical sensors that image a deforming gel. We present Tactus, an open model that answers text queries from pressure data alone: on the STAG benchmark (27 objects, held-out recordings), it reaches 0.771 +/- 0.062 top-1 over four runs (top-3 0.935), matching, and at best exceeding, the dataset's supervised closed-set CNN at 0.76, with no trained classifier head. The recipe is small-data: 187 training recordings, masked-autoencoder pretraining on 144k unlabeled same-sensor frames, and the sensor's own calibration affine, which recovered more accuracy than every architecture change combined. The released model's errors concentrate in a few contact-ambiguous classes, are uncorrelated with text-target geometry (Spearman rho <= 0.05 over 702 class pairs), and survive paraphrased and even bare-name queries within one point; two diverse frames recover 89% of eight-frame accuracy. Failures are reported with equal precision: cross-sensor pretraining pooling gave no gain, vision co-training degraded touch, and a mis-normalized input pipeline silently discarded 97% of the sensor's dynamic range while producing plausible intermediate results. Weights, code, and the memory layer the model plugs into are released openly.

</details>


### [251] [Robust and Personalized Federated Learning for Aircraft-Engine Prognostics under Benign and Adversarial Client Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04045)
*Chinmoy Mitra,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在飞机剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测中的双重挑战：良性异构性（不同工况）与对抗性异构性（恶意攻击），并系统评估了四种个性化方案和五种攻击-四种聚合方法的组合，揭示了"个性化+鲁棒聚合"联合方案可在保持精度的同时显著降低后门攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习使飞机机队运营商能在不共享原始遥测数据的情况下联合训练RUL模型，但面临两类关键挑战：良性异构性（不同运营商面临不同运行条件和故障模式）和对抗性异构性（被攻陷的运营商提交投毒更新）。现有的RUL联邦学习研究很少同时系统评估这两类挑战，且缺乏针对物理场景设计的攻击以及安全导向的评估。

Method: 使用多任务一维卷积神经网络（1D-CNN），基于C-MAPSS基准数据集进行结构化非IID划分。对良性异构性比较了四种方案（共享表征个性化、近端正则化、服务端重加权等）；对对抗性异构性评估了五种攻击（包括物理动机的传感器值后门攻击）对抗四种聚合方法（标准平均、Krum等）。

Result: 共享表征个性化弥补了约70%的本地到集中式RMSE差距（近端正则化为21%，服务端重加权仅10%）。物理后门攻击对标准平均达到94.9%的攻击成功率且不改变干净准确率。Krum将攻击成功率降低一个数量级，是唯一能抵御协同攻击者的聚合器。个性化单独无防护作用，但与鲁棒聚合结合可将攻击成功率降至2.8%且精度代价很小。结果在客户端数量和六工况更难数据集上一致。

Conclusion: 仅靠准确率无法认证模型安全性，攻击成功率必须被显式评估。鲁棒聚合（如Krum）是抵御协同攻击的必要条件，而与个性化结合可实现鲁棒性与协作表征学习的平衡，为机队RUL联邦学习的安全部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables aircraft fleet operators to jointly train remaining-useful-life (RUL) models from engine sensor telemetry without sharing raw data. This study examines two complementary challenges: benign heterogeneity, where honest operators observe different operating conditions and fault modes, and adversarial heterogeneity, where compromised operators submit poisoned updates. We conduct a controlled, safety-oriented evaluation using a multi-task one-dimensional convolutional neural network and a structurally non-IID partition of the Commercial Modular Aero-Propulsion System Simulation (C-MAPSS) benchmark. We compare four remedies for benign heterogeneity and evaluate five attacks against four aggregation methods, including a physically motivated sensor-value backdoor designed to mask engine degradation.
  Shared-representation personalization closes approximately 70% of the local-to-centralized root-mean-square-error gap, compared with 21% for proximal regularization and 10% for server-side reweighting. The backdoor achieves a 94.9% attack success rate against standard averaging while leaving clean accuracy statistically unchanged, demonstrating that accuracy alone cannot certify model safety and that attack success must be evaluated explicitly. Krum reduces attack success by an order of magnitude and is the only evaluated aggregator that withstands coordinated attackers, whereas personalization alone provides no protection. Combining personalization with robust aggregation restores robustness (2.8% attack success) with only a small accuracy cost, revealing a trade-off between robust update selection and collaborative representation learning. Results remain consistent across client counts and on a harder six-condition dataset. Code and data partitions are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [252] [Recurrent Residual Quantization: A Progressive Multi-Precision Representation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04048)
*Yu Luo,Bo Dong,Wenhua Cheng,Haihao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为递归残差量化（RRQ）的后训练量化框架，通过将权重表示为低比特量化基座加上一系列量化残差修正项，实现从单一检查点获得多种有效精度（2/4/6/8比特）的灵活量化，无需校准且避免联合多比特优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量化方法需要为每种目标比特宽度准备独立的检查点，无法满足大语言模型在不同部署约束下对精度、内存占用和吞吐量之间灵活权衡的需求。

Method: RRQ框架从经过PTQ或RTN获得的2比特模型出发，通过RTN逐级添加轻量级2比特残差来构建4、6、8比特表示，形成递归残差量化结构。该方法无需校准数据，不涉及联合多比特优化。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B设置中，完整的全RTN 2/4/6/8比特包在1,293秒内构建完成，比MatGPTQ快3.3倍。在六个最新的LLM上的实验表明，在6比特和8比特下精度具有竞争力，4比特下表现因模型而异。

Conclusion: RRQ提供了一种高效、校准免费且支持多精度灵活切换的量化方案，可在单一检查点下实现多种有效比特精度，为大模型部署中的精度-内存-吞吐量权衡提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under diverse deployment constraints requires flexible trade-offs between accuracy, memory footprint, and throughput. However, conventional quantization methods typically require a separate checkpoint for each target bit-width. We introduce Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ), a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that represents weights as a low-bit quantized base together with a sequence of quantized residual corrections, enabling multiple effective precisions from a single checkpoint. Starting from a 2-bit model obtained via post-training quantization (PTQ) or round-to-nearest (RTN), RRQ progressively adds lightweight 2-bit residuals generated via RTN to construct 4-, 6-, and 8-bit representations. The method is calibration-free and avoids joint multi-bit optimization. In our Qwen3-8B setup, the full all-RTN 2-/4-/6-/8-bit package is constructed in 1,293 seconds, 3.3 times faster than the measured MatGPTQ construction. Experiments on six recent LLMs show competitive accuracy at 6 and 8 bits, with model-dependent behavior at 4 bits. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [253] [CAMP: A Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04051)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMP是一个周期感知的多尺度补丁混合器，用于长期时间序列预测。它通过自适应周期学习模块为每个输入窗口识别主导频率并生成周期分量，通过地平线引导的补丁混合器实现随位置变化的上下文细化，并通过多分辨率对齐建模去周期残差。在七个长期预测基准和四个PEMS交通数据集上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有周期感知预测器通常只在数据集级别选择单一周期，无法处理周期随时间变化或多周期共存的情况；补丁模型对所有补丁位置统一处理，忽略了距离预测边界远近所需的差异化上下文；去周期后的残差涉及多时间分辨率，单一尺度难以充分描述。

Method: CAMP由三个核心模块组成：(1) 自适应周期学习模块，为每个输入窗口单独识别主导频率并生成历史和未来周期分量，无需预定义周期长度；(2) 地平线引导的补丁混合器，引入位置相关的细化机制，让早期补丁融入更广泛的时间上下文，同时保留靠近预测边界的信息；(3) 去周期残差通过时间对齐的多分辨率表示进行建模，在单一预测框架内捕获不同尺度的互补动态。

Result: 在七个长期预测基准上，CAMP在六个数据集上取得最佳平均MSE，在六个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳MAE；在四个PEMS交通基准的十六个设置中取得最高MSE胜出次数。

Conclusion: CAMP通过窗口级别的自适应周期识别、位置感知的补丁混合以及多尺度残差建模，有效克服了传统周期感知预测器的局限，在多个长期时间序列预测基准上展示了优越性能。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often governed by recurring patterns, but their dominant periods may vary across datasets, forecasting settings, and individual input windows. Existing cycle-aware forecasters commonly rely on a single period selected at the dataset level, which can be restrictive when periodic behavior changes over time or when multiple cycles coexist. Moreover, patch-based models typically process all patch positions uni- formly, although patches farther from the forecast boundary may require broader contextual refinement, while recent patches contain information that should be preserved more directly. Af- ter cyclic behavior is removed, the remaining dynamics may also span multiple temporal resolutions and cannot be adequately de- scribed at a single scale. We introduce CAMP, a Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer designed to address these challenges. The Adaptive Cycle Learning module identifies dominant fre- quencies separately for each input window and generates both historical and future cyclic components without requiring a pre- defined cycle length. The Horizon-Guided Patch Mixer intro- duces position-dependent refinement, allowing earlier patches to incorporate broader temporal context while preserving infor- mation close to the forecast boundary. CAMP further models the de-cycled residual through temporally aligned multi-resolution representations, enabling complementary dynamics at different scales to be captured within one forecasting framework. Across seven long-term forecasting benchmarks, CAMP achieves the best average MSE on six datasets and the best or tied-best MAE on six. It also obtains the highest MSE win count across sixteen settings on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [254] [LaPrune: Controllable Differentiable Sparsity at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04057)
*Jakub Antczak,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LaPrune，一种精确预算的可微分稀疏化层，通过LapSum屏障和归一化二阶矩约束，在保持选择质量的同时控制掩码的硬度（从均匀分布过渡到hard top-k），并给出了饱和比例、近二元极限定律及最坏情况保证的理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k选择用于确定稀疏模型中激活的组件，但硬选择阻断梯度，而连续松弛方法往往将掩码硬度与所选质量耦合在一起，导致无法独立控制两者的精度。

Method: 提出LaPrune层，采用LapSum屏障保持选择质量，通过归一化二阶矩约束将掩码从密集的等质量分配推向每个预算下的硬top-k。该归一化硬度参数对分数尺度不变，而固定的LapSum温度则不具备此性质。

Result: 推导了饱和比例的群体预测、近二元极限定律以及近零比例的紧致最坏情况保证，证明了方法的理论性质。

Conclusion: LaPrune实现了数学精确预算的可微分稀疏化，解耦了掩码硬度与选择质量，并通过归一化设计获得尺度不变性。

Abstract: Top-$k$ selection determines which components of a sparse model remain active. Hard selection blocks gradients, while continuous relaxations often couple mask hardness to the selected mass. We introduce LaPrune, a mathematically exact-budget differentiable layer that controls the normalized second moment while preserving the selected mass. A LapSum barrier preserves the selection mass, and a normalized second-moment constraint moves the mask from a dense equal-mass allocation toward hard top-$k$ at each budget. We derive a population prediction of the saturated fraction, a near-binary limiting law, and a tight worst-case guarantee on the near-zero fraction. The normalized hardness parameter is invariant to score scale, while a fixed LapSum temperature is not.

</details>


### [255] [SJEPA: Learning Elegant Latent Dynamics with Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04060)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SJEPA是一种免重构的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），其核心创新在于通过混合转换模型（符号律+正则化神经校正）学习具有紧凑符号描述的预测表示，以"最简单且充分的动力学"为原则，在预测保真度、表示质量、符号简洁性与符号-神经分配之间实现可控权衡。实验表明，联合学习比事后拟合发现更简洁的符号动力学，且未约束的算子压缩会导致表示坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）虽然能够学习抽象状态，但其转移模型通常是不可解释的黑盒神经映射，缺乏对底层动力学的符号化、简洁化描述能力。作者希望突破这一局限，让学习到的预测表示不仅具有良好的预测性能，其诱导的动力学还能具有紧凑的符号描述，从而提升可解释性和泛化能力。

Method: SJEPA采用混合转换模型，由两部分组成：(1) 一个符号律（symbolic law）负责捕获符合所选语法的主要动力学；(2) 一个正则化的神经校正项处理所选语法之外的残余动力学。其核心设计原则包括：通过表示约束保持信息丰富、非坍缩的预测坐标；通过算子压缩偏向低复杂度的符号-神经转换，同时保持预测充分性。作者还形式化了诱导动力学的复杂度概念，分析了预测坐标的非可辨识性，并理论表明未约束的算子压缩会直接导致表示坍缩的捷径。框架支持两种学习模式：交替表示方程学习和基于固定表示的符号动力学拟合。

Result: 在受控摆钟实验中：(1) 联合学习发现明显更简洁的符号动力学，其长时域滚动误差和散度均低于事后拟合方法；(2) 未约束的单步诊断验证了理论预测的坍缩捷径确实存在；(3) 在语法错误设定的情况下，校正正则化能保持可表示的符号机制，并将神经组件引导至残余动力学。实验揭示了预测保真度、表示质量、符号简洁性与符号-神经分配之间可控制的权衡关系。

Conclusion: SJEPA证明了联合嵌入预测架构可以在不进行重构的前提下，学习到兼具预测能力与可解释符号描述的表示。该工作为可解释的表示学习提供了新范式，通过显式控制动力学复杂度与表示质量之间的权衡，使符号-神经混合模型成为可信任决策系统中的可行方案。研究同时暴露了算子压缩与表示坍缩之间的深层联系，为后续设计更稳健的预测表示学习方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn abstract states by predicting target embeddings from context embeddings, but their transition models are typically opaque neural maps. We introduce SJEPA, a reconstruction-free JEPA framework that learns predictive representations whose induced dynamics admit compact symbolic descriptions. Its hybrid transition combines a symbolic law with a regularised neural correction for dynamics outside the selected grammar. The central principle is to learn the simplest adequate dynamics: representation constraints preserve informative, non-collapsed predictive coordinates, while operator compression favours low-complexity symbolic-neural transitions that remain predictively adequate. We formalise this principle through induced-dynamics complexity, analyse predictive-coordinate non-identifiability, and show that unconstrained operator compression creates a direct shortcut to representation collapse. The framework supports both alternating representation-equation learning and symbolic dynamics fitted to fixed representations. In controlled pendulum experiments, joint learning discovers substantially simpler symbolic dynamics with lower long-horizon rollout error and divergence than post-hoc fitting, while an unconstrained one-step diagnostic realises the predicted collapse shortcut. Under grammar misspecification, correction regularisation preserves the representable symbolic mechanism and directs the neural component towards residual dynamics. The results expose a controllable trade-off among predictive fidelity, representation quality, symbolic parsimony, and symbolic-neural allocation.

</details>


### [256] [Spatiotemporal Graph Transformer for Traffic Intelligence in Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04075)
*Laha Ale,Letian Lin,Na Cao,Zheng Ma,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时空图Transformer框架，用于边缘计算中的流量预测。该框架通过图神经网络捕获服务区域间的空间相关性，利用Transformer自注意力学习历史流量中的长期时间模式，并在真实蜂窝网络数据集上超越了GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU等基线模型，实现了更有效的主动资源预配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中的流量预测对主动资源管理至关重要，但实际蜂窝边缘系统中流量表现出强空间相关性和长时程时间依赖性。现有的循环预测方法虽能捕获短期动态，但在非平稳条件下难以建模长期流量演变，因此需要一种能同时建模空间交互和时间依赖的预测框架。

Method: 提出一种时空图Transformer框架，将图神经网络与Transformer自注意力机制相结合。具体而言，使用GNN捕获服务区域间的空间相关性，利用Transformer自注意力学习历史流量观测中的长期时间模式，并通过将空间表示学习与时间推理解耦来实现大规模时空流量建模。

Result: 在真实蜂窝网络数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的图Transformer在多个预测视界上均一致地优于GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU等基于循环图网络的基线模型。生成的预测结果使主动资源预配置更加有效，并相比反应式管理策略降低了过载风险。

Conclusion: 图增强的注意力机制在构建智能自适应的边缘计算系统方面展现出巨大潜力，通过联合建模空间相关性和长时间依赖，能够有效提升流量预测精度，从而支持更优的主动资源管理决策。

Abstract: Accurate traffic forecasting is essential for proactive resource management in edge computing, where service demand evolves dynamically across both space and time. In practical cellular edge systems, traffic exhibits strong spatial correlations among neighboring service regions and long-range temporal dependencies driven by user mobility and application behavior. Existing recurrent forecasting approaches can capture short-term dynamics but often struggle to model long-horizon traffic evolution under non-stationary conditions. To address this challenge, we propose a spatiotemporal graph Transformer framework that jointly models spatial interactions and temporal dependencies for traffic forecasting in edge computing. The framework employs graph neural networks to capture spatial correlations among service regions and leverages Transformer-based self-attention to learn long-range temporal patterns from historical traffic observations. By decoupling spatial representation learning from temporal reasoning, the proposed approach provides an effective mechanism for large-scale spatiotemporal traffic modeling. Extensive experiments on a real-world cellular network dataset demonstrate that the proposed graph Transformer consistently outperforms recurrent graph-based baselines, including GCN-RNN, GCN-LSTM, and GCN-GRU models, across multiple forecasting horizons. The resulting forecasts enable more effective proactive resource provisioning and reduce overload risk compared with reactive management strategies. These results highlight the potential of graph-enhanced attention mechanisms for building intelligent and adaptive edge computing systems.

</details>


### [257] [SpecDrop: Parameter-Free Category-Conditioned Routing for Modular Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04084)
*Boyao Wang,Zhihan Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过SpecDrop（一种无需学习参数的固定路由方案）研究MoE网络中向量路由的训练信号粒度与目标任务类别的对齐问题。实验表明：当训练信号粒度与类别对齐时（如CIFAR-100、ImageNet-1K），路由带来的增益主要源于类别监督而非路由算法本身；当粒度模糊（如语言建模、指令微调）时，路由退化为无路由基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE网络依赖学习到的路由器、门控和负载均衡损失，但在相同总参数预算下，学习路由的性能可能不如等权重的无路由基线。作者质疑：瓶颈究竟是路由算法本身，还是训练信号粒度与目标类别之间的对齐程度？

Method: 提出SpecDrop，一种固定参数免费的拓扑路由方案：每个分支对其分配的类别获得权重p_a，其他类别获得小泄漏权重p_i>0，通过类别无关的固定分母合并，无学习路由参数、无辅助损失，推理时需要类别标签。在视觉任务（每个图像一个超类标签）和语言任务（训练单元跨多个类别）上分别验证。

Result: 视觉任务上SpecDrop在CIFAR-100（ResNet-110）达79.23%，ImageNet-1K（ViT-S/16）达79.89%，超过不使用标签的参数匹配基线（CIFAR-100上比dense高+4.75，ImageNet-1K上比No-Routing+SE高+6.53）。但与同样使用标签的基线相比，masking dense模型输出更优（85.2/83.7）。SpecDrop实现58%/100%的分支-类别对齐，masking增益仅0.00（CIFAR）/ +1.06（ImageNet）。在模糊分区（SlimPajama-6B语言建模、SuperNI指令微调）上，路由退化为无路由基线（在种子噪声范围内）。

Conclusion: 粒度对齐而非算法选择是决定路由何时有效的关键因素。SpecDrop的贡献在于将类别标签转化为训练好的模块化结构，而非在准确率上超越标签感知的基线部署方式。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) networks pursue specialization through learned routers, gates, and load-balancing losses, yet at matched total-parameter budgets learned routers can underperform equal-weight No-Routing baselines. Is the bottleneck the routing algorithm, or the alignment between training-signal granularity and the target categories? We probe the question with SpecDrop, a fixed parameter-free routing scheme: each of $K$ branches receives weight $p_a$ for its assigned category and a small leakage $p_i > 0$ otherwise, merged through a category-independent fixed denominator, with no learned routing parameters and no auxiliary losses; the category label is required at inference. On vision tasks where each image has one superclass label (CIFAR-100 on ResNet-110; ImageNet-1K on ViT-S/16), SpecDrop reaches 79.23% on CIFAR-100 and 79.89% on ImageNet-1K, exceeding parameter-matched baselines that do not use the label (+4.75 over dense on CIFAR-100; +6.53 over the No-Routing+SE control on ImageNet-1K). These gains quantify what category supervision buys when deployed through routing -- not an advantage over label-aware deployments of the baselines: given the same label, masking a dense model's outputs is stronger for accuracy alone (85.2 / 83.7). SpecDrop's contribution is converting the label into trained-in modular structure: 58%/100% branch-category alignment, and masking gains of 0.00 (CIFAR) / +1.06 (ImageNet) -- the output-space restriction is largely internalized during training. On fuzzy partitions, where training units span multiple categories (SlimPajama-6B language modeling with a 30M Transformer; SuperNI instruction tuning over Llama-3.2-1B with LoRA), the routing mechanism reduces to the matched No-Routing controls within seed noise, the null our thesis predicts. Granularity alignment, not algorithm choice, localizes when routing helps. Code: https://github.com/Beryex/SpecDrop

</details>


### [258] [Out-Of-The-Loop Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04113)
*Gustavo Sutter,Hao Wang,Luis Ricardez-Sandoval,Pascal Poupart,Agustinus Kristiadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出标准多保真贝叶斯优化（MF-BO）算法在处理最高保真度函数过于昂贵、但已有历史最高保真度数据（黄金标准数据）的现实场景中表现次优。作者提出通过结合历史高保真度数据及任务描述符（可显式给出或从非结构化元数据中提取）来缓解这一问题，并在合成函数、化学和超参数优化等真实问题上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多保真贝叶斯优化中，最精确（最高保真度）的目标函数往往过于昂贵而无法直接参与优化循环，但实践中常存在来自先前实验的黄金标准数据（例如化学家通过仿真筛选出top-k候选分子后揭示其真实目标函数值）。标准MF-BO算法未利用这些历史数据，导致在这些真实场景下表现不佳，即使处于理想假设下也是如此。

Method: 作者首先论证了标准MF-BO算法在存在历史高保真度数据场景下的次优性。随后提出将历史高保真度数据与任务描述符（task descriptors）相结合的方法，任务描述符可以显式给定或从非结构化元数据中提取，用以在优化过程中借鉴历史任务的信息。

Result: 在合成函数以及化学和超参数优化等真实世界问题上验证了所提方法的有效性，相较标准MF-BO算法取得了更好的优化性能。

Conclusion: 通过在MF-BO框架中融入历史高保真度数据及其任务描述符，可以显著改善标准算法在最高保真度函数过于昂贵场景下的优化效果，为实际工程和科学中的应用提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Black-box optimization is a ubiquitous problem in science and engineering, often dealing with expensive objective functions with cheaper lower-fidelity proxies available. Multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MF-BO) is a principled approach to this problem, leveraging correlations across different fidelities when querying the objective. However, for many important MF-BO tasks, the true highest-fidelity function is prohibitively expensive to be part of the optimization loop. Nevertheless, practitioners often have gold standard data (observations of the highest-fidelity function) obtained from previous experiments that might provide information for the current task. For instance, in molecular optimization, chemists often pick the top-$k$ candidate molecules using various computer simulations, and later reveal their true objective function values. In this work, we demonstrate the suboptimality of standard MF-BO algorithms in the real-world scenarios above, even under ideal assumptions. Next, we mitigate this problem by incorporating historical high-fidelity data accompanied by task descriptors---which can be explicitly given or extracted from unstructured metadata. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods on synthetic functions, as well as real-world problems in chemistry and hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [259] [LiNC: Lightweight Noise Correction via Per-Sample Trust and Gaussian Mixture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04147)
*Abhishek Moturu,Babak Taati,Anna Goldenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiNC是一种轻量级标签噪声校正方法，通过为每个训练样本添加一个可学习的信任参数，在标准训练过程中动态决定何时使用观测标签、何时信任模型自身的预测。利用3组分高斯混合模型将信任值分为干净、模糊和噪声三类，并执行软校正和硬校正两个阶段，在MedMNISTv2的10个2D数据集上（标签噪声高达50%）取得了稳定的准确率提升和强错标检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像数据集中普遍存在标签噪声（如标注者间差异、注释错误、模糊病例），这会严重损害基于这些数据集训练的机器学习模型的可靠性和临床有效性。现有方法缺乏高效、轻量级的解决方案。

Method: LiNC核心方法包括：(1) 每个训练样本引入一个可训练的信任参数，在标准训练循环中学习何时使用观测标签、何时依赖于模型预测；(2) 使用观测标签与模型自身预测分布的凸组合作为训练目标，由样本级信任参数控制权重；(3) 利用梯度方向使干净与噪声样本的信任值在早期训练阶段向相反方向演化，产生可分离的信任分布；(4) 用3组分高斯混合模型对信任值进行聚类，分为干净、模糊、噪声三类；(5) 对噪声样本执行短时软校正阶段，最后执行硬校正阶段。

Result: 在MedMNISTv2的10个2D数据集上、标签噪声高达50%的条件下，LiNC一致性地提升了准确率并具备强大的错标检测能力。训练时间复杂度仍由基础网络主导，额外内存随训练集大小线性增长，渐近开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: LiNC提供了一种简单高效、可扩展的标签噪声校正方案，在医学图像分类任务中既能提升模型性能，又能可靠地检测错误标签，同时保持极低的额外计算和内存开销，具备实际临床应用的潜力。

Abstract: Label noise is common in medical imaging datasets due to factors such as inter-rater variability, annotation errors, and ambiguous cases. This can severely undermine the reliability and clinical effectiveness of machine learning models trained using those datasets. To address this challenge, we introduce Lightweight Noise Correction (LiNC), which adds a single trainable trust parameter per training sample and learns when to use the observed label and when to defer to the model during a standard training loop. The key idea is to train using a convex combination of the observed label and the model's own predictive distribution, controlled by a per-sample trust parameter. We show that the gradient of this objective drives trust values in opposite directions for clean versus noisy samples in the early training phase, yielding separable trust distributions. We use a 3-component Gaussian Mixture Model over the trust values to separate them into clean, ambiguous, and noisy cases and then execute a short soft-correction phase on the noisy cases and a final hard correction phase. Experiments on ten 2D datasets from MedMNISTv2 under label noise of up to 50% show consistent gains in accuracy and strong mislabel detection. LiNC adds negligible asymptotic overhead: the training-time complexity remains dominated by the base network, with additional memory growing linearly with the size of the training set.

</details>


### [260] [MINT: Tensor Decomposition on Stacked Recurrence Matrices for Time Series Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04157)
*Kaamil Kaka,Audrey Der,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Zachary Zimmerman,Vikram Jayaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量化自相似矩阵的时间序列数据挖掘原语，用于对大规模单变量时间序列数据集进行共聚类模式挖掘。该方法计算大小为N×(n-m+1)×(n-m+1)的点图张量，并利用张量分解方法挖掘共聚类模式，在轨道交通、电力需求、风力涡轮机和汽车交通等数据集上验证了MINT管道的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重现图（Recurrence Plots）作为时间序列数据挖掘原语已在多个领域（如恒星光曲线、声音波形、CCT遥测）得到广泛应用。本文旨在将其推广到更广泛的场景，特别是需要同时处理多个（N条）时间序列并进行跨传感器模式挖掘的场景，因此提出了张量化自相似矩阵作为一种新的原语。

Method: 该方法将自相似性（点图）计算扩展为张量形式：对N条长度均为n的时间序列（子序列窗口长度为m），计算大小为N×(n-m+1)×(n-m+1)的点图张量，随后利用张量分解方法对该张量进行挖掘，以发现共聚类（co-clustered）的跨传感器模式，形成名为MINT的完整流水线。

Result: 在公共交通、电力需求、风力发电机和汽车交通四类数据集上进行了实验验证，结果表明MINT流水线能够在包含规则间隔动机（motif）的高度规律数据集中有效地共聚类跨传感器模式。

Conclusion: 张量化自相似矩阵及其配套的MINT挖掘流水线是一种有效的、可扩展的时间序列数据挖掘原语，特别适用于挖掘具有规律性重复模式的大规模数据集中跨传感器的共聚类模式，且其张量本质天然支持向多变量数据集的扩展。

Abstract: Recurrence plots are a time series data mining primitive applied to a variety of domains (e.g. star light curves, sound waveforms, CCT telemetry). This work proposes tensorized self-similarity matrices as a primitive for univariate time series datasets ($N\times n$) of $N$ time series of length $n$ with a subsequence window of length $m$, and whose tensor-based nature is naturally extensible to multivariate datasets. The proposed method to compute this primitive computes dot plots of size $N \times (n-m+1) \times (n-m+ 1)$ from these datasets, where the subsequent tensor is mined using tensor decomposition methods to mine for co-clustered patterns. We demonstrate our results in mass rapid transit, electricity demand, wind turbine, and car traffic data, finding the MINT pipeline effectively co-clusters cross-sensor patterns in highly regular datasets containing motifs at regular intervals.

</details>


### [261] [Understanding Fault Tolerance of Adversarially Robust Pruned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04173)
*Manali Dangarikar,Cory Merkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了剪枝、对抗训练和硬件故障注入对深度神经网络鲁棒性的联合影响。基于MNIST数据集上的紧凑型三层CNN，作者发现对抗训练虽能提升对输入扰动的鲁棒性，但会增加对硬件卡零权值故障的敏感性；而剪枝并不会显著增加故障敏感性，剪枝水平的变化对故障率和攻击强度的影响也不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的神经形态硬件上部署DNN面临三个并发的挑战：剪枝压缩需求、对抗输入扰动的脆弱性以及硬件诱导的权值故障（如卡零错误）。尽管这些因素已被单独研究，但它们对模型可靠性的联合效应却鲜少被关注。

Method: 使用一个紧凑的三层CNN在MNIST数据集上进行三组实验：(1) 比较自然训练与对抗训练模型在同时发生硬件故障和对抗攻击时的故障容忍度；(2) 评估剪枝对对抗鲁棒性的影响；(3) 刻画故障率、对抗扰动幅度和剪枝水平三者之间的联合精度曲面。

Result: 实验结果显示：对抗训练提升了模型对输入扰动的鲁棒性，但增加了对卡零权值故障的敏感性；与直觉相反，剪枝并未显著增加故障敏感性，且剪枝水平的变化在不同故障率和攻击强度下的影响很小。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在设计部署于神经形态硬件的DNN时，必须联合考虑对抗鲁棒性和硬件可靠性，而不能独立地优化其中任何一个因素。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) deployed on resource-constrained neuromorphic hardware face three concurrent challenges: the need for model compression through pruning, vulnerability to adversarial input perturbations, and susceptibility to hardware-induced weight faults such as stuck-at-zero errors. While each of these factors has been studied in isolation, their combined effects on model reliability have received little attention. This paper presents an empirical investigation of how pruning, adversarial training, and hardware fault injection interact to affect the robustness of convolutional neural networks. Using a compact three-layer CNN trained on MNIST, we conduct three experiments: (1) comparing the fault tolerance of naturally and adversarially trained models under simultaneous hardware faults and adversarial attacks, (2) evaluating how pruning affects adversarial robustness, and (3) characterizing the joint accuracy surface across fault rates, adversarial perturbation magnitudes, and pruning levels. Our results show that adversarial training improves robustness against input perturbations but increases sensitivity to stuck-at-zero weight faults. Contrary to intuition, pruning did not significantly increase fault sensitivity, and varying the pruning level had little effect across fault rates and attack strengths. These results highlight the need to jointly consider adversarial robustness and hardware reliability.

</details>


### [262] [TS2TabPFN: Time Series Classification and Extrinsic Regression through Feature Extraction and a Tabular Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04174)
*Gabriel da Costa Merlin,Diego Furtado Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS2TabPFN框架，将显式特征提取与TabPFN 2.5（表格数据基础模型）相结合，用于时间序列分类（TSC）和外在回归（TSER）任务。实验表明该框架在TSER任务上显著优于现有最先进模型，在TSC任务上也超越了大多数当前最佳算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列方法存在割裂：基于特征的方法强调特征工程的控制力，而深度学习模型依赖端到端的自动预测能力，二者之间的鸿沟导致无法同时兼顾特征可控性与模型自动化性能。

Method: 提出TS2TabPFN框架，通过将显式时间序列特征提取与TabPFN 2.5（表格数据基础模型）相结合，利用TabPFN强大的预测能力来处理提取后的结构化特征。

Result: 在TSER任务上以统计显著性大幅超越最先进模型；在TSC任务上超越大多数当前最佳算法，且具备鲁棒性和高效性。

Conclusion: 将基础模型与结构化特征相结合能够克服单一范式的局限性，开创了时间序列领域新的最先进水平（SOTA）。

Abstract: Time series data are ubiquitous in practical applications, where classification (TSC) and extrinsic regression (TSER) have emerged as essential tasks for obtaining value from temporal sequences. While the literature has seen significant progress through feature-based and deep learning models, existing methods often focus either on the quality of feature extraction or on the intrinsic predictive power of complex architectures applied to raw data. This division creates a gap between the control offered by feature engineering and the automated performance of end-to-end models. This paper proposes TS2TabPFN, a framework that bridges this gap by integrating explicit feature extraction with TabPFN 2.5, a cutting-edge foundation model for tabular data, to leverage its predictive capabilities. Our extensive experimental evaluation demonstrates that TS2TabPFN significantly outperforms state-of-the-art models in TSER tasks with statistical significance, providing a robust and efficient alternative for TSC and surpassing most of the currently best-performing algorithms. These results suggest that combining foundation models with structured features overcomes single-paradigm limitations, establishing a new time series state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [263] [A Comparative Study of Feature Selection Methods for EHR Diagnosis Codes in Opioid Use Disorder Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04180)
*Zihan Ding,Yinan Liu,Tengfei Ma,Rachel Wong,George Leibowitz,Benjamin Littenberg,Xia Zheng,Richard N. Rosenthal,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种用于电子健康记录（EHR）中阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）预测的特征选择方法：复发富集、NTK驱动的早期梯度敏感性、LightGBM-SHAP、弹性网络和LLM引导的语义选择。研究发现性能随特征预算增大而提升但边际收益递减，NTK敏感性在准确性和稳定性之间取得最佳平衡，LLM引导选择虽独立性能较低但提供了互补的临床意义信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR预测建模中的特征选择至关重要，因为输入变量通常高维、稀疏、噪声大且冗余。大规模特征集不仅增加计算负担和过拟合风险，还使模型难以解释，限制了临床实用性。目前缺乏对多种特征选择范式在统一框架下的系统比较研究。

Method: 聚焦诊断相关特征，在统一预处理和评估框架下比较五种特征选择范式：复发富集、NTK启发的早期梯度敏感性、LightGBM-SHAP、弹性网络和LLM引导的语义选择。通过下游预测性能、重采样稳定性和罕见诊断编码的代表性三个维度进行评估。

Result: 预测性能随更大特征预算而提升，但超出中等规模后边际收益递减。NTK敏感性在准确性和稳定性之间提供了最佳整体平衡；LLM引导选择虽然独立性能较低，但贡献了互补的临床有意义信号。

Conclusion: 在EHR的OUD预测中，特征预算存在规模上限，NTK敏感性是综合表现最优的特征选择方法，而LLM引导的语义特征选择与传统统计方法互补，值得在特征选择策略中整合使用。

Abstract: Feature selection is a critical step in electronic health record (EHR)-based predictive modeling, where input variables are often high-dimensional, sparse, noisy, and redundant. Large feature sets not only increase computational burden and overfitting risk, but also make model interpretation difficult, leading to limited usefulness in clinical settings. In this study, we focus on diagnosis-related features and compare five feature selection paradigms for opioid use disorder (OUD) prediction: recurrence enrichment, NTK-motivated early gradient sensitivity, LightGBM-SHAP, Elastic Net, and large language model (LLM)-guided semantic selection. We use a unified preprocessing and evaluation framework and assess each method by downstream predictive performance, resampling stability, and representation of infrequent diagnosis codes. Our results demonstrate that performance improves with larger feature budgets with diminishing returns beyond a moderate size. NTK sensitivity provides the best overall balance of accuracy and stability, and LLM-guided selection contributes complementary clinically meaningful signals despite lower standalone performance.

</details>


### [264] [From Non-Convex Self-Concordant Regularization to Scalable Quasi-Newton Training of PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04206)
*Chenhao Si,Kang An,Shiqian Ma,Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCORE是一种受自和谐启发设计的准牛顿优化方法，用于物理信息神经网络（PINN）训练。其核心机制是利用从学习到的逆度量中计算出的单一准牛顿缩减量，同时决定强Wolfe检验的候选步长和定义下一割线几何的自适应移位。该方法无需构建Hessian矩阵或Hessian-向量乘积，在粘性Burgers、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、Korteweg-de Vries和复数Ginzburg-Landau方程上均优于BFGS和自尺度Broyden基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
